* **Tests and the D2 gate.** `tests/test_dos.c` (7.6: activation from a folder and a ZIP, every
  failure by injection, pins, EXEPACK and derived hashes, picture, palette, font and text goldens
  from the independent Python decoders, registry, shapes against `shapes.py` goldens, the files
  unchanged before and after); Win32-headless `test_locate.c` (folder and ZIP sources, case-insensitive
  names, the data-directory rule); `registry.py check`; `nodos.py check`; P0 at D2 (E.13.6). The D2 gate
  is 7.6 in Checked and Release; Asan deferred to the final pass (K8).
* **Open** (E.17): E1 activation cost against 150 ms (measured at D2), E2 the final required set
  (pruned at D8), E3 whether `MGRAPHIC.EXE` stays required, E4 registry rows added by D6/D7 (the L1
  tables [6444] and [640A], the latter also B's `LEADER_FONT`, RV2-17), E5 last-access time (a host
  fact, FIDELITY), E6 hashing cost in harness runs, E7 and E8 (low-memory cut and arena-full not
  modelled).

**As implemented (D2).** Gate record [ext-D2-acceptance.md](ext-D2-acceptance.md) (2026-10-11): 7.6
met in Checked and Release; the Asan run is deferred to the final pass.

* **Modules** (`present/dos/`, portable C in the portable loop of `build.cmd`): `pdos_assets`
  (activation, status, message, failed file, file ids, accessors), `pdos_exe` (MZ, EXEPACK in place,
  packed relocations, the 23 overlays, `CIV_UNP.EXE`), `pdos_pic` (chunks, LZW R:0000:1318, RLE
  R:0000:12BC, X0/X1 rows, palettes), `pdos_font` (`FONTS.CV`, G#4, G#12, the row words), `pdos_text`
  (the "rt" records, the read-only section lookup R:1F4D:01AD), `pdos_tables` (registry resolution and
  validation), `pdos_shapes` (the cut O5:1455, the unit recolouring O5:1A14..1ADB), `pdos_common.h`
  (decode results and reasons; the diagnostics `pdos_diag_note/count/name/reset`) and the generated
  `pdos_registry.h/.c`.
* **Activation** (`pdos_assets_open`): no source gives `PDOS_E_NONE`; then every required file in
  pin order, read whole and checked by SHA-256 (`CIV_UNP.EXE` when `CIV.EXE` is missing, F143); the
  image (EXEPACK or `CIV_UNP.EXE`'s load module, the derived hashes of root and relocations, the
  overlays, the driver); the tables; `FONTS.CV` and the texts; the pictures and palettes (each raw
  buffer freed after decoding); the shapes. The first failure frees everything and is the status
  (`PDOS_E_MISSING`, `_SIZE`, `_HASH`, `_READ`, `_FORMAT`, `_REGISTRY`), with
  `pdos_assets_failed_file` and one sentence naming the file and the source; `pdos_assets_close`
  resets the status to `PDOS_E_NONE`. Cost (E1): 72 ms in Release from a warm cache (budget 150 ms);
  about 7.1 MB kept.
* **Read service, both back ends** (`host.h`; plain 8.3 names of the source's top level only).
  Win32 (`host_win32.c`): `hw_set_dos_source(from, zip, size)`; a folder, each name found by
  `hw_find_name` and opened read-only (`CreateFileW`, `GENERIC_READ`, `OPEN_EXISTING`); or the ZIP
  that phase R holds in memory for the run, whose folder of its one `civ.exe` (else of its one
  `CIV_UNP.EXE`) is the source. Headless (`host_headless.c`): `hh_set_dos_source` (a folder, or a
  path ending in `.zip`, read into memory), `hh_set_dos_zip` (a ZIP the caller holds) and, for tests,
  `hh_dos_inject(name, HH_DOS_MISSING|_SIZE|_ERROR|_FLIP)`.
* **civ64** (`win64/civ64_main.c`): after `civ64_open_data` and the dump and font checks, a folder
  source equal to the data directory stops civ64 with exit code 2 (`civ64_dos_dir_ok` in
  `civ64_locate.c`: both final paths compared ignoring case; a folder inside it is accepted, E.18-1,
  F119); then `hw_set_dos_source` and the one call of `pdos_assets_open()`, before the window, the
  clock and `vm_init`. The status and the message are kept for the profile switch (D3; RV1-6,
  RV1-8). Without DOS files the status is `PDOS_E_NONE` and nothing else changes.
* **Harness** (`tests/headless/harness.c`): `--dos-data DIR|ZIP` and `--dos-inject NAME:kind`
  (RUNNING.md); the store is activated in `hh_run` before `vm_init`, its result written only to
  `<out>/dos-assets.log` (never to `run.log`, `events.log`, `presented.log` or the summary line), and
  closed at the end of the run.
* **Generated registry, pins, offsets and goldens.** `build.cmd` runs `gen/dos/registry.py gen`
  before the language checks; it writes `pdos_registry.h/.c` (only when their text changes) from
  the pins `tests/dos-files.txt` (158 required files, 1,918,109 bytes; `CIV_UNP.EXE` optional; the two
  derived hashes; written by `registry.py pins`) and the registry `docs/dos/OFFSETS.md` (11 rows). The
  goldens `tests/dos-decoded.txt` (1,330 lines) are written by `registry.py goldens` from the
  independent Python models `dosexe.py`, `pic.py`, `fontcv.py`, `texts.py` and `shapes.py` (the cut
  model).
* **Checks in `test.cmd`** (which now requires `build\dosdata`): `test_dos` (`tests/test_dos.c`, the
  items of E.13.1 and E.13.7; its decoded content compared line by line with the goldens),
  `w32_test_dos_host` (`tests/win32/test_dos_host.c`: the Win32 read service and `civ64_dos_dir_ok`),
  `registry.py check` (pins, derived pins, `CIV64_DOS_EXE_*` of `civ64_locate.h`, generated files
  current, every OFFSETS.md row against the files and the listings, every cut-table row against
  `ovl05.lst`, the recolouring constants, O5:152B, the include rule, the goldens) and `nodos.py check
  --variant <variant>`; in Checked and Asan also the stub-core runs with `--dos-data` and with
  `--dos-inject FONTS.CV:flip` (P0 at D2).
* **Shared lookups.** `hw_find_name` (`host_win32.c`: an entry ignoring ASCII case, of several the
  ordinal smallest) serves phase R's `find_name` and the folder read; `zip_find_base` (`res/zip.c`:
  the one file entry with a given last component, -2 for more than one) serves phase R's `find_exe`
  and both back ends.
* **Deviations from the design** (one line each; reasons in ext-D2-acceptance.md):
  * E.3.3: `civ64_dos_dir_ok` lies in `civ64_locate.c`, not `civ64_start.c`;
  * E.3.2, phase R: `hw_find_name` shared; `zip_find_base` added to `res/zip.c` (phase R's local
    `ascii_ieq8` removed);
  * E.3.2: headless `hh_set_dos_zip` and `--dos-inject` (missing, size, error, flip; at most 8);
  * E.5.3: a ZIP without `civ.exe` uses the folder of its one `CIV_UNP.EXE` (both back ends);
  * E.4, E.9.2: interface additions (`pdos_common.h`, `PdosPin.kind`, `pdos_assets_failed_file`,
    `pdos_file`, `pdos_text_sections`, `pdos_assets_image`, `pdos_image_is_reloc`); close resets the
    status to `PDOS_E_NONE`;
  * E.10: `PdosTableDesc.shape_a/shape_b` carry `groups0=AxB` and `range=A..B`; a `u16`, `ptrs` or
    `imm16` descriptor with a stride below 2 is refused;
  * E.9.3: `PdosCut.rstep` and the kinds `COPY_PREV`, `COPY`, `UNITS`, `WHOLE`;
    `PdosShapeStore.owner`;
  * E.1: no `pdos_page2_initial`; `PDOS_PAGE2_ART_*` over `pdos_pic(PDOS_F_SP299_PIC)`;
  * E.8.2: a section file shorter than 526 bytes refused; a name whose group reaches the end of the
    file kept as not found; an empty first record at 0200h refused (F142);
  * E.7: at most 64 fonts (`PDOS_FONT_MAX`);
  * E.13.3: the Win32-headless cases are the new `tests/win32/test_dos_host.c`, not `test_locate.c`;
    "Locate during a run" is not tested (D3);
  * E.13.5: `nodos.py` does not index windows shared with the Windows game (3,788), also scans
    `w32_test_*.exe`, takes `--variant` and self-tests;
  * E.13.6: no `ext_g1.py --variant dos`; the P0 subset ran by hand (the stub-core runs are in
    `test.cmd`);
  * E.13.1 item 4: a missing `CIV.EXE` is not a failure, the store takes `CIV_UNP.EXE` (F143);
  * E.4.1: `registry.py` also has `pins` and `goldens`.
* **FIDELITY**: F119, F120 and F124 implemented; F139..F143 added (arena, shape storage, picture
  refusals, section lookup, `CIV_UNP.EXE`).

### 12.7 Composition, shadows, replay, palette, fonts and re-skins (D1 area B; phases D3..D7)

Design of extension 5.3, 5.4 (classes N, R, A, W; replay; core decisions), 5.7 (the pointer), 5.9
(the palette rule) and 5.10 (fonts and text), and the DOS profile's callbacks. Detailed design:
[verify/ext-d1-draft-B.md](verify/ext-d1-draft-B.md) (Revision 3).

* **Files** (`present/dos/`, phase): `pdos_mach` (pages 0..3, view ports, DAC; D3), `pdos_draw` (the
  driver functions G#2/3/6/10/14/19/20/31/32/39/41/42 and root fills and lines in portable C, a scale
  `k` of 1 or 2; D3), `pdos_glyph` (G#21 and the root text helpers; D3), `pdos_pal` (the 6-to-8 rule,
  loads, splices, fades, cycling, play palette; D3), `pdos_compose` (N and A canvases, frame choice,
  W over N, damage, dumps; D3), `pdos_shadow` (shadow surfaces, brackets, copies, owner hooks; D3),
  `pdos_scene` and `pdos_scene_tab.c` (the scene table and stack; D3), `pdos_ui` (boxes, the menu box
  model R:1D05:0031/0475 and its row query; D4), `pdos_cursor`, `pdos_replay`, `pdos_prov`,
  `pdos_rand`, `pdos_fontmap`, `pdos_charmap` (D4), `pdos_reskin` (D4..D6), `pdos_play` (D6; built in D6e as `pdos_aplay`, the `pdos_play_*` names being the play
  palette's),
  `pdos_scn_<scene>.c` (D5..D7), `pdos_profile.c` (the DOS `PresentProfile`); `scale.c` gains
  `present_host_rect_inner` and `present_axis_taps` (M); `tests/harness/pdos_scene_check.py`.
* **Callback order** (`pdos_profile.c`): an event goes to the scene stack, then C's `pdos_seq_on_event`,
  then the shadow engine, then re-skins, play elements and composition; an entry probe to the scene
  stack (push), C, the font map, the shadow engine (entry reads), provenance, the scene renderers; an
  exit probe first runs C's timelines to the probe's time, then the shadow engine (exit draws, with
  the returning function's row still on top), renderers, provenance, the font map, the scene pop and
  C's `pdos_seq_on_probe` (Choice B24, RV2-12). `advance` runs C's timelines and updates the pointer,
  visible or not.
* **Canvases.** N: page 0 of `pdos_mn`, 320x200, drawn by C's timelines (intro, title, Birth) or by
  replay into the scene window's logical shadow, with R overlays composed at frame time. A: the
  virtual screen's current size in the Windows layout, base = the core's frame through
  `present_core_xrgb()`, with D's map element, B's small map, status window and unit panel at 2x, R
  elements, W elements unchanged; each layer converted with its own palette before composition.
  `frame()` presents N while the top non-R scene is N (unless a W window covers it), else A.
  `input_frame` is 1 or 2 accordingly.
* **Shadow engine.** Surfaces keyed by bitmap and window handle, claimed lazily at logical, map or
  half resolution. A bracket is the time between a probe's entry and exit: `PdosMacro` (key, `ra`
  resolved with `obs_callsite`, `PDOS_MAC_DROP` or `PDOS_MAC_CONTEXT`, rule-set bits, `target`,
  `enter`, `leave`), registered with `pdos_shadow_macros(owner, ...)`, which refuses and counts a
  macro whose key lacks the exit phase; the outermost DROP bracket on a surface owns it. Owner hooks
  (`PdosShadowHooks`: `claim`, `on_draw`, `on_copy`, `on_resize`, `on_free`) serve D's map model with
  two rule sets. Copies between shadows mirror `BitBlt`; unexplained events change nothing and are
  counted.
* **Replay and scenes.** Each logical primitive and text probe (graphics.md 4.7) draws its DOS form at
  exit at the logical position (device to logical `L(d) = floor((10d + 9) / 16)`, exact preimages of
  `ScaleLogical(v) = (int16_t)((long)v * 160 / 100)` first); shapes and pictures by provenance. The
  scene table in code (row, class, entry and exit keys, `ra`, template, renderer, palette, timeline,
  adapter, colour map) is checked against DOS-PROFILE.md by `pdos_scene_check.py`. The core's
  decisions (map order, blink, newspaper choices, city placements) are followed; DOS-only effects use
  `pdos_rand` (`srand` 16-bit, `rand` 343FDh/269EC3h, `random(n)` with an arithmetic shift), seeded
  per effect instance from events, probes or `advance` only.
* **Palette, fonts, text.** 6-to-8 by rounding, `(v * 255 + 31) / 63`; fades and cycling as the DOS
  root routines; scene palettes; the play palette from WdwMapOpen's exit, cycled per core
  `CyclePaletteColors` probe. The font map (Windows id and call site to DOS font, the leader font
  `8 + b` from `LEADER_FONT`); G#21's colour bytes honoured in DOS static text; core text folded from
  Windows-1252 to 7-bit (B13).
* **Re-skins (class R) and play art (class A).** DOS menu boxes for MenuZ, list and message boxes
  (every `#MsgBox` re-skinned, B4), the menu bar at the Windows geometry with DOS titles in font 1 at
  scale 1 (B14, B15); the T4 setup page before dialog 144 exists (B21); hit geometry `pdos_r_hit` and
  `pdos_menubox_row` for C; the small map (D's P9 rule), status window and unit panel.
* **Prompt rules met.** P0 (reads only through `w16q_*`; writes only presentation memory; posts
  nothing); O6 (every callback whether visible or not); 5.3 (palettes before composition, W over N
  with a counted fallback); 5.4 (classes, replay, core decisions read, never recomputed); 5.7 (the
  pointer from E's shapes, hidden under C's rule); 5.9 (one recorded rule, a platform fact, inferred);
  5.10 (font map, G#21 semantics, alignment from intent, the Windows-1252 fold).
* **Choices** (B 17, one line each): B1 rounding 6-to-8; B2 R overlays composed at frame time; B3 the
  core frame through `present_core_xrgb()`; B4 every `#MsgBox` as R; B5 busy pointer hidden; B6 shadows
  keyed by handles; B7 unexplained events counted, not pasted; B8 target kind from the globals at entry;
  B9 `L(d) = floor((10d + 9) / 16)`; B10 a hand-written scene table checked against the document; B11
  per-effect generators seeded by name and ordinal; B12 the truncation model; B13 the Windows-1252
  fold; B14 Windows-only titles in capitals; B15 menu-bar text at scale 1; B16 R boxes anchored at the
  dialog's top-left at 2x (reversed in D6fSd for the A boxes whose ShowTextDialog call carries DOS's x, y: 12.9 "As
  implemented (D6fSd)"); B17 the small map from D's snapshot (`pmap_src_current`); B18 the DOS
  status column at 2x; B19 the engine inside the DOS profile (`--mirror 2` needs `--dos-data`); B20
  outermost bracket owns; B21 the setup page's Go Back from a cited template constant (withdrawn in D5cR: no Go
  Back row, "As implemented (D5f; area B)"); B22 C's
  sequencing before B's drawing; B23 one driver with a scale `k`; B24 the scene pops after exit draws.
* **Tests and gates.** Unit tests without emulation: `test_pdos_draw`, `_glyph`, `_ui` (menu-box layout
  with RV2-11's `h8` and row clamp, the row query), `_rand` (with the ordinal independence of RV2-18),
  `_pal`, `_shadow`, `_profile`, `_cursor`, `_setup`, `_replay`, `_compose`, `_reskin`, `_play`;
  `pdos_scene_check.py`; A's 7.3 and 7.4 with the DOS profile; D4..D7 scenario counters
  (`unexplained`, `w_over_n`, `colour_nearest`, `font_unmapped`, `prov_unknown`) 0 for covered scenes;
  D-7 hashes of N frames at the default zoom and of A frames with a fixed game screen. Gates: D4 7.7
  and 7.8, D5 7.8 and P0, D6 tests, P0 and Z for DOS, D7 every row.
* **Open** (B 19): OB1 (play-palette selection inputs, D6; settled in D6e), OB2 (the intelligence-advisor labels, D7),
  OB3 (DrawTextInRect's vertical placement, D4), OB4 (font 1 on every play path, D6/D7), OB5 (the
  drop-down check mark, D6), OB6 (minimap colours, D6; settled in D6e), OB7 (unit-panel name tables, D6; not needed
  since D6e, whose unit panel shows the core's strings), OB9 (city
  screen square positions, D7), OB10 (DGROUP:D206, D4), OB11 (B1EC and D208, D4). OB8 is resolved.

**As implemented (D4a).** Record [ext-D4a-record.md](ext-D4a-record.md) (2026-10-12), with the
independent review [verify/ext-d4a-review.md](verify/ext-d4a-review.md) and its fixes (the record's
"Review fixes"). D4a is area B's base: the DOS machine, the portable-C driver, glyphs and text helpers,
the palette, the N and A canvases, the shadow surfaces, the scene table and stack, `pdos_rand`, the font
map, the Windows-1252 fold and the pointer. The results are compiled (C11 and C17 `/W4 /WX`, clang C99
parse) and headless (test programs and the harness on `build/gamedata` and `build/dosdata`) in Checked,
CheckedNoObs and Release. No DOS program was executed, no UI was shown, no DOS byte is in the repository
or in a binary (`nodos.py check`); Asan was not built or run (K8). The Files bullet above names D3 or D4
for these modules; they were built in D4a.

* **Modules** (`present/dos/`, portable C17, always compiled; the observer-only parts sit under
  `#if CIV_OBSERVER`, so `checked noobs` links the same sources). `pdos_mach` (indexed surfaces with
  damage and gen, the view port with the scale `k`, the DAC, four 320x200 pages, the `vga` and
  `box_texture` flags); `pdos_draw` (fill, line, pixel, sprite clipped and unclipped, recolour, G#20 copy
  forward with no overlap handling, span skip, G#10 dissolve, the 16-bit offset arithmetic with the
  64000-byte wrap rule, `PDOS_DIAG_OOB` / `LOOP_CAP`); `pdos_glyph` (G#21 with the store's fonts or a
  test font set, the root text helpers R:0182:002A..00EF, truncation R:1F4D:04F7, wrapping
  R:1F4D:0000/0088, `$` tokens R:1F4D:0471); `pdos_charmap` (the fold, B13); `pdos_rand` (the C run
  time's srand/rand, `random(n)`, the effect seed FNV-1a-32); `pdos_ui` (clamp, outline, bevel, texture,
  panel, box, ornamental border, the page-2 art from SP299.PIC); `pdos_pal` (the 6-to-8 rule, DAC loads,
  splices and clear, fade arithmetic, cycling slots, play slots 96..103/104..111/112..127, the scene
  recipes, the play palette); `pdos_scene` with the hand-written `pdos_scene_tab.c` (141 rows; the stack:
  push at entry, pop at its own exit, a configured exit or window destruction, pending window-bound rows,
  re-targeting by template, depth 16, listener, ready flags, `pdos_scene_wants_n`); `pdos_fontmap` (font id
  and call site to DOS font, tracker, coverage log, leader-font hook); `pdos_shadow` (keyed shadows,
  logical/map/half resolutions, owners with hooks, DROP/CONTEXT macros, brackets, copies, the 7.5 hook
  interface, the unexplained ring); `pdos_compose` (the N and A canvases, elements at 1x/2x with
  transparent index 0 and clips, the frame kind, the 8-bit dump form, damage); `pdos_cursor` (the DOS
  pointer for the core's cursor id: torch, GO arrow, none, the game's cursor; area C's hidden rule);
  `pdos_profile.c` (the callbacks in 2.1's order through these modules, the pointer, the page-2 art, the
  play palette before the first play state). Changed: `pdos_common.h` (15 `PdosDiag` counters,
  `pdos_diag_add`), `pdos_assets.c` (their names, so they reach `presentation-diag.log`),
  `gen/dos/registry.py` (`OBSERVER_FILES`, `ALLOWED_OBSERVER_INCLUDES`), `obs_lint.py` (`check_b_tables`:
  the keys, call sites and phases of the `ROW(...)` rows and the `PdosMacro` initialisers) and
  `ext_frames.py` (the dump check, Deviation 10 below).
* **The DOS profile presents its own frames.** From D4a the profile presents its own N frame (320x200,
  shown 8-bit through `present_shown_indexed`: the 8-bit dump form) and its A frame (the Windows layout,
  with the core frame where no DOS art exists yet); `shows_core_frame` stays 0 (shown through the tick).
  This supersedes D3's "its frame is the Windows frame" (12.5, "As implemented (D3)"). Until a renderer
  calls `pdos_scene_set_ready` no N scene is ready, so the profile presents its first, black, N frame and
  then the A frame (the core frame while no element is set; 6.3), with the torch pointer at frame scale 2.
  *(Since UX1 the pointer is the shape's own pixels on both frames, drawn by the back end at
  `present_pointer_factors`: since UX1b between its size before UX1 and the Windows game cursor's, 12.2
  "Cursor"; draft B 6.7; FIDELITY F159.)*
* **Tests** (each prints `PASS|FAIL test_X: N checks, M failed`; hand-written vectors, hashes or live
  reads of the store; no DOS byte). Checked / CheckedNoObs after the review fixes (Release as Checked):
  `test_pdos_draw` 140 / 140, `_glyph` 182 / 182, `_pal` 236 / 236 (with P4: all ~140 palettes of the
  store against PALETTE.md 6, no mismatch, `pdos-p4-report.txt`), `_rand` 49 / 49, `_ui` 34 / 34,
  `_scene` 118 / 118 (with the font map), `_shadow` 117 / 115, `_compose` 68 / 68, `_cursor` 62 / 62,
  `_profile` 38 / 36: 1044 / 1040 (1039 / 1035 before the fixes). `pdos_scene_check.py` (a `test.cmd`
  step after `obs_lint.py`): the 141 rows against DOS-PROFILE.md 1.1..1.5 and 1.8 (class counts
  57/44/30/10) and the font map against 2.4, 1075 checks, with `info:` lines for rows 6, 24, 119 and row
  9's exit probe.
* **P0 and 7.4 evidence.** P0: the profile reads the emulation only through `w16q_*` inside the observer
  guard, writes only presentation memory, posts nothing, draws no core random number and never reads
  `g_randSeed` (FIDELITY F144); `P0_OK checks 279` (Checked: V0..V7 with V1 `--graphics dos` and V2
  switching every 997 ms) and 56 (CheckedNoObs, V5 against the Checked V0), `EXT_G1_OK runs 77` on both
  observer variants. 7.4: `test_pdos_compose` (the same frames after 0, 1 and 5 peeks) and
  `test_pdos_profile` (never visible / switched at ticks / visible from the start: identical per-step
  peek hashes); `ext_frames.py` on S1 `start` (299 sample lines), S3 (719) and `birth` (2049): A, A', B
  and C identical, A's `presented.log` equal to A''s, 13.1's switch assertions hold, A shown through the
  tick only (374, 609 and 955 tick shows, 0 core shows), 376 `tick-NNNNN.bmp` of 8 and 24 bits. All eight
  steps of the final cycle and of the cycle after the fixes are green. No scene renderer is ready yet,
  so these runs have the profile drawing but no DOS art on screen.
* **Deviations** ([record](ext-D4a-record.md#deviations), one line each): (1) draft 9.1, 7.3: probe keys of
  the scene rows and macros are `OBS_P_*` row numbers (`uint16_t`), not addresses; `PDOS_NO_KEY` 0xFFFF
  is the absent key (row 0 is `_ErrorMessage`); `obs_lint.py` checks them. (2) 9.1: scene-row exits,
  `PdosSceneRow` has `exit_ra`, `exit_at` and `dlg`; rows 2, 5, 9 and 98 use a configured exit. (3) 9.2:
  a probe may pop several rows (the scan continues below a popped slot; a return pairs with one entry, so
  a recursive entry pops one level per return). (4) 3: names `PDOS_SCR_W` / `PDOS_SCR_H`, `PdosMach`'s
  `port[4]` and `cur`, the `vga` and `box_texture` flags. (5) 9.1, 7.3: configuration errors are refused
  and counted (`scene_config`, `macro_config`), not faulted; 0 for the delivered tables. (6) 7.4:
  injectable shadow queries, `PdosShadowQ` through `pdos_shadow_set_query`, default `w16q_*`. (7) 5.3,
  7.3: `pdos_cycle_setup` returns `int`; `pdos_shadow_context(uint16_t key)`. (8) 7.3: an exit from a call
  site the macro does not name is ignored, not an `exit_orphan`. (9) 5.5: the A machine's DAC preload,
  SP257.PIC's palette at reset (equal to SP256.PAL), so the pointer is visible on A frames before the
  first play state; WdwMapOpen's exit sets the play palette as designed. (10) 6.3 / 13.4: the dump check
  of `ext_frames.py` is now "bit counts a subset of {8, 24} with 24 present, no `presented-NNNNN.bmp`"
  (was "all 24-bit"); the run shows `[8, 24]`.
* **Span-skip column (review F3; supersedes draft B 4.2).** G:0000:1986 is one value per machine,
  `PdosMach.span_x` (013Fh at init and reset); every page points at it (`PdosSurf.span`), G#49 writes it
  and G#39 reads it for a fill on any page. A surface outside a machine (a shadow, an element; `span`
  NULL) keeps its own value, 0FFFFh at init; `pdos_surf_clear` resets only the surface's own value. Draft
  B 4.2's "span-skip column per surface" is superseded for the pages of a machine; the draft is not
  edited.
* **Review.** Independent read-only review: 0 BLOCKER, 0 MAJOR, 6 MINOR; the driver, G#21 and the R:0182
  helpers, `pdos_pal` and `pdos_rand` match the listing for every function traced. Fixes: F1
  `pdos_write_row` counts words in 16 bits (count FFFFh writes 0 words and reads nothing); F3 above; F4
  the record's CopyRect wording; F6 LoadPaletteBlock with last < first writes nothing (DOS would write
  about 65536 triples from undefined memory; unreachable with the shipped files), documented; F2 and F5
  recorded limitations. Five vectors added to `test_pdos_draw` (135 to 140); no frame expectation
  changed.
* **Limitations for D4b/D5.** F2: the sprite arena is not modelled (`pdos_grab` allocates `w * h` host
  bytes and grabs every row; DOS BeginSprite/AddSpriteRow build nothing or fewer rows when the G#18 arena
  is closed or full). D4b/D5 must check, for each renderer that transcribes a G#2 call site, the arena
  its scene opens with G#18 against the sum of `rows * (w + 4)` of the grabs before the next G#18, and
  carry an arena budget if a grab can exceed it. F5: ShowPage's ghost pointer is not modelled (G#31 draws
  the pointer into page n and restores page 0 only, so a later CopyRect from page n can carry it). D4b/D5
  must check whether a scene shows the pointer (DGROUP:5876 set, DGROUP:5403 clear) across a ShowPage and
  then copies from that page.
* **What is left.** D4b: `pdos_seq` (the intro, title and Birth timelines; the first caller of
  `pdos_scene_listen` and `pdos_cursor_set_hidden_rule`), `pdos_replay`, `pdos_prov`, `pdos_reskin`,
  `pdos_play` (built in D6e as `pdos_aplay`) and the `pdos_scn_*` renderers (their calls are marked in
  `pdos_profile.c`). OB1 (`pdos_play_back`, the BACK palette selection; settled in D6e); the `GR_PortDataToBitmap` rebinding wiring (the
  engine's `on_resize` hook is built and tested, the 7.5 owner D6 is not there); the N-canvas window
  binding beyond `update_n_window` (D5); E's leader-font table (`pdos_fontmap_set_leader_table`, D7;
  until then ids 200..215 are unmapped and counted in `font_unmapped`; set since D7k from the registry's
  `LEADER_FONT` at the leader's reset, "As implemented (D7k; areas B and C)" below). Only an emulated run exercises
  `update_wover`'s window enumeration, `follow_cursor` and the real P0 equality (no `test_pdos_*` covers
  them). Entry-only probes are not scene rows (RV2-1); rows 6, 24 and 119 use the observer table's call
  sites. Not verified (review O1..O3): DAC values are stored unmasked (equal while every value is <= 63);
  bytes outside the string in `pdos_truncate` and `pdos_wrap_prepare` (fixed C answers); on
  `OBS_DLG_CREATE` only the topmost pending window-bound slot is considered (to be tested in D4b/D5).

**Added in D4b2b2 (area B).** Record [ext-D4b2b2-record.md](ext-D4b2b2-record.md) (2026-10-12); S3, S6, S7
and S9 are items of the D4a record's [second review](ext-D4a-record.md#second-review-2026-10-12). Compiled and
headless in Checked, CheckedNoObs and Release. Area C's parts of the unit are in 12.8, "As implemented (D4b)".

* **The menu-box model** (`pdos_ui.h/.c`; B 4.4, C.12.5): `PdosMenuBox`, `pdos_menubox_layout`, `_draw`,
  `_select`, `_row`, `_hotkey`: the layout, colours, highlight and hotkeys of R:1D05:0031..0475 on given
  lines; the layout takes the machine and the port (the pixel read at an odd y, the font). Framed iff y is
  even (R:1D05:05E4: at y 139 frameless, superseding B 4.4's "or y == 139"); the highlight swaps the bar
  colours, so a select and a deselect restore the pixels; `_hotkey` follows R:1D05:03BF..03FD and only
  selects, from the current selection + 1 without wrap (record Deviation 2; D4c decides the translation);
  `disabled` keeps the listing's 16-bit word. Not modelled: the advisor variant, the help line, the OK form,
  the item marks, the EGA flash. First user: C's title box; D4c's filter will use `_row` and `_hotkey`.
  Tested in `test_pdos_seq` (`test_menubox_model`).
* **S6, S7, S9** (`pdos_shadow.c`). S6: a late-bound DROP bracket with a NULL target, not itself muted,
  mutes the DROP brackets opened inside it on the same surface (`bind_pending`). S7: brackets open on a
  freed (kind, key) are detached and draw, drop and record nothing (`free_surface_shadows`). S9: a macro
  registration closes the open brackets without `leave` (`pdos_shadow_macros`; Deviation 11). S7 and S9
  count the new diagnostic `detached` (`pdos_common.h`, `pdos_assets.c`; the `pdos` line of
  `presentation-diag.log`). Vectors `test_review_s6_s7_s9` in `test_pdos_shadow` (135 checks in Checked
  and Release, 133 in CheckedNoObs).
* **Still open.** S3: no macro with a drawing `leave` exists yet (the title box draws on `pdos_mn` from the
  sequencer, not through a bracket); the first unit that registers one (D4b3 or D5's replay) takes option
  (a) or (b) of the D4a record with its vector *(settled in D6b by option (a): "As implemented (D6b; area B)",
  below)*. F2's logical-text part stays D4b3's. The pointer's hidden
  rule now has its caller (12.8, C.11.5) and `pdos_seq` (D4b2b1) is the first listener of
  `pdos_scene_listen`.

**Added in D4b3 (area B).** Record [ext-D4b3-record.md](ext-D4b3-record.md) (2026-10-15), F2 of the D4a
second review (its logical-text part). Compiled and headless in Checked, CheckedNoObs and Release. Area C's
parts of the unit are in 12.8, "Added in D4b3".

* **F2's tracker per graf** (`pdos_fontmap.h/.c`). SetCivFont and SetTextFontSizeColor are noted on the
  graf they set the font on (g_graf, a far pointer read with `w16q_rd32` at the probe), with
  SetTextFontSizeColor's size; `pdos_fontmap_current_on(graf, &id, &size)` answers per graf; the tracker
  keeps the 8 grafs noted last (least recently noted dropped; record Deviation 10). The old
  `pdos_fontmap_note`/`current` API stays (graf unknown).
* **Logical text.** `pdos_fontmap_logical(ra, &id)` reads the logical text's font (word +10h of
  *g_pScreenPort, `OF_ScreenPort_fontId`) through `w16q_*` under `CIV_OBSERVER`, inside the observer's
  guard, and maps it with the pure `pdos_fontmap_port_font`. Vectors in `test_pdos_scene` (126 checks; 127
  in CheckedNoObs with the no-observer check).
* **Still open.** F2's leader-face part (`g_playerCivId[n]` rather than `id - 200`) is D7's, with the leader

**As implemented (D5a; area B).** Record [ext-D5a-record.md](ext-D5a-record.md) (2026-10-15/16): draft B 7.2's
"Open for D5" (the D4a second review's S4, O1, O2, O3 and P1) resolved; with it the D4a record's left items "the
N-canvas window binding beyond `update_n_window`" and "only the topmost pending window-bound slot" (above) are
done. 7.3's S3 stays open for D6: D5a adds no macro, and a D5 unit that adds one drawing at `leave` must resolve
S3 first *(D6b settled S3 by option (a), "As implemented (D6b; area B)", below)*. UX1b, D5a, D5b and D5c landed together in one build slot on 2026-10-16 (46 files copied at 00:24:40
UTC; the three-step cycle ALL_GREEN 01:20:28 UTC, `build/d5l-status.txt`; [ext-D5a-record.md](ext-D5a-record.md)
"Landing"); compiled and headless in Checked (CheckedNoObs and Release privately in the stages); no DOS program
was executed; Asan deferred. D5a changes no DOS frame: its only trace in the recorded runs is
`presentation-diag.log`'s `n_clip 0`. Area C's part is in 12.8, "As implemented (D5a; area C)".

* **S4 with O1, the N-window binding** (`pdos_shadow.c/.h`). D4a decided once, at a shadow's creation, whether
  a 512 x 320 logical window's shadow was page 0 of `pdos_mn()`. Now every logical window shadow has its own
  private surface and **at most one is attached to page 0**, the N window's. `pdos_shadow_set_n_window(hwnd)`
  (called by the profile after every event and probe) detaches the old one on a change (pending changes copied,
  then page 0's part under the client copied into the private surface) and attaches the new one as soon as its
  shadow exists. A 320 x 200 logical client (512 x 320 device) sits at (0, 0) and its surface is page 0 (as
  D4a); any other size sits at its logical position, the new fourth query `PdosShadowQ.wnd_place` (the
  client's offset from the 512 x 320 rectangle centred on the desktop's client; default `w16q_wnd` under the
  observer, else (0, 0)) mapped by L(d), keeps its own surface and is copied into page 0, clipped, after each
  event or probe (`n_sync`); a client that does not fit counts the new diagnostic `n_clip` once per attach
  (`PDOS_DIAG_N_CLIP`, `pdos_common.h`; its name in `pdos_assets.c`). **O1 decided:** page 0 is never cleared
  at an attach; the private surface is copied in only when it changed since its creation, resize or last
  detach, otherwise the window takes page 0 over as it is (B 11.5's takeover without a redraw), because a clear
  would blank the DOS view's own drawing and an unchanged copy would bring back stale Windows pixels. A resized
  attached window is detached, resized (black) and attached again; a destroyed one leaves page 0 as it is. New:
  `pdos_shadow_logical_template(tmpl)` (7.2's templates 130, 142, 143, 144, 146..149, 151) and
  `pdos_shadow_n_attached()`.
* **The N window** (`pdos_profile.c`). `update_n_window` takes the topmost N row with a bound window of a
  logical-layer template (record Deviation 5; D4a took any N row's window, so dialog 139 over 148 would now have
  detached 148); 148 stays the N window under the title menu. **P1:** `update_wover` walks the visible top-level
  windows down to the N scene window's top-level ancestor (`top_level_of`, bounded at 32), not to the window
  itself. **O3** (`pdos_scene.c`): at `OBS_DLG_CREATE` every pending window-bound slot is considered from the top;
  the first whose row's template is the dialog's, or that the template re-targets to, is bound; only when none
  is, the topmost pending slot is withdrawn. **O2:** measured privately, about 0.25..0.30 µs per event or probe
  for `update_n_window` and `update_wover` together (13..26 % of the DOS profile's callback time, 0.3..2 % of a
  headless run's wall time); the call pattern is kept.
* `pdos_compose.c/.h` unchanged (the frame kind reads page 0 and the rows' readiness, not which shadow is page
  0). Tests: `test_pdos_shadow` 157 checks (S4's vector `test_n_binding`; `test_window_and_n`'s resized N window
  now keeps a private black shadow), `test_pdos_scene` 136 (`test_o3`).

**As implemented (D5b; area B).** Record [ext-D5b-record.md](ext-D5b-record.md) (2026-10-16): the class R re-skins
on the N canvas, `pdos_reskin` (draft B 11.1..11.4, 6.1 Choice B2 and 6.4 Choice B4 implemented for the N canvas;
11.3 and 11.4 for the A canvas stay D6f's). Landed with D5a (above). No recorded flow presents a D5b box: the
boxes are presented only while the top non-R scene row is a ready N row, and the 12 recorded flows that open
ShowTextDialog's dialogs 136 or 145 open them with no ready N row below (mostly in play, the A frame until D6),
so no DOS frame changed. Area C's part is in 12.8, "As implemented (D5b; area C)".

* **The builder and the elements** (`pdos_reskin.h/.c`, new). `PdosRSpec` is a box's content in DOS terms (id,
  kind MENU, MSGBOX or BUILT for D5c..D7 renderers, font, DOS position, header lines then rows `' ' + text`,
  each row's w16 control, screen rectangle and core item index, a disabled mask, the highlighted row).
  `pdos_r_spec_header` folds the core's text (10.4), splits at line feeds, wraps at 34 columns by R:1F4D:0088's
  rule (Choice D5b-3; changed in D6fSc for the MenuZ boxes of dialogs 136 and 145 and the message boxes: DOS's line
  rule at 200 pixels, Choice D6fSc-1, and the DOS game's own text for five paired boxes; `pdos_r_spec_header` keeps
  its 272 pixels for `pdos_menu.c`; 12.9 "As implemented (D6fSc)") and drops a line's leading spaces and `_`; `pdos_r_spec_row` folds and removes `&` marks
  (`&&` keeps one). `pdos_r_set` lays the box out with B 4.4's `pdos_menubox_layout` in the spec's font (DOS font
  1 by default, Choice D5b-4; OB4 stays open), clamps it inside 320 x 200 (moved left or up, y keeping its parity,
  so the framed or frameless form of R:1D05:05E4 is kept), seeds the element's surface with the composed page 0
  under it, draws it with `pdos_menubox_draw`, highlights with `pdos_menubox_select` and sets it as an opaque N
  element (B2: composed at frame time, z in first-set order, at most 4); it is drawn again when page 0's
  generation changes. A change of the highlight only moves it on the drawn box (R:1D05:0238), keeping the DOS
  order, OPEN L28's colour effect included (a disabled row's 3 becomes 22 after the highlight leaves it).
  `pdos_r_hit(frame, x, y)` (11.4; frame 1 only, topmost element first; pure) and `pdos_reskin_anchor(hwnd, x,
  y)` (11.3; a renderer's DOS position for a window's box at its next build; 8 anchors).
* **The observer side** (B 11.2; `CIV_OBSERVER` only). **Menu box:** a MenuZ 1220:0156 (pushed at its ENTER) is
  bound at its first key poll when its dialog is the window of a class R scene row drawn by the re-skins (rows
  14 and 22, ShowTextDialog 1220:00D2's dialogs 136 and 145): header the MenuZ text up to its first `_`
  (SplitMenuText's title), rows the RanDoMRadio items 500 + k, then **one extra last row for a visible Go Back
  (102) or Cancel (2)** with the core's text, disabled in colour 3 (OPEN P17 settled) *(withdrawn in D5f by the
  user's direction of 2026-10-16: no such row; "As implemented (D5f; area B)", below)*; the highlight follows
  `mz_sel` (record deviation D5b-1: `W16qBtn` has no checked state); rebuilt on TEXT, ENABLE, SHOW or HIDE of the
  dialog or a child, dropped at the dialog's HIDE or DESTROY. **Message box (B4):** at `OBS_WND_SHOW` of a
  `#MsgBox`, read with `w16q_msgbox` (12.5): its lines as header lines, one row per button (since D5f none for
  IDCANCEL), the default highlighted, `OBS_FOCUS` moving the highlight; at L(d) of the window's top-left from the N canvas's reference,
  y rounded down to even (Choice D5b-5: always framed), then any anchor and the clamp; no caption, icon or DOS
  "OK in an outline" form (deviation D5b-2). In `update_wover` a window shown as an R element is not a window over
  the N scene (`pdos_reskin_is_elem`), so a `#MsgBox` with no W row of its own over a ready N scene presents the N
  frame with a DOS box (`w_over_n` no longer counts it); ShowErrorMessage keeps the A frame by its W row 141 (F79).
  Elements are built on any frame (a bound box in play too: one path for D6).
* **`pdos_compose_n_read(rect, below, dst, pitch)`** (`pdos_compose.h/.c`): page 0's rows of the rectangle with
  the visible N elements below `below`'s z place composed over them (the frameless box's sampling of the composed
  page 0). **OPEN L28** read: the colour effect is reachable through the builder; the y = 139 bar colour only
  through a renderer's anchor at y 139 (no generic box is there in the recorded flows). Tests:
  `test_pdos_reskin` 63 checks (Checked, Release; 56 in CheckedNoObs; synthetic fonts, no game or DOS file),
  `test_observer` 108 (`test_msgbox_query`; 103 before).

  advancing by its G#4 width, the cells after the text spaces. The field's text and caret are the core's EDIT

**As implemented (D6a; area B).** Record [ext-D6a-record.md](ext-D6a-record.md) (2026-10-16): the shadow engine's part of
unit D6a (the map display model with the Windows rule set and M2's present check; BRIEF-d6.md "Units" D6a): S8 of the
D4a second review done (draft B 7.1, 7.5), the `GR_PortDataToBitmap` rebinding wiring (B 7.1; the D4a record's left
item "What is left", above) and three engine rules with a per-rule-set unexplained count (B 7.4, 7.5). D6a landed
ahead of the D5 gate by the orchestrator's ruling at 03:45 UTC: it changes nothing visible and overlaps no D5 file, and
the D5 gate's eight-step cycle covers it (the 13 staged files, the new `present/map/pmap_model.c` and the record copied
at 03:47:34 UTC by `build/d6a-prep/land.py`, every base matching; the three-step cycle ALL_GREEN 04:10:15 UTC,
`build/d6a-status.txt`; [ext-D6a-record.md](ext-D6a-record.md) "Landing"); compiled and headless in Checked
(CheckedNoObs and Release privately in the stage); no DOS program was executed; Asan deferred. The DOS profile's
display, its diagnostics and the core are unchanged (the record's nine comparison runs). Area D's part, the model
itself and the present check, is in 12.9, "As implemented (D6a; area D)".

* **S8** (`pdos_shadow.c/.h`; the D4a record's second review: `pdos_shadow_rebind` moved only `key`).
  `pdos_shadow_rebind` takes the new bitmap's device size when its create gave one; a different size reallocates the
  shadow black and calls the owner's `on_resize`; open brackets on the old handle follow it (their target and
  `PdosBracket.target_key`); brackets on the new handle are detached and counted (S7's recycled-handle rule).
* **GR_PortDataToBitmap 10B8:0E36** (B 7.1). Between its entry and exit the engine keeps the port's hdc (+0x18) and
  old bitmap (+0x1A) read at the entry; the old bitmap's delete (10B8:113F) rebinds its shadows to the DC's current
  bitmap instead of freeing them (`pdos_shadow_rebinds`); any other delete frees as before.
* **Engine rules** (B 7.4, 7.5; the record's deviation "B 7.4/7.5"). A copy asks the owner of both shadows once (the
  map's own copies were otherwise counted twice). A copy makes its destination bitmap's logical shadow only when the
  source shadow is logical (a map shadow's copy into DrawTile's buffer makes none). `OBS_BMP_DAMAGE` alone is no
  longer unexplained: it precedes the same drawing's `OBS_DRAW`, which is judged (with map shadows in place every
  DAMAGE event of P would have counted; the DOS count is unchanged with the rule); raw bits (`OBS_BMP_BITS`) still
  are. The unexplained count is kept per rule set (`pdos_shadow_unexplained_rs`, `PdosUnexplained.rule_set`), so

**As implemented (D6b; area B).** Record [ext-D6b-record.md](ext-D6b-record.md) (2026-10-16; BRIEF-d6.md "Units" D6b,
decisions 3 and 4): the logical replay `pdos_replay` and the provenance of shapes and pictures `pdos_prov` (draft B
8.1..8.6; PROF 2.2, 2.3), and S3 of the D4a second review settled by option (a) (B 7.3; the D4b2b2, D4b3 and D5a items
above). Nothing changes on screen: the replay's logical shadows wait for D7's ready N rows, and in play every kind-2
primitive met so far lies inside D's map macros (12.9), where the replay is muted. The 13 staged files and the record
were copied at 06:20 UTC (`build/d6b-prep/land.py`, every base rechecked; test.cmd's new section `ext_replay`). The
first three-step cycle stopped at step 2 (`d2_registry`: `registry.py check`'s include rule rejected `pdos_prov.c` and
`pdos_replay.c`, observer readers like `pdos_shadow.c` that `OBSERVER_FILES` did not name, and `pdos_replay.c`'s
`<stdio.h>`). Fix 1 at 06:45 UTC (`build/d6b-prep/land_fix.py`): `pdos_replay.c` builds its diagnostic line with a
local `line_field` helper instead of `snprintf` (no `<stdio.h>`; the rule is not widened), and `gen/dos/registry.py`
adds `pdos_replay.c` and `pdos_prov.c` to `OBSERVER_FILES`. The cycle from step 1 is ALL_GREEN 07:09:05 UTC
(`build/d6b-status.txt`, logs `build/d6b-<n>-<step>-r2.log`; [ext-D6b-record.md](ext-D6b-record.md) "Landing");
compiled and headless in Checked (CheckedNoObs and Release privately in the stage); no DOS program was executed; Asan
deferred. The core, every DOS frame and every earlier diagnostic value are unchanged (P0_OK 304; in the stage's 27
DOS comparison runs and the landing's 27 V1 `presentation-diag.log` files only the new fields differ, besides the
`pmap` line's host-time fields).

* **Registration and frames** (`pdos_replay.h/.c`, new; B 8.1, 8.2, 2.1). The owner "replay" registers 16 DROP
  macros (the DOS rule set, any call site, target `t_prim`, leave `l_prim`, the new flag `PDOS_MAC_LEAVE_DRAWS`) on
  8.2's primitives: FillRectLogical 11E8:0504, FrameRectLogical 1220:1086, FrameRectDoubleDevice 1220:114B,
  DrawScreenLine 10D8:0000, DrawTextAtPortXY 10D8:00E0, DrawScreenText, DrawShadowedScreenText,
  DrawScreenTextWithShadow and their three centred forms (10D8:01B0..0528), DrawShapeAndFlush 11E8:0674, DrawMapSprite
  11C8:008A, DrawImprovementIconLogical 1030:84BE, DrawTextInRect 1098:0ED1 and TextBoxDraw 1068:258F. **8.2:**
  FlushSquareToWindow 11E8:07C3 is a frame only (a copy), for the kind check. A frame (at an entry before the shadow
  engine, whose macro targets read it; at an exit after it, whose leaves draw from it) keeps the primitive's argument
  words, its text (256 bytes), g_monochromeMode, the map scrolls, the shape handle (`g_mapSprites[index]`,
  `g_improvementIcons[id - 1, less 3 from 24]`, ids 22..24 none), the (v, ScaleLogical(v)) pairs of every
  ScaleLogicalX/Y inside it (the preimage source) and its MapLogicalPointToPort call; TruncateStringToLogicalWidth
  11D0:02E4 notes (buffer, original text, width) for the next draw of that buffer (10.3), TextBoxSetup 1068:2072 its
  text and device rectangle for the handle it returns, until TextBoxDrawAndClose's exit.
* **Target kind and surface** (B8). At the entry, from the globals MapLogicalPointToPort 1220:0F2F reads: 3 while
  g_currentDialogPort is set (in city mode the map transform, else ScaleLogical), 1 for 0 <= x < 200, 2 for y >= 8 and
  x >= 200, else 0; DrawScreenLine's point is (min x, y1). **8.2:** DrawImprovementIconLogical is ScaleLogical without
  the call (kind "scaled", 1030:84E1); DrawTextAtPortXY, DrawTextInRect and TextBoxDraw are device primitives (kind
  "device"). Under the observer, at MapLogicalPointToPort's exit inside a frame, its AX and the point it wrote are
  compared with the computed kind and point (`kind_checks0..3`, `kind_mismatch`, `point_mismatch`). The target is the
  shadow of the DC the primitive draws with: the map port's hdc for kind 2, `g_currentGrPort` for the shapes and the
  icon (`g_lastNonMainGrPort` for kind 3), else `g_graf`'s hdc.
* **Gating** (B 8.1; the record's deviation). Nothing is replayed while a scripted row (`PDOS_RD_SCRIPT`: intro,
  title, Birth, setup, names, Customize) is on the scene stack, also under a ready replay row above it (row 25's dialog
  148 and row 5's Play on EARTH, made ready while area C's timelines draw them); kinds 1 and 2 are drawn only into
  shadows that exist or that their owner claims; a logical shadow (kind 3, the device primitives in a dialog, the
  scaled icon) is drawn, and made lazily, only while the topmost non-R row is an N row whose replay renderer is ready
  (D7's renderers set it); kind 3 in city mode is counted (`city`; OB9, D7) and so is a call site whose DOS font is
  not drawn (`font_none`). **8.1:** a replay primitive inside another DROP bracket on the same surface is muted by the
  engine (B20) and draws nothing (`muted`): inside D's DrawMapTile, DrawUnit, DrawUnitAt and DrawCityLabels the DOS
  rule set is D's (D6c).
* **Drawing** at the exit, only when the bracket saw an event (`nothing` otherwise), so the core's quirks that decide
  whether something is drawn stay the core's: fills R:0000:0BFA, frames R:1D05:0A05 (the double frame with its
  one-pixel inset), lines R:0000:100A, text in the call site's DOS font through the Windows-1252 fold (R:0182:005C,
  centred R:0182:00B3, the one-pixel shadow R:0182:0086, none in monochrome mode; a truncated buffer by R:1F4D:04F7 on
  the original), DrawTextInRect centred vertically with the flags' alignment and 0x30 shadow, TextBoxDraw wrapped by
  R:1F4D:0000/0088 or line by line in the DC's text colour, shapes by provenance as G#6 sprites. **8.2:** replayed text
  is transparent: the core draws its report and play text with a TRANSPARENT background (OpenReportScreenWithHeader
  1018:0130, ShowCivilizationScore 1040:1172), and a scene whose DOS text is opaque gets that from its D7 renderer
  (inferred). Fonts (10.1): logical text by the font map at its call site, asked only once a target is decided
  (`font_unmapped` counts drawn text only); device text and shapes by the DOS font last set on the graf, font 1 when
  none is known (inferred).
* **Coordinates** (B 8.3, B9) by the target shadow's resolution. LOGICAL: kind 3 and the scaled icon at their
  logical point; device values through an exact preimage first (`preimages`), else L(d); a device rectangle [l, r) to
  [L(l), L(r - 1) + 1). **8.3:** MAP (k 2): the kind's device point through a k = 2 port whose origin carries the
  point's parity, so DOS 2 x 2 pixels start at the device pixel (both parities, odd and even scrolls); HALF: floor(d /
  2), the right and bottom edges rounded up.
* **Colours** (B 8.4): an EGA argument is the DOS index; a system index equal to `g_egaToSystemColor[c]` is c; then
  the scene row's colour table (`struct PdosColourMap`); **8.4:** then the nearest colour of the core palette's RGB
  in the DAC the shadow is shown with (the N machine's for a logical shadow, the A machine's, the play palette, for
  map and half shadows), counted `colour_nearest`; TextBoxDraw's COLORREF is PALETTEINDEX by the same rules, or its
  RGB's nearest. Since D6d (D6cF R2, the D6c review) the search in the A machine's DAC skips the play slots 60h..7Fh,
  which cycle; the N machine's DAC is searched whole (12.9 "As implemented (D6d; area D)"; FIDELITY F186).
* **Provenance** (`pdos_prov.h/.c`, new; B 8.5, 8.6; PROF 2.2, 2.3). At ExtractGameSpriteShapes 10E8:231F's exit the
  14 handle tables (775 handles) are read once and each handle is mapped by DOS-PROFILE.md 2.2's rows S1..S23 (S11,
  S12 have no counterpart, Open 15; S19..S22's sizes inferred there). **8.5:** a handle in two tables takes the last
  row that names one; a table entry no row names removes only a cut's entry left under a recycled handle. Cuts:
  GR_PicRead 10B8:079D's entry notes the port's picture; TileExtract 11A8:05C7 gives the port and device rectangle at
  its entry and the handle at its exit. **8.5:** TileExtract alone covers ExtractScaledTile 1160:33FC (which returns
  its handle, 1160:3469) and ExtractPictureTile2x; a rule for the port's picture (`pdos_prov_rule(lo, hi, fn)`, at
  most 16, D7's T1..T20) maps (picture, rectangle) to the counterpart, `PdosProvFn` taking `const PresentRect *`
  (draft B: `const W16Rect *`); without a rule the handle's entry is removed. **8.6:** picture 137 (EARTH) is never
  mapped. **8.5:** TileDispose 11A8:00D1 has no probe, so a disposed handle keeps its entry until a cut or a sheet
  read reuses it (the core never draws a disposed handle). An unknown handle draws nothing (`prov_unknown`). 4,096
  entries and a 65,536-word handle index (128 KiB); a full pool counts `no_memory`.
* **S3, option (a)** (`pdos_shadow.c/.h`; B 7.3). With `PDOS_MAC_LEAVE_DRAWS` (only with `PDOS_MAC_DROP`, else
  `macro_config`), while such a bracket owns its target, a copy FROM the target (DrawShapeAndFlush's flush 11E8:0776,
  CopyPortToWindow 10D0:0854) is queued in the bracket for the macro's rule sets (at most 8, `PDOS_S3_QUEUE`; a
  further one is applied at once and counted) and applied in arrival order after the leave, also when the bracket
  closes without its leave; it is dropped and counted when the bracket was detached (S7) or closed by a registration
  (S9) (`pdos_shadow_s3_deferred`, `_full`, `_dropped`). Copies of other rule sets, and those of macros without the
  flag (D's map macros), are applied when they arrive, as before. Only the replay's 16 macros set the flag.
  `PdosBracket.text_rgb` keeps the DC's text colour of its last TEXTOUT / DRAWTEXT (TextBoxDraw's colour). S3 of the
  D4a second review is closed.
* **The owner table**: setup, pmap and replay hold 3 of `PDOS_OWNER_MAX` 4 (the replay registers macros only, no
  hooks); D6e's class-A module takes the last slot or raises `PDOS_OWNER_MAX`. Since D6e setup, pmap, replay and
  aplay hold 4 of `PDOS_OWNER_MAX` 5 (raised by D6e; the fifth slot for the tests' own owner).

  `prov_unknown` appended to the `pdos` line; `tests/headless/harness.c`: the `replay ...` line of

* **Left to later units.** D6c: D's DOS rule set draws inside its map macros, where the replay is muted; D's

  N rows enable the logical replay; the T1..T20 rules use `pdos_prov_rule`; D7's renderers that make N replay rows

* **The setup box has DOS's rows only (D5cR; reverses ruling 4 and P17's Go Back row on the setup pages).**
  `pdos_scn_setup.c`'s `Page.nrows`: the difficulty page's five levels, the competition page's five counts, the
  tribe page's tribe rows. Tribe row k is the core's item k + 1 (the core's item 0 is "Custom.": `ncore` = rows + 1
  there), so `row_of_item(0)` is -1. No row maps to `PDOS_SETUP_GOBACK` (`gb_row` -1); the disabled Go Back row of
  the difficulty page, the Go Back row-only redraw and its page-0 snapshot are removed (11.5's B21 no longer
  applies). The core's Go Back and Custom. texts are still read for the state compare but not drawn, so
  `setup_text_diff` stays 0. **The bar where the core's selection has no DOS row** (the core preselects Custom. on
  the tribe page, or a Windows-profile click chose it): the bar stays where it was on the same page and starts on row
  0 on a new page, where MenuZ starts it (R:1D05:0031); the name pages' background draws the tribe page with the bar
  where the DOS -1 left it (`cbar`; row 0 when unknown). The core's Go Back is Windows-only on the three pages
  (FIDELITY F168, F169). test_pdos_setup's printed geometry: the difficulty box 7 -> 6 lines (bottom 97 -> 89), the
  tribe box 15 -> 13 lines with five rivals and 17 -> 15 with six.
* **Customize without a Cancel row; the choices in sync (D5eR; reverses ruling 5).** Each box has DOS's header and
  three items only (`pdos_scn_custom.c`: `LINES` 6, `build(b, q)`, `draw_box(q, t)`; `PDOS_CUSTOM_TEXT` and the
  Cancel row's text read removed); the box a question leaves stays as drawn, with its highlight, and D5e's redraw of
  the left box without the Cancel row is gone. The core's Cancel (0x66) is Windows-only (FIDELITY F170). **The sync
  criteria** (FIDELITY F171): a Windows radio click is a selection, not an answer (DOS's sequential order stays);
  (a) every DOS selection or choice is the core's selection before the next present; (b) every change of the core's
  selection, a Windows radio click included, is shown at once in every drawn DOS box when the DOS profile is
  presented (`g_worldParams[q]` read at the core points; no timer read, no RNG effect); (c) the DOS highlight never
  differs from the core's selection at a presented DOS frame. **The harness's check:** `pdos_custom_box_sel(q)` (box
  q's highlight as drawn) and `pdos_custom_stale()` (the drawn boxes whose highlight differs from the core's selection
  read now; 0 outside the questions), called only by the harness: `custom_check()` (`tests/headless/harness.c`)
  counts the shows and the stale boxes per visible profile at every presented frame while the questions are asked,
  and `presentation-diag.log` gains `custom_sync shows_dos N stale_dos N shows_windows N stale_windows N first_us N`
  (before D6b's `replay` line; RUNNING.md).
* **`pdos_scn_fame`** (`pdos_scn_fame.h/.c`, new; SCENES 7.5, SCENES-PLAY 7.17; DOS-PROFILE rows 7 and 105, both
  `PDOS_RD_SCRIPT`; ruling 6; FIDELITY F173). At ShowHallOfFame 1080:0119's entry (the probe; from the title the call
  with argument -1, row 7; at the end of a game the call with the new entry's slot, row 105) the view reads the core's
  entries (g_hallOfFame, six records of 4Eh bytes; `w16q_read` at the probe step only) and draws O3:00D7(slot) on
  page 0: the screen in colour 15, "CIVILIZATION" and "HALL OF FAME" (FAME_TEXT) centred at (160, 16) and (160, 24)
  in colour 0, the rule (80, 32)..(240, 32) in colour 14, entries 0..4 from y 40 (entry 5 only for slot 5, in entry
  4's place); OPEN L7 settled from the listing (an empty entry " ---", 26 rows on; a full one three lines 8 rows
  apart; the cut at x 320; the population in R:1DC4:0337's form; the rating's 16-bit product O3:0468..0477; the new
  entry recoloured 15 -> 14, O3:04C6, G#3). The slot's "Clear" (O3:0174..01AA, P18) is omitted. The pointer is hidden
  while the title's Hall of Fame shows; the screen stays on page 0 at ShowHallOfFame's exit and the title box is
  drawn over it again (`pdos_seq.c`, R:01A8:0807) (left in the DOS graphics; left in the Windows graphics: the
  title picture, D5P). F2 / F5: F2 not applicable (no G#18), F5 none (everything on the
  screen page). OFFSETS.md rows FAME_TEXT DGROUP:33BE, FAME_RANKS DGROUP:33A2, FAME_POP DGROUP:2FD2 (no writer
  found); `pdos_registry.h/.c` and `tests/dos-decoded.txt` regenerated (1354 -> 1357 lines).
* **The picture windows' owner (B 7.5).** The setup owner of `pdos_scn_setup.c` also claims template 148 while it is
  the DOS view's window (the intro movie's, the Hall of Fame's): the core's drawing into it (the movie's frames, the
  title's backdrop, Birth's frames and captions, the clears of the title branches, the Hall of Fame's text) is the
  DOS screens' until the core's play; in play a movie window is the engine's again, except the Hall of Fame's while
  its screen shows. This removes the 520 unexplained draws every DOS run counted in the intro: `pdos.unexplained`
  is 0 in the 12 V1 runs without play-screen drawing and 531 or 533 lower in the other 15.
* **Decision 8: the counters by scene row** (ruling 8). `pdos_diag_set_row_fn` (`pdos_common.h`): with a row
  function set, each counted event of the five gate counters is also counted for the row it returns
  (`pdos_assets.c` `row_note`; `PDOS_DR_UNEXPLAINED`, `_W_OVER_N`, `_FONT_UNMAPPED`, `_COLOUR_NEAREST`,
  `_PROV_UNKNOWN`); `pdos_profile.c` `diag_row`: the scene row on top of the stack; above another row the picture
  dialog's row 25 counts for that row (the function that opened it); 0 with an empty stack. `presentation-diag.log`
  gains `pdos_rows r<row>.<counter> <n> ...` (`present.c`; nonzero entries only). `ext_p0.py` requires 0 for D4's and
  D5's rows (`GATE_ROWS` 1..9, 16, 20, 21, 25, 27, 105, 116) in every V1 and V2 run; the rows that keep counts (row 0
  after a Load, the play rows of D6 and D7) are listed in `ext-D5-acceptance.md` "Decision 8" (since D6g, the D6 phase
  gate, `GATE_ROWS` also holds D6's 52 rows and `ROW_ALLOW`'s entries name the runs they cover: 12.9, "As implemented
  (D6g; the D6 phase gate)"; row 0's counts after a Load are D7k's, not D6's; since D7a `diag_row`
  also skips rows 25 and 98 above a report of the DOS report renderer, so a report's events count on its opener's
  row, 67..72: "As implemented (D7a; areas B and C)"). **The `w_over_n`
  fallback** (`pdos_profile.c`): a hidden scene window (the title dialog at its creation, dialog 143 at its close)
  has nothing over it, so those two moments are no longer counted (`pdos.w_over_n` 1..3 -> 0 in every run).
* **The general rule in the re-skins (B 11.2; reverses P17's extra row).** D5b's extra last row for a dialog's Go
  Back (102) or Cancel (2) is gone: the MenuZ box (dialogs 136 and 145, rows 14 and 22, and every later MenuZ
  re-skin) shows the dialog's items only, and a message box one row per button except IDCANCEL (2), the default
  highlighted, none when the default is the Cancel button. Escape passes to the core's MenuZ (I-menu/return-escape) or
  message box (IDCANCEL), **provisional per screen** (the orchestrator's condition; FIDELITY F172). `test_pdos_reskin`
  67 / 56 / 67. No recorded flow shows a re-skinned box over a DOS screen before D6 (checklist D5-7 moved to D6).
* **Obligations for D6 and D7** (`ext-D5-acceptance.md`): when a D6 or D7 renderer shows a re-skinned dialog over a
  DOS screen it checks the listing; where DOS's own Escape there does something else (e.g. asks again), Escape takes
  the DOS meaning and the core's button becomes Windows-only, recorded with the listing address. No D6 / D7 DOS
  screen draws or hit-tests a Go Back, Cancel or similar dismiss row the DOS screen lacks; any other row the DOS
  screen lacks is reported to the orchestrator before it is added. Decision 8's counters stay 0 for D4's and D5's
  rows; D6 and D7 extend `GATE_ROWS` with their own.

**As implemented (D6c; area B).** Record [ext-D6c-record.md](ext-D6c-record.md) (2026-10-16; BRIEF-d6.md "Units" D6c):
the A canvas's part of unit D6c. Area D's part, the DOS rule set of the map, the dos-settled check and the map element,
is in 12.9, "As implemented (D6c; area D)", with the landing (the three-step cycle ALL_GREEN 10:15 UTC,
`build/d6c-status.txt`).

* **The screen exclusions of an A element** (`pdos_compose.c/.h`). `PdosElem` gains `nexcl` and
  `excl[PDOS_EXCL_MAX]` (12: the 7 popups of 8 menu levels and an outline's 4 strips): canvas pixels inside an
  exclusion are left to the canvas below, so what is drawn on the screen over the windows (an open menu's popups, the
  move or size outline) shows over the element. A zeroed element has none, so every existing element (all are memset
  before use) is unchanged (`test_pdos_compose` 68, unchanged). The map element (D's `Wn_dos`, draft B 6.2's "map
  client" layer and 7.5's last bullet) is the first to set them. **Choice:** the exclusions live in `PdosElem` rather
  than in a map-only clip, since the move outline and the menu popups are drawn over every window and any later A
  element over a window needs them.
* **Palette.** The map element's `rgb8` is NULL, so it follows the A machine's play palette: D4a's wiring steps the
  play palette's slots at each `CyclePaletteColors` 1208:22B7 (`pdos_profile.c`) and `pdos_compose` reconverts every
  such element on a new palette generation, so the DOS map's cycling colours move at the core's pace with no new hook
  (14.7's part for the DOS ring stays D6d's; built in D6d: the zoom layer is converted again at each step, 12.9 "As
  implemented (D6d; area D)").
* **Nothing added over a dialog** (the user's direction of 2026-10-16). D6c adds no row, box, dialog or menu and draws
  nothing over any dialog: the map element is clipped to the map client's visible region, which excludes every window
  over it, and to the screen exclusions. Checklist D5-7 (a re-skinned box in play) is not D6c's: D5b's re-skins reach
  the A canvas in play with D6f (BRIEF-d6 decision 1). For D6f: elements compose in insertion order and a re-set
  keeps an element's place, but the map element never covers another window's area.

**As implemented (UX3; changed by UX5 and UX5b).** Record [ext-UX3-record.md](ext-UX3-record.md) (2026-10-16; the user's direction of about
10:21 UTC, STATUS "User directions"): the menu texts "Integer scaling" and "4:3 aspect ratio for 320 x 200 screens"
(12.2), 4:3 the default again with the cfg key `aspect_4_3` (12.2; FIDELITY F116 superseded again), and the DOS
scenes exempt from the option. Landed 2026-10-16 (the copy-in 11:41:57 UTC; the three-step cycle ALL_GREEN 12:04:47
UTC, `build/ux3-status.txt`; Release 12:08:47 UTC, user build `build/user-builds/ux3/`); compiled, headless,
Win32-headless and harness-tested in Checked (CheckedNoObs and Release privately in the stage). The core and its
inputs are unchanged (P0 in `ext_aspect.py`, `EXT_G1_OK runs 77`). Unit UX5 (record
[ext-UX5-record.md](ext-UX5-record.md), 2026-10-16; the user's direction of about 19:39 UTC, "4:3 shall be applied to
DOS intro and DOS title screen also when selected update accordingly"; FIDELITY F116, F187) narrows the exemption to
DOS Birth: landed 2026-10-16 (the copy-in 20:28:06 UTC; the three-step cycle ALL_GREEN 21:17:06 UTC,
`build/ux5-status.txt`; Release 21:23:22 UTC, user build `build/user-builds/ux5/`); compiled, headless and
harness-tested in Checked. No core change (P0), no RNG use, no host or harness code change. Unit UX5b (record
[ext-UX5b-record.md](ext-UX5b-record.md), 2026-10-17; the user's revision list of about 07:00 UTC, item 3: the title
"momentarily ... squashed to square aspect" before Birth; FIDELITY F116) moves Birth's square start from Birth's start
to its first picture: landed 2026-10-17 (the copy-in 09:35:23 UTC; the three-step cycle ALL_GREEN 10:49:18 UTC after a
void first attempt, `build/ux5b-status.txt`; Release 10:56:23 UTC, user build `build/user-builds/ux5b/`); compiled,
headless and harness-tested in Checked (CheckedNoObs and Release privately in the stage). No core change (P0), no RNG
use, no host, harness-program or `present.c` change: one presentation-state word and one predicate in the DOS profile.
The first two bullets are UX5's rule as UX5b changed it; UX3's and D5P's earlier switch points follow as history.

* **The DOS profile's `square_only`** (`pdos_profile.c` `dos_square_only` = `pdos_seq_square_scene()`, `pdos_seq.c/.h`;
  the member is 12.5's). Since UX5 1, square whatever the option, only while the Birth view is on and the setup page
  is not yet shown (UX5: `g_bview && !pdos_setup_shown()`: DOS Birth, its story, its end fade and the black before the
  first setup page); since UX5b only from DOS Birth's first picture (O7:1A59, the copy of the picture to the shown page
  under the black DAC after the title's fade O7:1A39; `pdos_birth_pictured(&g_birth)`, kept when the core's Birth end
  abandons it, C.8.8) while the Birth view is on and until the setup page is shown: `pdos_seq_square_scene` =
  `g_bview && pdos_birth_pictured(&g_birth) && !pdos_setup_shown()`. `PdosBirthVars.shown` (`pdos_tl.h`, 22 of the
  union's 32 words) is set right after the O7:1A59 copy (`pdos_birth.c`, case `PDOS_BIRTH_PC_O7_1A41`) and cleared in
  the prepare (O7:0012); `pdos_birth_pictured(tl)` is 1 when the timeline is Birth's program, started (also after an
  abandon), its sequence `PDOS_SEQ_BIRTH` (not the post-Birth `FX_FADE`) and `shown` set. It reads `g_birth`, the story
  timeline, so a Birth ended after its first picture stays square through `g_post`'s end fade to the setup page, and
  one the core ends before it (Play on EARTH with a key during the title's fade) is never square. The flag was chosen
  over the BS3 phase (`pdos_bsync_of`), which is a current state, not a history, and BS's; else 0, the option
  applies: the title's fade to black at Birth's start (O7:1A39, since UX5b), the start-up (BOOT: the black, the credits, the panorama, the logo
  and title reveal, a held logo), the title box (after the intro, the C.6.6 box over the pan or the logo, and every
  redraw after a return from Customize World, the Hall of Fame or Load in either graphics), the outro, Customize
  World, the Hall of Fame, Load and every later scene (the setup and name pages, dialog 151's text, play, the end
  game's Hall of Fame). UX3's `g_ux3_branch` and its seven assignments are removed. D5P's `g_tb_sq` is renamed
  `g_tb_on_title` and keeps only its log role: the step `title_shape` still precedes each `title_redraw`, with arg 1
  for the box on the title picture or the intro's screen and 0 over a screen the branch drew (page 0 changed since
  the choice), the observable that tells a DOS-side return from a Windows-side one (`ext_adapt.py`'s D5P checks and
  `ext_aspect.py` read it); it no longer decides the shape. The Windows profile's `square_only` is NULL (it shows no
  320x200 frame).
* **The switch points** (since UX5; the first since UX5b). With the option on, a new game (Start a New Game, Play on
  EARTH, Customize World's OK) has at most two: 4:3 to square at Birth's first picture (O7:1A59, on the black frame
  after the title's fade O7:1A39; none when the core ends Birth before it), square to 4:3 when the setup page is shown
  at the hand-over R:01A8:087C (`pdos_setup_shown()`; Birth's end fade R:01A8:0840 ends black, so no Birth frame is
  4:3 and no setup frame square). Before UX5b the first was at Birth's start (SeekSequence ra 11E0:01AA, C.8.1 T1),
  about 1 s before the first picture, so the title's fade showed square (the user's report; 41 presented frames in the
  harness's fame run). Customize World, the Hall of Fame and Load have none:
  4:3 from the title on, 4:3 after their returns. A change of the option is a third kind, at once: the system menu's
  SC_CIV_ASPECT goes through `hw_set_presentation` -> `present_set_options` (`show_all`: `relayout`, the whole client
  scaled again, borders included) -> `refresh_cursor` -> `InvalidateRect`; the harness's `at <ms> policy integer
  square|4:3` calls the same `present_set_options`. Before UX5 that path ran during the intro and the title too, but
  `square_only` gave 1 there, so the layout stayed square. With the option off every DOS screen is square, as before.
* **Before UX5** (UX3's rule as amended by D5P; history). `square_only` was also 1 while the scene was BOOT, or the
  intro view was on (`g_view`), no title branch's screen was up (`g_ux3_branch` 0) and the scene was INTRO, TITLE,
  CUSTOM (before dialog 143's transition) or OUTRO. `g_ux3_branch` was set at a title branch's start
  (LoadGameFromFileDialog's entry, ShowHallOfFame's entry from the title, dialog 143's open when its transition
  starts) and cleared when the title box was drawn on the title picture (`tb_show`: R:01A8:0494, and since D5P the
  redraw after a Windows-side return, on the restored title picture), at the intro's start, at Birth's start and at
  the reset; since D5P it was kept when the box was drawn again over the branch's own screen (D5P (b), `g_tb_sq`, step
  `title_shape`: `tb_show` set `g_ux3_branch = (g_tb_sq == 0)`; before D5P the redraw R:01A8:0807 always cleared it).
  So the start-up and the title box were square (the record's decision 5); Customize World 4:3 from the transition's
  start at dialog 143's open (R:01A8:0591), after Cancel square from the redraw on the title picture, after OK 4:3
  through the outro; the Hall of Fame and Load 4:3 from their entries, left in the DOS graphics 4:3 through the box's
  redraw and a following choice's outro until Birth (the user's direction of 2026-10-16 about 13:56 UTC after build
  "sl", unit D5P, FIDELITY F187; before D5P square from the box's redraw), left in the Windows graphics square from
  the return; Start a New Game and Play on EARTH square through the outro and Birth. Checklist UX3-6 passed again
  2026-10-16 about 19:57 UTC in build `build/user-builds/d5p/`; UX5-1..UX5-7 check UX5's rule (build
  `build/user-builds/ux5/`).
* **The relayout** (`present.c` `frame_aspect`, `relayout_due`, `g_lay_aspect`). A 320x200 frame is laid out at 4:3
  only when the option is on and the visible profile's `square_only` gives 0; a change re-lays out as a frame-size
  change does (12.5, tick step 5a): the whole client drawn again, the cursor issued again at the new layout's factors;
  the mapping of host points always uses the shown layout. The harness's default follows civ64 (4:3); its
  `--presented 2` adds the layout to each show line and the pointer factors to each cursor line.
* **Tests.** `test_pdos_aspect` (new, 61 checks; the predicate along the DOS flows and through the present layer),
  `test_present` 951 (`profiles_square_only`), `w32_test_host_win32` 329 (`test_ux3`, 12.2), `ext_aspect.py` in the
  new Checked section `ux3_aspect` of `test.cmd` (`EXT_ASPECT_OK`, 16 checks: six runs, every 320x200 show square or 4:3
  as the switch points say, every DOS pointer at the shown layout's factors, P0 between the 4:3 and square runs).
  Since UX5: `test_pdos_aspect` 93 checks (the predicate 0 from the reset through the intro, the title box, the
  branches, their redraws and the outro, 1 from Birth's start to the setup page with one change at the hand-over;
  through the present layer at 1920x1080 under 4:3 the intro and the title box laid out 4:3 and each switch of the
  option shown at once, the whole client drawn again), `test_pdos_seq` 214 (D5P's six title-picture vectors expect 0),
  `ext_aspect.py` 32 checks in nine runs (with `intro`, the whole DOS intro with no key and the option switched in the
  credits, the panorama, the logo, on the title box and in Birth, and `intro_ref`, its keys without the switches, P0;
  the new check 8: each switch shown at once, square inside Birth). Since UX5b: `test_pdos_aspect` 140 checks (a
  content checker classifying every presented 320x200 frame from Birth's start by what page 0 shows, black, the title,
  a Birth picture or the setup page, and requiring its shape: title 4:3, Birth square, setup 4:3, black either; along
  Start a New Game, Play on EARTH with the core's Birth end during the title's fade, EARTH to its picture, D4b4's
  auto-continue, Customize World and the Hall of Fame; through the present layer the item and the graphics switched
  during the title's fade and in Birth), `test_pdos_seq` 215 (D5P (b)'s Birth vector: 0 at Birth's start, 1 from its
  first `show_picture`), `ext_aspect.py` 52 checks in 12 runs (with `earth_fade`, `earth_fade_sq` and `fame_sw`; check
  2's square span from Birth's first `show_picture`; check 9: every show from Birth's start to 1 s after its setup page
  fits its content, no tolerance). The base fails them (41 squashed title frames in each of the fame, custom,
  fame_win and intro runs, 59 in EARTH's). User checks UX5b-1..UX5b-7 and the rewritten UX5-5.

**As implemented (D6e; area B).** Record [ext-D6e-record.md](ext-D6e-record.md) (2026-10-16/17; BRIEF-d6.md "Units"
D6e): draft B 12.1..12.4 (the DOS small map, the Status window and the unit panel as class-A elements), B 5.5 item 2
(the play palette's return), the D6c review's R3 (12.9 "D6cF") and draft D 19 ("not zoomed"). Landed 2026-10-17 (the
copy-in 00:55:23 UTC; the three-step cycle ALL_GREEN 01:23:53 UTC, `build/d6e-status.txt`; Release 01:28:06 UTC,
user build `build/user-builds/d6e/`); compiled and tested headless in Checked (CheckedNoObs and Release privately in
the stage); P0 holds (`P0_OK checks 476` unchanged). FIDELITY F131 (implemented), F132 (extended), F191 (the
deviations).

* **The module** `present/dos/pdos_aplay.h/.c` ("A-canvas play"; draft B 2's `pdos_play.c`, renamed because
  `pdos_pal.h` already uses the `pdos_play_*` names for the play palette): public names `pdos_aplay_*`, the shadow
  engine's owner "aplay", the size macros `PDOS_APLAY_SM_W` / `PDOS_APLAY_SM_H` (80 x 50).
* **Seven DROP macros** of the owner "aplay" (the DOS rule set only; B 7.3): DrawSmTiles 1210:09B4, RedrawSmMap
  1210:073F and PlotSmMapSquare 1210:07AE (the small map), RedrawStatusWindow 1218:0801, DrawStatusCivPanels
  1088:13D5, DrawMiniPalace 1088:1A82 (its target only inside DrawStatusCivPanels) and DrawUnitInfoPanel 11F0:468C
  (the Status window); their targets are the World Map's and the Status window's hwnds; the leaves draw nothing.
* **Drawing at the functions' exits.** Each element is drawn whole from the module's model at the exit of the
  function that changed it (`pdos_aplay_on_probe` from the profile's exit step 3, after the engine's leaves: the
  panel functions nest, and a leave inside an enclosing DROP bracket is muted), into a surface of the module that
  the A canvas composes at the window's client, clipped to its visible region and to what is drawn over the windows
  (open popups, the move/size outline: the map element's rule; `pdos_aplay_present` at every core present). A hidden
  or minimised window, or one without a visible region, removes its element. The module claims no shadow, so the
  core's drawing to the two windows reaches no DOS shadow and nothing is counted unexplained (the status element is
  not a claimed HALF shadow: a deviation, F191).
* **The small map** (PROF row 139; Choice B17's alternative: the module's own reads, since the include rule keeps
  `present/dos` from `present/map`'s headers and D's snapshot holds the main map's view only). At DrawSmTiles' exit
  the module reads through the query the state DrawSmTiles read (the visibility with the viewer's mask, the terrain
  through the screen buffer and `g_terrainByMapColor`, the cities, the human, `g_smMapScale`, the view frame and
  origin, the World Map's visible flag). The model is one DOS colour per square: never seen 0, ocean 1, land 2 (OB6
  settled, R:0866:034C..0376), a city in its owner's main colour DGROUP:1946. It is drawn at the core's scale (one
  square `g_smMapScale` device pixels, element factor 1) with its east-west wrap copy, colour 0 outside the image,
  the bevel R:1D05:0A66 at the client's edges and the view frame (D's P9) as a colour-15 outline R:1D05:0A05 one DOS
  pixel outside the core's frame (F131, F191). PlotSmMapSquare's exit plots one square in the DOS colour of the
  core's (EGA 6 land -> DOS 2, PROF row 132; EGA 15, the blink, 15; the blink since DBL on the DOS clock,
  `pdos_ublink.h`, 12.9 "As implemented (DBL; areas B and D)"), MoveUnit's city plot in the owner's main colour.
  **The zoom area** (MZW, 12.9): `pmap_smrect.c` calls `pdos_aplay_set_zoom_area` at each evaluation of the World
  Map's patch, so at -1 and +1 the outline surrounds the area the main map window shows (MZW's rectangle) with its
  wrap copies, at level 0 the core's frame; the squares are not changed by it.
* **The Status window** (PROF rows 136, 138). Inside the three panel functions the TEXTOUTs into the Status back port
  are captured in order (the status panel's population, year and treasury/rates lines; the unit panel's lines), the
  DrawTile entries give the mini palace's handles by slot and the research and warming icons' (`PDOS_PT_STATUS`),
  `g_peaceTurns` is read at DrawStatusCivPanels' exit and the unit model taken at DrawUnitInfoPanel's (the veteran
  flag, the city's two stored units, the stack ring in the core's order, at most 128). The column is drawn in Choice
  B18's layout at 2x from the client's top-left on a DOS-resolution surface: the texture everywhere, the status panel
  DOS (0, 58, 80, 39) at (0, 0) (the sky 11, the peace bar colour 15 when n > 1, OPEN L4: D808 as the peace turns,
  inferred; the ground line 2; the palace strips by provenance; the lines in font 1; the icons), the unit panel DOS
  (0, 97, 80, 103) at (0, 39) extended to the bottom (the lines, "Veteran", the garrison and the stack ring four to a
  row, "+" when units are left; for the end of turn "End of Turn", "Press Enter", "to continue"). The "End of Turn"
  blink: the core's EGA 14 or 0 outside the panel functions sets the word's colour, DOS 7 or 0 (PROF row 132); since
  DBL on the DOS clock (`pdos_ublink.h`; 12.9 "As implemented (DBL; areas B and D)"): 7 in phase A, 0 in phase B, the
  core's TEXTOUTs ignored during the blink. The
  unit panel's lines are the core's (Windows) strings with the DOS static words (OB7 not needed; F191).
* **The play palette's return** (B 5.5 item 2, decision 5). At RestoreMapAfterDialog 1208:14D5's entry the A
  machine's DAC becomes SP256.PAL with entries 128..255 from `BACK*.PAL` (R:0238:1BEB) as GetCourtStyle 1160:3487
  selects it (OB1 settled, R:0238:1C11..1C46: government / 2 toward 0, 'm' after advance 14h, government 3 alone
  '3'); a style without a DOS file (back3m) or a missing file leaves the palette (`pal_none`). Applied only when the
  recipe differs from the DAC outside the play slots 60h..7Fh (`pal_same` otherwise): the core calls
  RestoreMapAfterDialog after every dialog, the DOS program R:0238:1BEB only after full-screen scenes (F191); the TX

* **The report renderer** (`present/dos/pdos_scn_report.c/.h`, new; 297 portable sources). A table of the reports it
