  already pushes rows 67 (ShowCityStatus's entry), 25 (GR_DialogOpen of dialog 148, bound to its window) and 98
  (CloseReportScreen's entry); the renderer makes them ready while the report shows, so `pdos_scene_wants_n` turns the
  frame to N (320 x 200) for the report and back to A at its end. The frame begins at OpenReportScreenWithHeader's
  entry inside a report of the table (O14:0000/001C): the play palette kept (PDOS_PAL_KEEP: the O14 reports change no
  DAC entry), page 0 filled with the opener's background argument (8 for City Status, as O14:186F passes), rows 67, 98
  and 25 ready (row 25's earlier readiness restored at the end), the pointer hidden (O14:0000 .. O14:013B).
  ProcessCity(city, -1) inside the report (a computation without a screen, DOS R:0D12:0045) pushes row 97, whose
  readiness is held while it runs, so the frame never turns to A inside it. The end is the report window's destruction
  (GR_DialogClose destroys it before calling RestoreMapAfterDialog), a recycled window handle or the opener's exit
  without a destroy: the rows are no longer ready, the report's exit kind is named for the return, the step
  `report_end` is logged. Decision 8: `diag_row` skips rows 25 and 98 above a report, so its events count on the
  opener's row (12.7 "As implemented (D5f; area B)").

  F213).
* **The negative claim** (`pdos_shadow.c/.h`). Inside a DrawTile on the report port DrawTileImpl makes its own bitmaps
  (the buffer composed as raw bits by SetBitmapBits, or without a palette device the image and mask bitmaps), and
  DrawTile is an entry-only probe, so no DROP bracket can hold them. The report declines a shadow for every bitmap
  made while its DrawTile runs: a claim below 0 makes no shadow, neither a later owner's nor the engine's own, and is
  asked again at the next get; their copies and raw bits are not followed and their copy to the port is dropped (the
  DOS shape was drawn at the DrawTile's entry). Existing owners return 0 or 1 and are unaffected; the replay's own
  DrawTile brackets (DrawShapeAndFlush's, DrawImprovementIconLogical's) are left to it. Later owners (D7d, D7j) may
  decline their transient bitmaps the same way.
* **Intelligence's INFO wait, the dossier and the return.** From ShowIntelligenceAdvisor's first PollMouseClick
  (1108:0000, polled at 1018:1641, `RA_INFO_POLL`) until CloseReportScreen or the dossier's opener the renderer is in
  the INFO wait: the DOS pointer shown (O14:1079) and `pdos_report_info_wait()` selecting the scene
  `PDOS_AS_REPORT_INFO` (0x04000000) with three rows I-report/info (C.12.8): a lone modifier consumed; any key passed
  ('1'..'7' choose a dossier in both); either button passed at the class-P point of the DOS point in the report's
  dialog 148 (C.14.2: the client origin + ceil(16L / 10), which PollMouseClick's (d * 10) / 16 turns back into the DOS
  point), consumed without the window or outside it. `pdos_adapt.c` gains the class-P path for frame-1 presses;
  `adapters.py` allows class P for that scene only. The dossier (ShowNestedCivDossier 1018:176C; O14:1164) nests over
  the report (the outer window, port and background kept), is drawn in background 1 and at its window's destruction
  returns to the report's window, still N; the INFO wait starts again at the next poll. A report that showed a dossier
  ends with exit kind 1B44 (O14:110A, 1147..114D), else none (O14:1154). The core's CloseReportScreen after the poll
  waits for a second key or click (FIDELITY F210); Military's second page follows the first on the N frame without the
  map between them (F211).

  g_terrainByMapColor, g_cities, g_units) through the renderer's query (`w16q_read`; an injected function in the

  units and cities, 0..31h when row 0 or 49 is seen; both 0 with D806); a square the human has seen (or every square

  without D806); units as 3 x 3 squares with a black shadow in [1946h + 2 civ] (a foreign civilization's only when

the D7a+D7b landing and its docs pass, UX5b's, D7c's, DBL's, D7j's and D6fS-a's landings and the docs pass docs-e4c
(three-way merges in `build/d7d-stage/obj/rebase-*/`; the registry files and DOS-PROFILE.md 1.7.2 regenerated, not

ALL_GREEN 15:29:05 UTC (`build/d7d-status.txt`), Release 15:32:56 UTC, user build `build/user-builds/d7d/`; compiled

the rename and the sale and Buy questions (D7f, D7g); until then they show the A frame (F237). (The city view and the

question since D7f: "As implemented (D7f; areas B and C)" below.)

* **Why a renderer** (the record's deviation 1). The core composes its city screen in two ports, g_cityScreenPort
  1260:0070 and g_cityOverlayPort 1260:0032, mostly with DrawTileToPortPixels 11A8:15CE (raw bits: no probe, no GDI
  event), so the logical replay sees almost none of it. The DOS-layout renderer (`present/dos/pdos_scn_city.c/.h`,
  new; 301 portable sources) draws R:0D12:0045's layout from the core's state read through `w16q_read` (the cities,
  the human's units, the unit and improvement types, the civilizations' advances, wonders and governments, the city's
  scratch totals 1260:00AE..00E3, the panel mode, the list's scroll) and the map module's hooks
  (`present/map/pmap_dos.c`: terrain, improvements, specials, the body, units, visibility, the square and unit drawers
  at k = 1), under the coordinator's three conditions: read only (no core call, no core random number, no emulation
  write; obs_lint clean); the self-check (at every render the transcription's food R:0D12:692D / 6ABC, shields with
  the factory and power-plant bonuses R:0D12:1357..14D0, trade with the routes R:0D12:283B..28D6, happy and unhappy
  after every HAPPY stage R:0D12:2AF2..32DD, the core's 1030:3694..3F4D, against g_cityYields, g_cityShieldTotal,
  g_cityTrade, g_cityHappyCount and g_cityUnhappyCount; a difference counted `mismatch_*`, 0 in every run); the core's
  totals win (every total shown is the core's; the transcription places only the per-square icons and the
  intermediate HAPPY rows; FIDELITY F231).
* **The session.** At ProcessCity's entry with mode 1 the dialog-148 window created before GR_DialogOpen's exit at ra
  1030:00F8 is the city window and the bitmaps of the two GR_PortOpen calls (1030:019B, 1030:01C3) are the ports; the
  N machine keeps the play palette and page 0 is filled black (R:0D12:04A2..04BA); rows 97 and 25 are made ready.
  Every copy into the city window with SRCCOPY (CopyPortToWindow 10D0:0854) renders the whole DOS screen again
  (R:0D12:045F's full redisplay); a nested ProcessCity(city, -1) keeps the session. `pdos_city_sync`, after every
  event and probe, keeps rows 97 and 25 ready only while the topmost non-R level of the scene stack is the session's
  (F237). At the window's destruction or ProcessCity's exit the rows are restored and the exit kind is named for D7a's
  return trigger (`pdos_fx_exit_set`): R:0238:1B44 where DOS writes [6B64] = 1 (R:0D12:4FE3 VIEW, 56BF RENAME, 54A1
  the map cursor 'p', 589B the sale of the City Walls: building bit 7 seen lost between two renders), else none
  (R:0ADE:040B..0419 restores the saved screen).
* **The layout** (R:0D12:0483..4851): the header and the 5x5 map (the city's name upper-cased with its population
  R:1DC4:0337, the squares by R:1AEA:03BA / 11D4, unseen squares skipped for the human, the colour-12 outlines of the
  squares the other cities within distance 5 work, R:0D12:014F..02F7); the yield icons (R:0D12:692D's step, icon 0Dh
  for a zero); the tabs and the lower panel (2687..277B) with the HAPPY rows (2AF2..32DD), the MAP tab's world map

* **The core's arrival and DOS's** (DOS listing; the core's reconstruction). ShowSpaceshipArrival(arrivingCiv) opens
  dialog 148 (GR_DialogOpen(0x94) 11D8:0092), clears the screen port, loads picture 0x15B3 into a picture port
  (LoadPictureToPortBitmap 11D8:00F6; 0x15B4 only in the dead monochrome branch, WI 1.2) with its palette, fills
  g_textSubstParams 13C8:0000, copies the picture to the window and opens a 512 x 30 caption port (GR_PortOpen
  11D8:035E); its first loop takes 153 steps of 12 ticks (11D8:03A8..0542, each step ending with CopyPortToWindow
  11D8:0517), ended by SetGameCursor 11D8:0546; then for the human's ship PlaySoundEffect(1) 11D8:05A8 and picture
  0x15B4 (11D8:05C8), for another civilization's GR_DialogClose 11D8:06AC and OpenPalaceView(1) 11D8:06B6; its second
  loop takes up to 480 steps of 5 ticks, a caption every 40 (11D8:06FC), PollMouseClick 11D8:07DB and IsKeyPending at
  each step, ending at a key or click; then WaitForKeyOrClick 11D8:081C. DOS (ovl02.lst O2:0BD7..1529, DGROUP:D762
  set, the VGA path): the set-up (PLANET2.PIC's 24 cuts 49 x 49, PLANET1.PIC with its palette into page 1, font 6, the
  dot at x -10); the text (KING.TXT's section `*SPACE` for the human's ship, `*SPACE2` for another's, its five tokens
  replaced, its length taken, its first byte then set to 0 at O2:0ECA); the pan, 320 columns of 8 frames with a
  catch-up skip (O2:10A9..10C6), each column refilling page 1's band with the next '^'-chunk every 22 columns, the
  planet's next cut (k mod 23) at (135, 51), page 0's bands and planet scene shifted in from the right, the ship's dot
  on odd columns; the rest of the text after the pan (O2:119F..11DC); the human's ship (SPACEST.PIC into page 1, the
  fade to black in 5 steps, SPACEST's palette, six cycling slots O2:12A1..134C) or another's (O17:07EC(1) at O2:1356);
  the rest's chunks (O2:137E..145C: the chunk centred at (160, 5) in 11 and (160, 4) in 15, the dot (280, 32) on,
  wait(80), off, wait(80); no key test), the last chunk not drawn; the key wait R:1459:0918 (O2:146C), the slots
  stopped, page 0 black, R:0238:1BEB and the caller's R:0238:1B44 (R:0238:0792). The pointer is hidden before O2:0BD7.
* **The drawing** (`present/dos/pdos_scn_arrive.c/.h`, new, pure; 305 portable sources): the listing's drawing as
  functions of the N machine (pages 0 and 1, the DAC), the pictures and the text, with no emulation, clock or random
  number: `pdos_arrive_text`, `_begin`, `_step` (one pan column), `_rest`, `_next`, `_chunk`, `_dot`, `_page1`,
  `_show`, `_copy1`, `_clear`, and the six slots' table (1: 15 10h..1Fh, 2: 100 20h..22h, 3: 42 23h..28h, 4: 300
  29h..2Fh, 5: 60 30h..35h, 6: 37 36h..3Fh). The tokens' values are the core's five strings ($US, $THEM, $BUCKS,
  $RPLC1, $RPLC2 = g_textSubstParams[0..4]), read through `w16q_read` at the first step, folded (B 10.4); the bytes
  past the text are zero.
* **The pace and the flow** (TC, TX; `present/dos/pdos_scn_space.c`, the owner "space"). The set-up at GR_DialogOpen's
  return (11D8:0097: the play palette with PLANET1's over it, rows 130 and 25 ready, the N frame; TS); the text at the
  first step's copy (11D8:0517); the pan's columns at the core's step s: floor(320 s / 153) ..
  floor(320 (s + 1) / 153) - 1, spread evenly over its 12 ticks from the copy and never past them (TC; F260); the pan's end at SetGameCursor
  11D8:0546 (the remaining columns at once); the human's ship at PlaySoundEffect(1) 11D8:05A8 (SPACEST into page 1, the
  fade of 5 steps, 30 frames, then the picture and the six slots) and another civilization's at OpenPalaceView(1)
  11D8:06B6 (the sad palace composed from the core's palace state by D7j's composer, its fade with DGROUP:DEBA taken as
  8 (F264), SAD's palette and page 1 to page 0; row 104 ready), both TX on the arrival's own timeline
  (PDOS_SEQ_FX_FADE); the rest's chunks at the core's second-loop step n (PollMouseClick 11D8:07DB): chunk n / 40, the
  dot on for the first 20 steps of the 40 and off for the last 20 (TC); DOS's key wait after the last chunk, or the
  core's WaitForKeyOrClick 11D8:081C after a key or click (F261); the end at ShowSpaceshipArrival's return: the slots
  stopped, page 0 black, the rows back, exit kind 1 (R:0238:1B44). The TX timelines reach each probe's time before it
  is handled (`run_tl`); the core's end inside a fade completes it at once (`pdos_tl_tail`).
* **The windows, the ports, the rows and the input.** The arrival's window (dialog 148) and, for another
  civilization's ship, the palace window of OpenPalaceView(1) are claimed by "space" at the logical resolution: every
  core drawing and copy on them is absorbed (`arrive_absorbed`) and the replay's place hook draws nothing there. The
  bitmaps the arrival's own ports make (LoadPictureToPortBitmap 11D8:00F6 and 05C8, GR_PortOpen 11D8:035E, and inside
  OpenPalaceView(1)) are declined while the arrival runs (no shadow: the claim's negative return, D7b's mechanism;
  `arrive_bitmaps`; F265). Row 130 is pushed at ShowSpaceshipArrival's entry (the A frame until its dialog opens);
  row 25 (dialog 148) and, for the palace window, row 104 are held ready while the arrival shows and put back as they
  were at its end. The DOS pointer is hidden from the set-up to the end (`pdos_space_pointer_hidden`,
  `pdos_profile.c`'s cursor rule). `pdos_adapt.c` makes the arrival the scene PDOS_AS_WAIT with its window (or the
  palace window) as the class-X window, so the I-wait rows decide: a key passes, a left press passes at that window,
  the right button and a lone modifier are consumed (F263). No new PDOS_AS bit (0x10000000 stays free) and no new
  shadow owner (8 of `PDOS_OWNER_MAX` 9); DOS-PROFILE 1.7.2 regenerated (the I-wait rows' texts name the arrival).
* **DOS's star motion on the spaceship screen** (row 102; F259, F238 changed). DOS's wait loop O18:0AF3..0B38 moves
  its 20 stars (O18:0C49) after waiting 4 frames before the launch and 2 after it (O18:0B01..0B18: the civilization's
  bit of DGROUP:4CD8), in the wait for a key and in a timed showing alike; the core moves its stars once per showing
  and, in the wait, only after the launch (1070:0E59..0E88). Where the core's stars stand (the wait before the launch,
  a timed showing) DOS's motion now runs on the screen's own timeline (TX, PDOS_SEQ_FX_FADE) from the showing's first
  copy (1070:0DBD): per star its erase, its class's step (+2, +1, +1 on an odd count), the wrap at x F0h to x 1 with a
  new row `pdos_random(C0h) + 8` (R:1DC4:005D at O18:0C77; effect "space_stars", one instance per start:
  `pdos_fx_rand`), its point where page 0 is 0, drawn into the port's shadow and mirrored to the window's and page 0.
  It stops at the core's next star frame (whose stars, at the core's own positions, replace the moved ones), the
  launch, EvaluateAndShowSpaceship's entry or return and the screen's end. The generator never reads, writes or
  advances the core's g_randSeed and reads no clock.

**As implemented (D7e; areas B and C).** Record [ext-D7e-record.md](ext-D7e-record.md) (session E4, 2026-10-17;
`verify/BRIEF-d7.md` "Units" D7e, track B): with the DOS graphics three scenes of play are the DOS game's, drawn on the
N canvas (320 x 200) from the core's state while the core runs its Windows counterparts: the city view O19:0000 (row
88; City > View, V or the VIEW tab of the DOS city screen, a wonder or improvement completed, the view after a
founding), the founding scene O22:0EAF (row 109) and the city animations O19:167B (the invaders), O19:18C1 (the riot)
and O19:1AE1 (the celebration) (rows 111..113). Any key or a left press ends a scene where the core's wait or Button()
takes it (I-wait, class X); no Go Back or Cancel row exists (DOS has none). Staged on the tree after D7d's landing,
rebased onto D7l's, D7m's, D6fS-b's, HM's and D7l2's landings and the docs passes docs-e4d and e4e by three-way merges
(`git merge-file -p`; OFFSETS.md's two conflicts with D7l2 resolved with both kept, D7l2's first; `pdos_profile.c`'s
cursor rule with both terms, `pdos_space_pointer_hidden() || pdos_cityview_pointer_hidden()`; the registry's outputs
regenerated; `adapters.py gen` leaving the merged DOS-PROFILE.md unchanged), then onto D6fSc's landing (OFFSETS.md, `ext_adapt.py`, `pdos_registry.c/.h` and `tests/dos-decoded.txt` merged
and regenerated; `BASE-SHA256.txt` written again, 18 files; `build/d7e-prep/land.py` `DRY_RUN_OK 18`); landed alone after D6fSc
(the copy-in at 22:59:02 UTC; the three-step cycle ALL_GREEN 23:42:19 UTC, `build/d7e-status.txt`; Release
23:46:36 UTC, user build `build/user-builds/d7e/`); compiled and tested headless in Checked (CheckedNoObs and
Release privately in the stage); P0 holds (`P0_OK checks 476` unchanged). FIDELITY F269..F276 (F237 noted); FORMATS
6.1's `[DGROUP:6444]` established. The Windows graphics are unchanged. Not built (the deviations): DOS's layout
generator's own draws (the core's grid is drawn), DOS's animation frame counts (the core's pace). Not in the unit: the
nuclear explosion (D7n; since D7n: "As implemented (D7n; areas B and C)" below), the production list, the rename and
the questions over the city screen (D7f, D7g).

* **The city view** (row 88; SCENES-PLAY 7.2; DOS listing O19:0000..0FEE; FIDELITY F269). O19:0000 lays the city out
  on a 19 x 12 grid (index 12 gx + gy) seeded by the sum of the name's letters; the core's ShowCityView 11B0:0000 does
  the same with its own generator (DOS-PROFILE Open 21: the generators differ, the DOS one reseeds the shared C
  stream). The module never generates: it reads the core's grid from ShowCityView's frame (`[bp-110h]`, BP = the entry
  probe's arg_base - 6) at the core's copy into the view's window (CopyPortToWindow 11B0:10F9), with the city record,
  the wonders' cities, the owner's and the human's advances and the happy, unhappy and specialist counts (`w16q_read`
  only). **The drawing** (page 1, then to page 0): HILL.PIC (O19:0913); the big wonders 1, 3, 7 and 15 from
  WONDERS2.PIC at their places (O19:0923..09DC, the far table `WONDER_RECTS`); the walls (O19:09DF..0A2D, from 2Dh
  when wonder 7 is the city's); every grid cell in the listing's order (O19:0AFA..0C9A): the roads with the DOS road
  mask (O19:0B0D..0B88: the direction steps `DIR_DX` / `DIR_DY`, the neighbour's linear index 12 column + row,
  unbounded as DOS's) and the stub roads (O19:0BC1..0BEC), the houses of CITYPIX1.PIC (O19:102E), the buildings of
  CITYPIX2 or CITYPIX3.PIC (when the owner knows advance 25h; O19:106C), the wonders of WONDERS or WONDERS2.PIC at their
  heights (O19:10B9..10FA); the coast and its pier (O19:0CAA..0D0C); the 1000h improvement (O19:0D0F..0D64); the name
  and the year in font 6, colour 15 over the colour-0 shadow (O19:0D7A..0DEC), or the pending message's box at (80, 8)
  (O19:0DF1..0E0B), with the core's strings. The cuts are O19:137F's (CITYPIX1's house and road slots with the
  alternative roads when the owner knows advance 3Ah, the building slots 0..22), drawn with colour 0 transparent.
  **The modes** (O19:0E47..0FD4): page 0 black, HILL.PIC's palette (not for -3), page 1 to page 0; -3 (after a
  founding): the HILL.PAL fade-in R:0000:04AA(15) (90 frames, TX); the cycling slot 4 (40h..4Fh, rate 15,
  R:0000:0382, TX); -1 (the city screen's view): the citizens O19:111F(city, 18h, 8Ch, 100h) of POP.PIC (the second row
  when the human knows advance 23h); a value >= 0 (a new wonder or improvement): the first pass without it, 60 frames
  (O19:0F3D), the second with it and G#10 DissolveToScreen(1, 10) (TX; F272); -2 (before an animation): the cycling
  stopped, no wait. **Readiness:** row 88 is ready from the view's entry when an N scene shows below it (the DOS city
  screen, the founding: page 0 keeps what it shows until the view is drawn, as DOS's screen does), else from its first
  draw; row 25 with the view's window. **The end:** the view's window destroyed (GR_DialogClose 11B0:114B after the
  core's wait): page 0 black (O19:0FC7), then R:0238:1BEB (O19:0FCF); to the map the exit kind 1BEB (D7a's trigger
  fades the play palette in from the DAC the view left); back to the DOS city screen (the view entered over it: row
  97 ready below row 88, past the city window's row 25) the fade's end at once, the play palette in the N DAC, no exit
  kind (F275, inferred).
* **The founding scene** (row 109; SCENES-PLAY 7.4; DOS listing O22:0EAF..1143; F271, F273). At the core's first copy
  (11B8:16D7): page 0 black, HILL.PIC on page 1 and its palette, page 1 to page 0, the core's caption centred at (160,
  4) in font 6 colour 0 (O22:0FC9), the strip (0, 120, 320 x 40) saved at the top of page 1 (O22:0FF5); rows 109 and 25

* **The animations** (rows 111..113; SCENES-PLAY 7.3; DOS listing O19:167B, 18C1, 1AE1; F270). Over the view (-2) the

**As implemented (D7f; areas B and C).** Record [ext-D7f-record.md](ext-D7f-record.md) (session E4, 2026-10-17/18;
`verify/BRIEF-d7.md` "Units" D7f, track C, taken over from track A after D7l's landing): with the DOS graphics five

core call): the production list (dialog 140, City > Change or 'c': row 17, also row 86), the buy question ('b': row

binding `(bound_to_r(..) || leader_box(..)) && !pdos_rdlg_owns(..)`; the generated files regenerated after the last
merge); landed alone after D7k (the bases rechecked by `build/d7f-prep/land.py`, `DRY_RUN_OK 26`; the 22 changed and 4
new files with the record copied at 02:30:03 UTC, `build/d7f-prep/landed.sha256`; the three-step cycle ALL_GREEN
03:10:38 UTC, `build/d7f-status.txt`; Release 03:15:33 UTC, user build `build/user-builds/d7f/` with `STACK.SAV`);
compiled and tested headless in Checked (CheckedNoObs and Release privately in the stage); P0 holds (`P0_OK checks
476` unchanged). FIDELITY F281..F287 (F237 noted). No AA bit and no shadow owner taken (`PDOS_OWNER_MAX` 10 unchanged);
the scene bit `PDOS_AS_RLIST` 0x10000000 is the last free AS bit. The Windows graphics are unchanged. Not built (the
deviations): the production advisor boxes (P19 stands), the selection flash (G#3, 20 frames, SCENES-PLAY 7.12) and the
header icon (R:0ADE:0421 sets none). Not in the unit: the sale question (D7g).

* **The module `pdos_rdlg.c/.h`** (area B; P0). One slot per open dialog of the five kinds, found at OBS_DLG_CREATE by
  template (131, 137, 140, 141; the buy question: the plain MenuZ dialog created while ShowTextDialog's call from
  1030:5CCF is pending, return address `PDOS_RL_BUY_RA`), shown at its OBS_WND_SHOW (D6fS-a's cover hides the Windows
  dialog from that instant; the buy box's MenuZ cover is `pdos_reskin`'s), built again whenever the dialog's list,
  edit text, focus or enabled state changes and freed with its window. The controls (the list box, the HELP button,
  the EDIT, OK) are resolved lazily, since the core fills a list after its dialog shows. `pdos_reskin` leaves a MenuZ
  box this module owns (`pdos_rdlg_owns`). The scene rows (12, 15, 17, 18, 87) are set ready when the DOS form is first
  built (`pdos_scene_set_ready`).
* **Placement** (draft B 11.3). Over the N city screen (D7d's frame) at the DOS position; in play on the A canvas at
  2x at the Windows dialog's top-left (`pdos_reskin`'s `a_place_of` through the new `pdos_reskin_a_place`), following
  the frame at every core present (`pdos_rdlg_present`). The menu boxes (production, Find City, buy) are `pdos_reskin`
  R elements (PDOS_RK_BUILT, DOS sizes, as tall as their content); the entry and the stack are own surfaces
  (`own_surface`) whose A rectangles join the zoom layer's z order (`pdos_rdlg_a_rects`, appended by
  `pdos_reskin_a_rects`).
* **Text lines** (D6fSc's rule, `pdos_reskin.h`). A header line made of DOS's strings (the production menu's, the buy
  question's) goes through `pdos_r_spec_dos_lines` with its own line feed: DOS's hand-broken line kept as it is
  (R:1D05:0475 wraps nothing); Find City's prompt is the core's text, folded and wrapped at `PDOS_R_DOS_WRAP` (25)
  columns ("Select city to find:" is 20, one line). The entry's prompt and the stack's rows are drawn by DOS's own
  routines on own surfaces (one line each).
* **The production menu** (row 17; SCENES-PLAY 7.12; R:0ADE:0421; F281..F283). ChooseCityProduction 1170:05CA opens
  dialog 140, sets the header (the city's name + DSTR 35BE) and adds one LB_ADDSTRING per unit, improvement and wonder,
  selects the current production and runs its modal loop; DOS builds DGROUP:222A + the city's name + DGROUP:2242 into
  BA06, each row's name, turns and ", ADM:" form, pages 1 + the entries above 30 for the human player's city without
  status bit 10h (R:0ADE:08DD..0949; "more..." DGROUP:225F / 2268, R:0ADE:1474..1489), takes font 2 above 20 entries
  or paged (R:0ADE:1438..1449), sets DGROUP:2FA0 = 1 (R:0ADE:144E) and calls R:0238:0008(text, 80, 8). The renderer:
  the header in DOS's words with the core's city name; one row per core item with the core's string (decision 15);
  DOS's paging and font rule; the help line. HELP (the dialog's button 103) is Alt+H or the right button (DOS's help
  flag DGROUP:2F9C, O8:062A, then the menu again, R:0ADE:1495..14E0): `PDOS_RDV_HELP` selects the item in the core's
  list, then clicks the HELP button (`PDOS_A_LBHELP`). The advisor boxes (R:0ADE:13CA..1424, PRODUCE.TXT, option bit
  1) are not drawn (P19 stands; the core's counterpart is ShowInstantAdvice before some of its calls, D7g's boxes;
  since D7g's finding (c) these are known to be DOS's O4:02D3 instant advice notes, row 117, so the core has no
  counterpart of the advisor boxes;
  `prod_build` keeps the menu's place and font independent of them). R:0ADE:0421 never sets DGROUP:2F9E (the portrait
  variant, R:1D05:0613), so the DOS production menu has no header icon and none is drawn.
* **The help line** (`pdos_menubox_help_line`, `pdos_ui.c`; named for D7g). R:1D05:05BA..05C1 makes the box two
  pixels taller when DGROUP:2FA0 is set; R:1D05:075B..0796 saves the port's font, selects font 2, draws "(HELP
  AVAILABLE)" (DGROUP:2FBD, the registry's `HELP_AVAILABLE`) at (right - 74, bottom - 4) in colour 10 and restores the
  font. `PdosMenuBox.help` is an input kept by `pdos_menubox_layout`, like D6f's `marks`; `PdosRSpec.help` carries it
  into an R element. D7g's help boxes of dialogs 136 and 145 set the same flag.
* **Find City** (row 12; P16 settled; F284). The core's sorted list (RunListBoxDialog 1098:1909, template 131's
  LISTBOX 103) as a DOS menu box at (64, 8) on N, in play on A at the Windows dialog's top-left at 2x: the core's prompt
  "Select city to find:" (DOS has none: its typed-name box O23:0319 is not shown) as the header, then the cities in
  font 1; as many rows as R:1D05:057D's clamp leaves (at most 30), the window following the bar (`pdos_rd_window`) with
  no "more..." row DOS lacks. The bar starts on row 0 (H2) and is kept while the core fills the list.
* **The text entry** (rows 15 and 89, the founding prompt; SCENES 10.4; F286, F287). O23:0000's box:
  R:0000:0BFA(80, 80, 160, 32, 15), the R:1D05:0A05 outline in colour 11 (O23:002D), "City Name..." (DGROUP:539E, the
  registry's `CITY_NAME_PROMPT`) at (88, 82) in colour 0, the field O23:0414(88, 96, 12) drawn as D5d's name field (8
  x 8 cells, the caret's cell recoloured 15 -> 11 over 10 rows, blinking through `pdos_fx`'s blink), in font 1
  (R:0D12:56AF sets font 1 before O23:0000 for City > Rename; the founding prompt's font inferred the same). The field
  shows the core's EDIT text and caret (`pdos_rd_field_layout`, scrolled when the caret leaves the 12 cells; at most
  18 cells fit in the box). SCENES 10.4 gave the prompt at (82, 88): the listing pushes y 82 (O23:0049), then x 88
  (O23:004D), corrected there. The keys pass to the core's EDIT (I-text, D5d's rows).
* **The buy question** (row 87; R:0D12:4CDF..4E37). The core builds "Cost to complete <name>: $<cost>\rTreasury:
  $<gold>\r" + "_No_Yes" (or "CIVIL DISORDER\r") at 1030:5B63..5CAD and shows it with ShowTextDialog at 1030:5CCF (a
  MenuZ box). The renderer parses the core's text (`pdos_rd_buy_parts`) and draws DOS's box R:0238:0008(text, 100, 80)
  in font 1: DGROUP:266E, the name with ": $" (DGROUP:2680) and the cost, "Treasury: $" (DGROUP:2684) and the gold,
  then " Yes" and " No" (DGROUP:26A3; DOS's row 0 buys: the core's item 1) or DGROUP:2693 (the registry's
  `BUY_TEXTS`). A text of another form keeps the generic box. Since D6fSd the box takes DOS's (100, 80) on an A frame
  through its MenuZ call's place (`pdos_reskin_a_place`, derived; 12.9 "As implemented (D6fSd)"; F289).
* **The unit stack popup** (row 18; SCENES-PLAY 7.19; R:0866:1F69; F285). DrawUnitStackList 11C0:3407(civ,
  firstUnit, selectedRow) is probed at its entry: its arguments name the ring (unit + 0Ah), read through `w16q_read`
  (at most 12 units, R:0866:1FC0). The popup R:1D05:096C(100, 96 - 8n, 120, 16n + 5, 3); per row r (y0 = top + 5 +
  16r) the unit at (106, y0) through the map layer's unit drawing (R:1AEA:0FB3, `pdos_rdlg_set_unit` from
  `pmap_dos.c`: the shape D4CE[64 + 32 civ + type] with its marks), the type's DOS name (`UNIT_RECORDS`, DGROUP:112A +
  22h type) and " (V)" (DGROUP:2224, `STACK_VETERAN`) for a veteran at (128, y0) in colour 15, the home city
  (DGROUP:2913 "NONE" for none) at (128, y0 + 8) in colour 14. No highlight: R:0866:20EE draws the bar only when
  DGROUP:1A3C (the mouse flag) is 0, and civ64 always has a pointer (ruling H2).
* **The adapter rows** (area C; C.12.8 I-menu, I-stack; P26). While one of these elements is the topmost on the
  frame's canvas, the scene is `PDOS_AS_RLIST` (0x10000000) with the dialog as `r_hwnd`, READY when the dialog is shown,
  enabled and active (the buy box: its MenuZ in its input loop). One decision `PDOS_DEC_RLIST` asks `pdos_rdlg_key`,
  `_press` and `_choose` (hooks for the tests: `rd_decide`, `rd_hit`, `rd_view_row`, `rd_lb_pick`, `rd_point`). Six
  rows after I-city: I-menu/list-mod (Ctrl, Shift, Alt, a lock key: consumed), I-menu/list-key (R:1D05:01DB..03FD:
  Up/Down move the bar without wrapping, a letter to the next matching row, Return/Space choose, Alt+H with the help
  flag, Escape -1, "more..." switches the page; the stack: any key is OK, R:0866:2104..210D), I-menu/list-press (a menu
  box: the bar follows the held button, the release decides; the stack: the left button on row r clicks the core's row
  r, y 5 + 33r + 16, with one unit then Return once the core has woken it, STACK_WOKEN; another button: Return),
  I-menu/list-release (the row chosen, or -1: the dialog's cancel, the buy question's "No"), I-menu/list-char
  (consumed) and I-menu/list-other (anything while the dialog is not active: consumed). A chosen list item becomes
  `PDOS_A_LBPICK`: `pdos_rd_lb_pick` makes item k the core's list selection from the list box's live state (a click on
  k when shown; else Home/End when that shows k, Page Down/Up as `user_listbox.c` moves them, a click on k once shown; a
  click on a shown row first when the list lacks the focus) as `PdosRdStep` steps, whose Windows events
  `pdos_adapt.c`'s resolve makes (the filter stage alone makes events: I1, obs_lint), released one act per sync, then
  Return at `PDOS_PR_LB_SEL` (k selected) or `PDOS_PR_LB_DONE` (the predicted state reached). Help: the same pick, then
  a click on the HELP button (`PDOS_A_LBHELP`). The buy question: a click on its MenuZ item, Return at the release
  (D6fS's MZ path). -1 is FCE's Escape (the dialog's own cancel). ADAPT_OK rows 147 -> 153 steps 27 (DOS-PROFILE 1.7.2
  regenerated).

* **The place** (`pdos_reskin.c`): `AT_STATUS` replaces D7g2's `DOS_KEEP` for the recommendation
  (`PDOS_R_PAIR_ADVICE`). `at_status` reads the Status window (g_statusWindowPort 12B0:0034, the port `wl.c` reads;
  shown, not iconic): its window rectangle, or in windowless play its client rectangle (`pdos_reskin_set_windowless`,
  which `pdos_profile.c` sets from `present_windowless_showing` at each present, since present/dos may not include
  `wl.h`). The place's x is its left, its y its bottom (`PdosRPlace.bottom`: `pdos_r_place_xy` puts the box's bottom
  there); the box's width `PdosRSpec.fix_w` is the width / 2 DOS pixels (the A element is 2x). The canvas clamp of
  11.3 still applies.

  frame leaves (the extent less the widest line: 11 DOS pixels for the plain box), then the box's right side is moved
  out so its extent is exactly `fix_w`. Font 2, colours, frame and texture unchanged; no header.

### 12.8 DOS clock, timelines, sequencing, adapters, I4 and I5 (D1 area C; phases D4..D7)

Design of extension 5.5 T1..T7, 5.6 I1 and I3..I6, I8, 7.7, 7.8 and section 8's "Birth inside world
generation", "RNG-driven presentation" and "Inverting the logical scale". Detailed design:
[verify/ext-d1-draft-C.md](verify/ext-d1-draft-C.md) (Revision 3).

* **Files** (`present/dos/`): `pdos_clock` (the clock model, pure), `pdos_tl` (the timeline engine),
  `pdos_intro.c` (intro, credits, logo, title box), `pdos_birth.c` (Birth and the post-Birth
  transition), `pdos_fx.c` (TX effects of other scenes), `pdos_seq` (the flow state machine),
  `pdos_adapt` and `pdos_adapt_tab.c` (the filter stage and adapter rows), `pdos_pend` (I4),
  `pdos_mouse` (I5); `tests/test_timeline.c`, additions to `test_input.c`,
  `tests/harness/ext_timeline.py`, DOS-form scripts for `ext_adapt.py`, `gen/dos/adapters.py`
  (DOS-PROFILE.md 1.7 version 2 generated from the table).
* **The ideal DOS machine** (rules M1..M12, C.4.1). One VGA frame is 17,024 PIT clocks (about 70.086
  Hz; P1); each timeline has its own grid from its start event; DOS code takes no time; retrace waits,
  palette loads, the key flush and the dissolve's BIOS wait take none; the first fade of a session
  spends one extra frame; fades write the whole palette every frame (`E` = 256, P7); cycling steps at
  `a + rate·i`; NSOUND's sound tick on six of seven frames with the divider reset at every `S#1` (P2,
  P29); `wait(n)` returns at frame `a + n`; the dissolve copies `max(1, floor(8,800/c))` pixels per frame.
  Frame k of a timeline is at `t0 + ceil(k · 17,024 · 10^6 / 1,193,182)` µs.
* **Timelines** (`pdos_tl`). Each DOS sequence is a C transcription of the listing's control flow at
  the level of its waits, driven by observer events and probes, the virtual time (`w16q_now_us`) and
  view actions from the filter. Before an event is applied the timeline runs to the event's time, so
  the output does not depend on how ticks partition time. Programs draw through B's primitives on
  `pdos_mn`; every step is logged to `timeline.log`. Data: code immediates as cited constants, the
  registry rows `BIRTH_SCHEDULE`, `BIRTH_CYCLE`, `TITLE_MENU`, and `pdos_text_line(PDOS_F_CREDITS_TXT
  / PDOS_F_STORY_TXT, i, &len)`.
* **Scenes.** The intro (row 1) starts at CreateSequencePlayer `ra` 11E0:00A5, runs about 68 s without
  a key (frames 54..4,766), and its skips follow the DOS listing per phase; N, L, E, C count when read
  during the pan (C.20-4). The title box (T4) shows when the DOS intro ends first; after a choice the
  omitted DOS waits are held by the box. Birth (row 9) starts at SeekSequence `ra` 11E0:01AA, follows
  the DOS schedule and cycle tables, ticks inside world generation at the generator's polls, and with
  the world done continues to the post-Birth fade and the first setup page (C.20-3). D5..D7 scenes get
  their TC/TX/TS timing in C.9.
* **Sequencing** (`pdos_seq`). A listener on B's scene stack plus the flow state (MenuZ readiness,
  title choice, world flags, setup step) follows the core through 19 probe rows (C.11.2), including
  RV2-2's caller rows for CustomizeWorldDialog (`ra` 11E0:0826) and RunNewGameSetup (`ra` 11E0:020A).
  A DOS sequence the core has left is abandoned (the intro's dissolve finishes); an unexpected event
  abandons it with a diagnostic. The pointer is hidden while a DOS intro or Birth timeline is live.
* **The filter stage for the DOS profile** (`pdos_adapt`). Frames 1 and 2 are decided by scene and
  adapter row: pass, consume, translate 1:1, translate into complete pairs or an I4 queue, or drag in a
  menu box (the model shared with B's `pdos_menubox_row`). A press decides its whole action (repeats,
  release, moves while held), whatever frame each part arrives with: `pdos_adapt_note` cancels pending
  translations on user actions of frames 0, 3, 4 and returns 1 for a later part of an action C decided,
  which A then routes to `pdos_adapt_filter` (C-11). Every posted press gets a posted release.
* **Pending translations** (`pdos_pend`, I4): at most one, of at most six steps of at most eight events,
  each released at the first input-taking sync after its expected core state; cancelled by another
  user action on any frame, by state loss, or after 10,000,000 µs of virtual time in one step (P22) (as
  built in D4c2: `pdos_pend`; deviations in its record: symbolic steps, one step per sync including the
  start's sync, the D5..D7 predicates refused, overflow at the release).
* **Mouse conversion** (I5, proved per class, C.14): PollMouseClick (signed `*10/16`, stores
  1108:017A/018F), WaitForKeyOrClick and CityScreenWndProc (unsigned `*10 >> 4`): d = ceil(16L/10);
  forward ScaleLogical rectangles: d = floor(1.6L); control rectangles (MenuZ's `RanDoMRadio` items
  created at 1220:09B0, Region controls, buttons, EDIT, list boxes, dialog 141 rows `(y − 5)/33`): the
  centre of the largest rectangle of the control's visible region; position-irrelevant waits: any point.
* **Prompt rules met.** T1 (starts at the core's probes), T2 (both profiles advance; switches pick the
  same instant), T3 (closed-form waits; the filter never waits), T4 (title box, setup page, holds),
  T5 (skips both ways; a left click ends the DOS intro, C.20-2), T6 (virtual time; frames only at
  ticks), T7 (core timing in play), I1 (only user-created translations), I3..I6, I8, and P0 (the
  filter is off the P0 input path; no generator; `g_randSeed` never read). **One exception by the user's
  direction (2026-10-15, D4b4):** the Birth auto-continue is the only time-driven input, an exception to I1
  and T3: the DOS timeline's Birth hand-over decides one Escape press and release for the core. It replaces
  T4's hold after DOS Birth while the DOS profile is visible; with the Windows profile visible the T4 hold
  applies as before. P0 for it is checked against the recorded input (12.11).
* **Choices** (C.18, one line each): the clock M1..M12; one grid per timeline; an event-sourced engine
  with closed-form waits; two sources for a DOS key; abandon on the core's next scene; T4 next screens
  (title box, setup page); "BUILDING NEW WORLD..." only while generation runs; omitted DOS waits after
  a menu choice; the title box drawn again over the current DOS screen on a DOS-side return, on the title
  picture on a Windows-side return (D5P); title hotkeys from the DOS text (no Q since D5P); filter decisions at the sync's time; the pairing rule; lone Ctrl,
  Shift, Alt and lock keys are no keys in DOS sequences with the DOS profile visible (the user's
  direction on D4-3, superseding C.20-7; D4cF); letters before clicks for MenuZ; one pending translation;
  10 s timeout; the control-rectangle centre point; P17, P22, P25, P26 as settled; the pointer scene
  rule; setup steps by item count and Go Back state; input during DOS-only transitions consumed where
  DOS would flush it; the note on frames 0, 3, 4 with A's routing.
* **Tests and gates.** `test_timeline.c` (clock inverses, the intro frames without and with keys, 200
  random tick partitions giving identical logs and machines, Birth, abandon, no generator use, T2);
  `test_input.c` additions (pairing across frames, I4 release, cancellation and timeout, I5 for every
  L, class C on a fixture dialog, adapter rows); `ext_timeline.py` (7.7: frames against the listing's
  values, switches, a host-input key, a CPU-bound gap); `ext_adapt.py` DOS-form against Windows-form
  pairs with equal `coreinput.log` and checkpoints (7.8, `sync N` host-form lines); obs_lint's area-C
  rules; `adapters.py check`. Gates: D4 7.7 and 7.8 for intro, title, Birth; D5 7.8 and P0 for setup;
  D6 and D7 add their rows.
* **Open** (C.19): C-2 (M3, M4, M10, M11 unobserved; DOS-oracle O1/O2, D4), C-3 (setup step
  discriminators, D5; settled in D5a, "As implemented (D5a; area C)" below), C-4 (dialog 143 Region ids and the help request, D5; settled in D5e, "As implemented (D5e; area C)" below), C-5 (exact O7:17CF
  transcription, D4), C-6 (title-box wrap when the core is not ready, D4), C-7 (I-city keys and
  regions, D7), C-8 (class S scenes, D7), C-9 (model goldens recorded at D4). C-1, C-10 and C-11 are
  closed.

**As implemented (D4b).** Four units, each with its record (2026-10-12): D4b1, the clock and the timeline
engine ([ext-D4b1-record.md](ext-D4b1-record.md)); D4b2a, the intro program

(`manual-checklist-ext.md` D4-4..D4-13).

* **Clock and engine** (D4b1). `pdos_clock`: M1..M12 and C.4.3 as pure functions and constants.

  waits `PDOS_W_FADE`, `PDOS_W_KEY`, wakes and tails (record Deviations 1..4, 6); a key at the very
  microsecond of a frame-instant key test is seen by the next test (Deviation 5); `PDOS_DIAG_SEQ` (`seq`)
  counts misuse and runaway programs (Deviation 9).
* **The intro program** (D4b2a). `pdos_intro.h/.c`: O2:01CD..0BD6 as a timeline program with seven resume
  labels named after the listing addresses (`PDOS_INTRO_PC_*`), `PdosIntroVars` in `pdos_tl.h`, the logo
  sprite in a caller-owned `PdosIntroCtx` (Deviation 3), the VGA path only (Deviation 2); only the dissolve
  is a finish tail; the program ends at the hand-over to the title menu R:01A8:0486
  (`PDOS_INTRO_PC_TITLE`). `pdos_rand_calls()` counts the DOS generator's calls for tests.
* **The sequencer** (D4b2b1). `pdos_seq.h/.c`: the flow state of C.11.1 from the probes of C.11.2
  (call-site rows resolved at reset; an unresolved one counted, never matched); the intro timeline on
  `pdos_mn()` started at CreateSequencePlayer ra 11E0:00A5 (t0); the core's intro skip (SetGameCursor ra
  11E0:08D7) as the DOS key; `pdos_seq_view_key` as source 2's entry point for D4c; abandon with finish at
  the core's title MenuZ exit (ra 11E0:0729) and at Birth's SeekSequence (ra 11E0:01AA); C.11.6 with
  `PDOS_DIAG_SEQ`. Wired into `pdos_profile.c` at draft B 2.1's places (reset; event step 2; entry step 2;
  exit steps 1 and 7; advance step 1, at every tick, visible or not). While the intro view is on, scene
  rows 1, 25 (dialog 148) and 2 are ready N rows, so the N frame is the intro.
* **The title box** (D4b2b2). C.7 in `pdos_seq`, continuing the intro's DOS run (seq 1): shown at the
  hand-over with no preselection (frame 4,766, or 146 frames after a key ends the pan), even while the
  core is still in its intro (T4); the logo on black held with a preselection; the row from the core's
  `g_menuSelection` (12B0:009C) read at MenuZ's key poll (IsKeyPending ra 1220:0AA5), else the view's own
  row; the chosen row kept after a choice; drawn again when the core reopens dialog 139; a title choice
  during the DOS intro (C.6.6) ends it with finish and shows the box with the chosen row. It uses 12.7's
  menu-box model and sends nothing to the core. The pointer rule `pdos_seq_pointer_hidden` (C.11.5) covers
  the intro's timeline. `pdos_seq_set_watch` runs the timelines to exact instants for the C-9 goldens.

below, which also extends the condition "the DOS profile visible", D7).

* **The decision** (`pdos_seq_autocontinue_release(out, max, now_us)`, `PiDispatch.pend_release`'s
  signature). `pdos_seq` decides the Birth auto-continue:
  * when: DOS Birth at its hand-over R:01A8:087C (`pdos_birth_at_setup`), the core in its skip-able Birth
    (scene BIRTH from SeekSequence ra 11E0:01AA, world done at BeginWorldCreation's exit ra 11E0:01B2,
    neither its skip SetGameCursor ra 11E0:094E nor its Birth end DestroySequencePlayer ra 11E0:01E8
    seen), the DOS profile visible (`pdos_profile_visible()` in `pdos_profile.c`, because the include rule
    of `gen/dos/registry.py` keeps `profile.h` and `input.h` out of `pdos_seq.c`), no press of the player's
    since Birth's start (`pdos_seq_note_press`, `pdos_seq_presses`), room for both events;
  * what: an Escape press and release (`HOST_EV_KEYDOWN`/`KEYUP` vk 1Bh, scan 01h, frame 0, not
    adapted), once per Birth, released at the next input-taking sync (46 µs after the hand-over in
    p4-birth); the core's skip poll (every 30 movie frames) takes it: 13 ms in p4-birth, 431 ms in
    `to_dos`;
  * on in civ64 (`civ64_present_defaults()` in `civ64_start.c`, called after `obs_init`); off in the
    harness unless `--birth-continue 1` (`pdos_seq_set_autocontinue`; kept across `pdos_seq_reset`);
  * the visible profile changes only at ticks: a switch to DOS after the hand-over produces the skip at the
    first input-taking sync after the switch's tick;
  * the exception to I1 and T3, replacing T4's hold after DOS Birth while the DOS profile is visible.
* **Why Escape** (core listing). PumpMessagesCheckSkipInput 11E0:095F removes every queued message, so one
  poll removes the WM_KEYDOWN, its WM_CHAR and the WM_KEYUP; no other reader of the queue during Birth on
  the normal path; the Birth dialog's DlgProc 1098:16E2 handles no key message and acts on WM_CHAR 0Dh
  only; if the movie ends first, the setup's first MenuZ flushes the events (FlushInputEvents 11F0:4EA0
  at 1220:0248). Record, "Proof that the skip consumes it and nothing reaches the setup".
* **Records.** `pdos_seq_autocontinue_count`, the presentation diagnostic `autocontinue`
  (`PDOS_DIAG_AUTOCONTINUE`), the hook `pdos_seq_set_autocontinue_hook` (coreinput.log's `autocontinue`
  line, RUNNING.md).
* **Tests.** `test_pdos_seq` 114 checks (+17), `w32_test_host_win32` 260 (+2; 246 CheckedNoObs),
  `tests/harness/ext_continue.py` (8 runs of p4-birth's start, 20 checks on Checked: `dos`, `replay`,
  `user`, `win`, `to_win`, `off`, `to_dos`; the refusal on CheckedNoObs). Every existing P0 variation,
  ext_g1, ext_timeline's Birth runs, the P4 scenarios and gate3b run with the feature off (harness
  default), their expectations unchanged.
* **Notes for D4c2.** `pdos_pend_release` must call `pdos_seq_autocontinue_release(out + n, max - n,
  now_us)` once per call or move the decision into its own queue; the two events stay in one sync, press
  then release, frame 0; D4c's T4 choice translation must not add a second skip (record, "Notes for
  D4c2").

**Added in D4c1 (area C).** Record [ext-D4c1-record.md](ext-D4c1-record.md) (2026-10-15): `pdos_adapt`
(the filter stage: pairing tables, DOS-form action bits, the first matching row of `pdos_adapt_tab.c`

queue interface `PdosPendOps` for D4c2, diagnostics adapt, pend, pair), `pdos_adapt_tab.c` (38 rows
since D4cF, 36 before, with I-intro/modifier and I-birth/modifier: I-intro, I-title, I-birth, I-none;
DOS-PROFILE.md 1.7.2 generated by `gen/dos/adapters.py`, ADAPT_OK rows 38 steps 12 in test.cmd) and `pdos_mouse` (I5 classes X, C, P, W, S; class C less the windows above the control). The
title box's hotkeys select only; Up/Down do not wrap; frame 2 passes; a translation needing a queued step
is consumed whole until D4c2. Tests: `test_input` (synthetic scenes), `test_pdos_adapt` (the real

**Added in D4c2 (area C).** Record [ext-D4c2-record.md](ext-D4c2-record.md) (2026-10-15): `pdos_pend` (I4,
C.13): one translation at a time as D4c1's symbolic steps, refused at its start beyond 6 steps or 4 acts a
step, with a produced act other than a key or click, or with a predicate the state source cannot tell
(DLG_OPEN, EDIT_FOCUS, CURSOR, STACK_WOKEN until D5..D7); released one step per input-taking sync (none at the
start's own sync) when its predicate holds on the sequencer's core state (MZ_READY, MZ_SEL), resolved by
`pdos_adapt_resolve` at that instant; cancelled by another user action, a lost state (the title's MenuZ
outside BOOT/INTRO/TITLE, the setup's outside BIRTH/SETUP, MZ_SEL after its MenuZ exits), 10,000,000 µs of
virtual time in one step (P22) or more than 8 events, never producing input; diagnostic `pend_cancel`.
`pdos_pend_release` is `PiDispatch.pend_release`'s default and calls D4b4's auto-continue. Tests:
`test_pdos_pend` (102 checks, 104 since D4cF: every predicate, the lost states, the limit at its boundary, cancellation on
frames 0..4, I1, C.13.6 examples 1..6), `test_input`'s default-release block, `ext_adapt.py`'s 13
DOS-form/Windows-form pairs (C.15.4, equal `coreinput.log`, frames, checkpoints and events) and
`ext_timeline.py`'s host-input run `hostkey` (T5, T4, I4 on the model); the harness's `adapt.log` in 7.8 runs
with the DOS profile registered. Compiled and headless in Checked, CheckedNoObs and Release at the D4 gate
(eight-step cycle ALL_GREEN, `build/d4c2-status.txt`); no DOS program executed; Asan not run.

**Changed in D4cF (areas A and C).** Records [ext-D4c1-record.md](ext-D4c1-record.md) and

a capture loss's release is no release on the box (R5); the repeats of a row's own press are consumed and a

**Added in BS (area C).** Records [ext-BS1-record.md](ext-BS1-record.md), [ext-BS2-record.md](ext-BS2-record.md),
[ext-BS3-record.md](ext-BS3-record.md) and [ext-BS4-record.md](ext-BS4-record.md), gate
[ext-BS-acceptance.md](ext-BS-acceptance.md) (2026-10-15/16): the Windows & DOS Birth synchronisation, by the
user's direction of 2026-10-15 about 14:03 UTC ("adapt Windows scenes to DOS counterparts in terms of timing").
BS1..BS4 landed together at 23:35 UTC; compiled and headless in Checked, CheckedNoObs and Release (the BS phase
gate's eight-step cycle ALL_GREEN, `build/bs-status.txt`, 2026-10-16 00:10:04 UTC); no DOS program was executed;
Asan deferred (user direction). The DOS Birth's instants are **DOS listing (model)**, the Windows pictures,
captions and script **Windows data**. `build.cmd` compiles `present\bs\*.c` (281 portable sources). The user's
checks BS-1..BS-6 are pending (`manual-checklist-ext.md` BS section).

* **BS1, the Windows Birth's store** (`present/bs/pbs_assets`). `host_data_read` (12.10) reads `CivData0..4.rsc`
  before the emulation; the store keeps, by the core's precedence, 'CvPc' 128..135 (decoded with colour base 0)
  and 'STR#' 139, all or nothing (status `ok`, `none`, `read`, `format`, `missing`, `decode`). The .rsc parser
  (OpenResFile 1190:0000, AddRFileFromImage 1190:1692, RestoMem 1190:0FDA), the STR# reader (GetIndString
  1100:078A) and the CvPc decoder (LoadGifPicture 10B8:11BC, PicDecompress 10B8:158C with its own effects H11
  and C15; the colour table 10B8:12E8..141C) refuse, never fault. `present/bs/pbs_check`'s asset part (harness
  `--bs-check 1`) compares each picture read, port bitmap and movie caption with the store through `w16q_*`.
  Tests `test_pbs_assets` (58), `ext_bs.py assets`.
* **BS2, the replica and its shadow mirror** (`present/bs/pbs_movie`, `pbs_check`'s mirror part). The replica
  runs the movie's 78 items over the core's inputs, forward only, frame by frame and item by item as
  AdvanceMovie's catch-up does (2 LoadClut, 3 and 4 the fades, 5 SetFont, 6 LoadPicture, 7 text, 9
  SwapAndShow; the 256-colour path only, 3.5); positions are (frame, item, phase), phase 1 lying between an
  item's palette op and the rest of its work (w16's forced present). The mirror (harness `--bs-mirror 1`)
  starts the replica at SeekSequence ra 11E0:01AA, attributes each core present by existing probes (D10: no new
  probe), compares the visible client, the system palette and the RGB composition at every present and ends at
  DestroySequencePlayer. Tests `test_pbs_movie` (44), `ext_bs.py mirror` (p4-birth 1436 of 1436 presents equal,
  EARTH 84 of 84).
* **BS3, the marks, the warp and the Windows view.** `present/dos/pdos_bsync.h`: DOS Birth's sync marks (PRE,
  FADE_OUT(n), FADE_IN(k), PIC(k), TEXT(j), CLEAR(j), BUILDING, POST_FADE, SETUP), set by `pdos_birth.c` at the
  program's own points (`PdosBirthVars` `bs_phase`, `bs_k`, `bs_start`, `bs_len`) and read by `pdos_bsync(&s)`
  (NONE, ABANDONED, or the mark with the timeline's frame and t0); no mark changes a DOS frame, a step, a clock
  or a draw. `present/bs/pbs_core`: the replica's start, shared by the mirror and the view. `present/bs/pbs_view`:
  the table (`pbs_warp_table`: 8 SwapAndShow frames, 8 FadeOuts, 44 texts, every fade reaching its full level;
  any failure turns BS off for that Birth with a log line), W (`pbs_warp_frame`, BRIEF-bs.md 3.6; f never
  decreases), the view's states (started at the core's Birth start with the setting on and the store open; shown
  at its first composed frame; held at the core's skip SetGameCursor ra 11E0:094E, D8; handed back at the tick
  after the core's Birth end DestroySequencePlayer ra 11E0:01E8, at once on a replica error), the composition
  (the live screen's indices with the movie window's visible client replaced by the replica's, through the live
  system palette with the god entries 0..223, and 224..225 once f reached TS[1], from the replica) and its
  damage tracking. `g_win` is never touched (D9; 12.11). **The landing's early-out** (note 4): `compose`
  returns at once when none of its inputs changed since the last composition (no core present, whose damage
  covers any change of the movie window's place or cover; the same replica frame; the same system palette and
  screen size), which cut the view's cost on p4-birth from +22 % to +7 % process CPU time (1,287 full
  compositions of 3,504,824 calls; the frames unchanged); `compose` also clears `frame_ready` when it frees the
  frame. `present.c` step 2b (after the profiles advanced: `pbs_view_advance`, then a whole-client redraw when
  the view became shown or stopped being shown and the Windows profile is visible), the view's probe listener,
  `pbs_view_core_damage` at every core present, `pbs_view_reset` with the run's reset; `present_birth_sync(on,
  &reason)` (the setting; with on, the store opened when the DOS profile is registered) and
  `present_birth_sync_active()` (D7's query). `profile_win.c`: `shows_core_frame` is 0 while the view is shown,
  `frame`/`peek` return the view's frame. On in civ64 (`civ64_present_defaults`, D6; without the DOS files the
  Windows Birth plays natively), off in the harness unless `--birth-sync 1`. The include rule D11
  (`gen/dos/registry.py` `check_bs_includes`): a `present/bs` file includes from `present/dos` only
  `"dos/pdos_bsync.h"`. Tests `test_pbs_view` (45), `ext_timeline.py --group bs`, `ext_p0.py` V8 and V8s.
* **BS4, the Windows end (D7).** In `pdos_seq_autocontinue_release` the condition "the DOS profile is visible"
  becomes "the DOS profile is visible or the Birth synchronisation follows this Birth" (`pdos_profile_bs_active()`
  in `pdos_profile.c`, beside `pdos_profile_visible`, through `present_birth_sync_active()`); every other
  condition of D4b4, the events and their proof are unchanged; `pdos_seq_set_bs_query(fn)` for the tests (a
  separate setter, not a third `PdosSeqQ` field). The P0 exception is 12.11's. Tests `test_pdos_seq` 125 checks
  (+11), `ext_continue.py` 31 checks (`bs_win`, `bs_win_replay`, `nodos`).

**As implemented (D5a; area C).** Record [ext-D5a-record.md](ext-D5a-record.md): C.11.2's rows that D4 left
(Deviation 3 of D4b2b1), C.11.3's title branches, C.11.4's setup steps (Open C-3 settled) and C.11.6's new cases
in `pdos_seq.c/.h`. Landed with UX1b, D5b and D5c (12.7, "As implemented (D5a; area B)"); no recorded expectation
changed.

* **New call-site rows** (in `OBS_CALLSITES`, resolved at the reset): GR_DialogOpen exits at ra 1108:0290 (dialog
  143), 10E8:0067 (144), 10E8:10BE (151); RunTextInputDialog 1220:16FD at ra 10E8:056F, entry and exit (its
  `OBS_AT_EXIT` marker, checked by `d3a_obs_lint`). **New `PdosCore` fields** (the block "extension D5a", read
  only, for D5b..D5f): `dlg_custom` (143), `dlg_setup` (144), `dlg_intro_text` (151) and the corrected
  `movie_wnd` (148), each the window of the dialog created inside that GR_DialogOpen call (`OBS_DLG_CREATE` with
  the expected template since the call's entry) and forgotten at its `OBS_WND_DESTROY`; GR_DialogOpen returns
  only its status in AL (1098:1604), so D4b2b1's `movie_wnd = AX` was the status (record Deviation 1); the MenuZ
  children `mz_dlg` (the parent of its first static 400 or radio 500..520), `mz_items` and `mz_goback` (control
  102 from its creation style and `OBS_WND_ENABLE`, read at the MenuZ's first key poll: 1, 0 or -1 unknown);
  `mz_sel` stays g_menuSelection read at the key poll (the core emits no `OBS_CTL_CHECK`, Deviation 2); the EDIT
  control 450 (`edit_wnd`, `edit_focus`, `edit_events`, `edit_t_us`: C.9's text-entry cursor restarts its phase
  at each); `setup_mz_result` / `setup_mz_step`, `text_active` / `text_result` (1 OK, -1 Go Back, 1220:1928) and
  `branch_result`.
* **C.11.3, the title's branches.** LoadGameFromFileDialog's exit (ra 11E0:0807) returns to TITLE only when
  cancelled (AX -1), CustomizeWorldDialog's (ra 11E0:0826) only when not confirmed (AX != 0), ShowHallOfFame's
  (ra 11E0:0847) always; otherwise the scene stays LOAD or CUSTOM until the core goes on (play; the outro and
  Birth). D4b2b1 set TITLE at every branch exit, which a chosen game or a confirmed world contradicted.
* **C.11.4, the setup steps (Open C-3 settled against RunNewGameSetup 10E8:0000).** At a setup MenuZ's first key
  poll (IsKeyPending ra 1220:0AA5; its radios and Go Back exist by then): more than five radios is step 2 (the
  tribe list), otherwise Go Back disabled is step 0 and enabled step 1 (an unknown Go Back keeps the step and
  counts `seq`); at RunTextInputDialog's entry step 3 when the last setup dialog to return was the step-2 MenuZ
  with result 0 ("Custom."), else step 4. The step comes from the dialog's own controls, so it survives Go Back in
  any order; counting the setup dialogs, C.11.4's alternative, is not needed. In the private harness runs the
  setup MenuZ's dialog is dialog 144 itself, with five radios (step 0, then step 1), fifteen (step 2) and
  RunTextInputDialog as step 4; no recorded scenario takes "Custom." or Go Back (unit tests only).
* **C.11.6 for the new states:** a setup MenuZ, RunTextInputDialog or the exit of dialog 144 or 151 outside
  SETUP, dialog 143's exit outside CUSTOM and a title branch's entry outside TITLE abandon the running DOS
  sequence (`seq`); a missing or foreign dialog at a dialog call site only counts `seq` and leaves the window 0
  (Deviation 3: an abandon would end a running intro or Birth for a bookkeeping gap). Tests: `test_pdos_seq` 170
  checks (`test_d5a_setup`, `test_d5a_branches`, `test_d5a_unexpected`).

**As implemented (D5b; area C).** Record [ext-D5b-record.md](ext-D5b-record.md): the input side of the re-skinned
menu box (C.12.5, C.14.2, C.14.3; `pdos_adapt.c/.h`, `pdos_adapt_tab.c`); 12.7 has the boxes. Scene
`PDOS_AS_RMENU`: `def_scene` takes it when the topmost R element is a core MenuZ's box (`pdos_reskin_menu`),
before the title states; core READY while that MenuZ is in its input loop for that dialog with a selection, else
WAIT; the adapter acts on frame-1 (N canvas) events only. Two hooks appended to `PdosAdaptHooks`, `r_hit` and
`r_view_row` (defaults `pdos_r_hit`, `pdos_reskin_view_row`), so press, move, release, the R2 key repeat and the
STEP / ROW / HIGHLIGHT view actions serve both the box and the title box. **Eleven I-menu rows** (the table 38 ->
49 rows; DOS-PROFILE.md 1.7.2 regenerated): Alt keys, the right button and characters consumed (Help of dialog 145,
command 109 and Alt+V are D6's); the edges consumed (no wrap); Up / Down, Return / Escape passed; Space as Return;
a hotkey as the item's letter (select only); other keys consumed; the press drags the box's own highlight (view
only); a release on an enabled row with a core item clicks that item's control and then Return once the core's
selection is it; a release outside, on a disabled row, on the Go Back / Cancel row (no core item; no such row
since D5f) or while MenuZ is not in its loop is consumed; Escape reaches the core (the dialog's cancel). A message box has no adapter scene
(record decision 7; its input follows the scene below). Tests: `test_input` (the 11 rows, `adapt_rmenu`; 250
checks with D5b, 293 since D5c's vectors).

**As implemented (D5c; area C).** Record [ext-D5c-record.md](ext-D5c-record.md): the sequencer's hand-over to the
setup view and the adapter I-setup (C.8.7, C.12.7, C.13.6 example 4; FIDELITY F162); 12.7 has the view.

* **The sequencer** (`pdos_seq.c/.h`). T4 (C.8.7): DOS Birth reaches its setup step while the core still plays its
  Birth; `setup_check` (before the Birth view's end in `run_to`, and at the end of `on_event` / `on_probe`) hands
  over once, at the frame of Birth's or the post-Birth timeline's setup step: the difficulty page, row 0, from
  that instant; the box's own row moves with the user's keys and drag until the core's MenuZ is ready and takes
  over. Each setup MenuZ exit (ra 10E8:0528, the new `PdosCore.setup_mz_exits`) with a choice gives that result
  (Go Back or Escape -1: none; "Custom.": none); the tribe page's civilization count follows the core's item count
  ((items - 3) / 2). The sequencer's scene rows (intro, movie, title, Birth, new game, EARTH, setup) are kept
  ready while the setup view shows (`rows_apply`); `pdos_setup_abandon` at `unexpected()`, Birth's start and the
  reset; `pdos_seq_setup_fade()` (the post-Birth fade before the setup) and `pdos_seq_setup_view_row()` for the
  adapters. `pdos_profile.c`: `pdos_setup_reset()` after `pdos_shadow_reset()`, `pdos_setup_on_event()` between
  the sequencer's and the shadow engine's on_event.
* **I-setup** (`pdos_adapt.c/.h`, `pdos_adapt_tab.c`, `gen/dos/adapters.py`): scenes PDOS_AS_SETUP0/1/2 (the page
  with its box and row map), PDOS_AS_SRESULT and PDOS_AS_SFADE; actions PDOS_AA_GOBACK, PDOS_AA_PORTRAIT,
  PDOS_AA_LREL_DIS and PDOS_A_BUTTON (a click on the setup dialog's button 102); `PdosAdaptSc.rmap/nrmap/ncore`;
  hooks `setup_view_row` and `goback` appended to `PdosAdaptHooks`. 26 rows (75 in all; DOS-PROFILE.md 1.7.2
  regenerated; `adapters.py check` ADAPT_OK rows 75 steps 19): fade, result, alt-right, edge, up-down (core ready
  / T4), goback, return, space, escape, hotkey, choose, key, press, release-disabled, release (core ready / T4 /
  core wait), portrait (x < 135: clamp((y - 12) / 35, 0, 4)), outside, release-other. Row k of the tribe page is
  the core's item k + 1, the Custom. row item 0. Up / Down pass only where the core's wrapping move lands on the
  item of the box's next row (else an edge, consumed); the Go Back row is reached by the mouse only *(since D5cR
  no Go Back or Custom. row: `I-setup/goback` removed, the bar-move and bar-choose rows, "As implemented (D5f; area
  C)", below)*; the drag
  highlight and the T4 row move change the box's own row only while the core's MenuZ is not ready; the fade
  consumes all input (also in T4); keys on the competition and tribe pages while the core's MenuZ is not ready
  are consumed.
* **Tests and the landing.** `test_input` 293 checks (26 I-setup vectors on the synthetic box, `adapt_setup`, the
  row count 75), `test_pdos_seq` (the three post-Birth checks expect the setup page), `ext_timeline.py` (`birth`
  check 3 and `birth_skip`: the step `setup_page` at the hand-over and the N frame after the core's setup; the
  golden `bgolden frame 12000` a74dd7dc... (black) -> d67d465f... (the T4 difficulty page); since D5cR
  dd778d0b..., the page without the Go Back row), `ext_adapt.py`
  C.15.4 case 9 (four DOS-form pairs d-setup-escape, d-setup-portrait, d-setup-tribe, d-setup-goback;
  EXT_ADAPT_OK checks 56 -> 68). The landing's one failure was BS4's `ext_continue.py` check 1, which expected
  the A frame after Birth's hand-over: it now requires timeline.log's `setup_page` step at the hand-over, the
  first DOS show within one second of it and every DOS show from then on the 320x200 N frame
  ([ext-D5c-record.md](ext-D5c-record.md) "Landing"). `presentation-diag.log`'s `pdos unexplained` fell where the setup runs (birth 680 -> 670, new and s3
  675 -> 615, earth 602 -> 541, custom 640 -> 580, settlers 670 -> 610): the core's dialog-144 drawing is the
  setup owner's.

**As implemented (D5d; area C).** Record [ext-D5d-record.md](ext-D5d-record.md): the text-entry cursor's blink
(C.9), the sequencer's start of dialog 151's view and of its border's effect instance (RV2-18), the adapters
I-text and I-wait (C.12.7, C.14.3) and C.15.1 item 7 restated; 12.7 has the name pages and the screen (FIDELITY
F163, F164). Landed alone in a build slot (12.7, "As implemented (D5d; area B)").

* **The blink** (`pdos_fx.h/.c`, new; C.9). `PdosBlink`: 10 frames on (O23:04DE), 10 off (O23:0514) on its own
  frame grid, restarted with the cursor shown (`pdos_blink_restart`); `pdos_blink_on(t)` = (frames since the
  restart) mod 20 < 10, `pdos_blink_next(t)` the next change. Times are given; no clock is read. The name page
  restarts it at its first draw and at each OBS_FOCUS / OBS_EDIT event of the EDIT (its event time, C.9; D5a's
  `edit_t_us`) and evaluates it at every sequencer step (advance included), so it never depends on visibility;
  each change shows at the first step at or after its frame edge (a port choice, F163).
* **The sequencer and RV2-18.** `pdos_fx_start("border")` runs only in `pdos_init_sync` at a core point, which
  `pdos_setup_sync` calls first at every sequencer step (on_event, on_probe, advance), whether the DOS profile is
  shown or not; frame(), peek() and a profile switch draw nothing and start nothing. Dialog 151's view starts at
  the GR_DialogOpen exit step (one "border" instance, its generator calls) and ends at the window's destruction; a
  second dialog 151 is instance 1; after the profile's reset instance 0 again, with the same pixels.
* **I-text and I-wait** (`pdos_adapt.c/.h`, `pdos_adapt_tab.c`, `gen/dos/adapters.py`; C.12.7, C.14.3). Scene bits
  PDOS_AS_TEXT (a name page: core READY while the core's text page runs at the page's step with its EDIT focused,
  else WAIT) and PDOS_AS_WAIT (dialog 151's view, its window `x_hwnd` for class X); action bits PDOS_AA_INS (Ins)
  and PDOS_AA_EDIT (a printable key, Left, Right, Del, Backspace); step actions PDOS_A_SPACE and PDOS_A_LEFT. Eight
  rows (83 in all; DOS-PROFILE.md 1.7.2 regenerated by `adapters.py`; ADAPT_OK rows 83 steps 21):
  I-text/ins/core-ready (Space then Left, at once), I-text/edit/core-ready (passed: the editing keys, Return,
  Escape), I-text/press/core-ready (Return, its release consumed), I-text/other (consumed); I-wait/mod (consumed),
  I-wait/key (passed), I-wait/press (passed in class X at dialog 151's window, else the movie's), I-wait/right
  (consumed). The I-wait press ends the view, so its release meets the A frame and keeps its own point (C.12.2).

  choose the highlighted item, Alt+H requests help (R:1D05:03E3 -> 0386); the right button requests help
  (R:1D05:0180); a left press is a drag whose release in an item's row chooses it, in the Cancel row cancels,
  elsewhere asks again; Escape is consumed (DOS asks the question again, -1). The Cancel row's hotkey (C) never

* **`w_over_n`** (record decision). Dialog 143's close by OK or Cancel adds one: the core's main window is activated
  above the hidden dialog before its destruction, and 6.4's fallback counts it, as it counts the title dialog's
  creation in every run; no A frame is presented there, and the N scene's window is not shown at that moment (shown
  0). A rule that skips the count while the scene window is hidden would remove both; it changes the fallback of
  every scene, so it is left to D5f's counter work (ruling 8: the counters are 0 for the D4 and D5 rows). *Done in
  D5f: a hidden scene window has nothing over it, so both moments count 0 ("As implemented (D5f; area B)").*

**As implemented (D5K and D5K2; area C).** Records [ext-D5K-record.md](ext-D5K-record.md) and
[ext-D5K2-record.md](ext-D5K2-record.md) (2026-10-16; the user's D5-6 report of about 10:10 UTC and the user's "Fix
Windows too" of 11:12, STATUS "User directions"): after a Windows-only Go Back onto the difficulty page MenuZ disables
the focused Go Back (1220:07FC), EnableWindow leaves no focus window (Windows 3.1's rule, `user_wnd.c`), the queue
gives the keys to the active window as system keys, and MenuZ ignores them, in both graphics. D5K landed 2026-10-16
(the three-step cycle ALL_GREEN 11:34:48 UTC, `build/d5k-status.txt`), D5K2 after UX3 (ALL_GREEN 12:35:19 UTC,
`build/d5k2-status.txt`); compiled and headless in Checked (CheckedNoObs and Release privately in the stages). FIDELITY
F183, F184.

* **D5K: the unfocused hook** (`present/dos/pdos_adapt.c/.h`; the rule is in `pdos_adapt.h`'s header).
  `PdosAdaptHooks.unfocused(x, y, off_x)` (the last member; NULL = `def_unfocused`) is 1 when the core's setup
  MenuZ is in its input loop (`PdosCore.mz_active`, `mz_ready`, `mz_kind == PDOS_MZ_SETUP`, `mz_dlg`), `w16q_focus()`
  is 0, and its dialog's OK (65h) is enabled and shown, with OK's I5 class-C point and an x left of OK's window; it
  reads only `w16q_*` and the sequencer (P0, obs_lint). While it answers 1, `key_mouse` (called from `decide` for the
  PASS and MAP decisions of a key press on a READY setup page) gives the key its Windows mouse form: Return passed,
  or Space mapped to Return, is a click on OK; Up or Down passed is a click on the item the core's move selects, then
  a left press on OK, a move off it with the button down and the release there (the BUTTON takes the focus and,
  released outside, notifies nothing), so the first DOS arrow gives the keyboard back; the key's release and repeats
  are consumed (`FD_PAIRS`). In a step (`pdos_adapt_resolve`, also a pending translation's later steps) a RETURN act
  is OK's click and a LETTER act the click on the item it would select. Every adapter row (98 rows, 22 steps), frames
  0 and 3, the core, the sequencer and the setup view are unchanged; with the focus present nothing differs.
* **D5K2: the dialog keeps its focus** (the platform rule: section 5, "Disabling the focus window", and 12.10). Since D5K2 the dialog's
  OK takes the focus when Go Back is disabled, so `unfocused` answers 0 after the Go Back and the DOS keys pass as
  keys; the hook stays as a defence (no harness pair exercises it; `test_input`'s 20 `adapt_unfocused` checks do).
  `ext_adapt.py`'s `d_press_off` and `d_info`'s `"lups"` (used only by D5K's Windows form of `d-goback-keys`) are
  removed.
* **Tests.** `test_input` 417 / 390 / 417 in Checked / `checked noobs` / Release (397 / 370 / 397 before D5K;
  `adapt_unfocused`); `test_user` 355 (343 before D5K2; `test_d5k2_focus`); `ext_adapt.py` (C.15.4): D5K's
  `d-goback-keys` (the user's exact D5-6 sequence; since D5K2 its Windows form is the keys), `d-goback-mirror`,
  `d-custom-cancel-keys`, and D5K2's `d-goback-winkeys` (the Windows-only Go Back, then Windows keys, against the
  pure Windows run): EXT_ADAPT_OK checks 107 -> 119 (D5K) -> 123 (D5K2), 33 DOS-form pairs. P0_OK checks 394,
  G-1's 77 runs, ADAPT_OK and DOS-PROFILE 1.7.2 unchanged.

**As implemented (FCE; area C).** Record [ext-FCE-record.md](ext-FCE-record.md) (2026-10-17; the orchestrator's
assignment "Escape on the game's list and modal dialogs", settled from the code first, and its ruling on outcome (b)):
a DOS-profile adapter rule beside D4c's filter-stage rules. Landed 2026-10-17 (the copy-in 02:32:11 UTC; the first
cycle stopped at `d2_registry`, the include rule of `present/dos`; the fix copied in 03:02:26 UTC; the three-step cycle
-r2 ALL_GREEN 03:41:10 UTC, `build/fce-status.txt`; Release 03:45:49 UTC, user build `build/user-builds/fce/`); compiled
and tested headless in Checked; no G-1 amendment. FIDELITY F193, F194 and F195.

Escape on the core's dialogs (extension FCE): in the DOS graphics a new Escape press without Alt or Ctrl on Find City
(131), a Civilopedia list (130 with its list), the production list (140) or the city name (137), recognised by their
controls (`present/dos/pdos_fce.c`), becomes the dialog's own cancel character: the filter stage emits a host character
1Bh marked `PI_CHAR_TO_DIALOG` (input.h; the adapter's `PDOS_CHAR_TO_DIALOG`, pdos_fce.h, checked equal by input.c)
with the dialog's window, host_sync.c posts it for that dialog (`w16_dlg_post_char`, `user_dlg.c`), and the game's
WM_CHAR 1Bh handler cancels (ListDialogSubclassProc 1098:1DA8, ModalDialogSubclassProc 1170:206F, RunTextEntryDialog's
loop 10F0:0196). The press's repeats are consumed, its release passes. Under Windows 3.1 those handlers are unreachable
(IsDialogMessage turns Escape into IDCANCEL, which no game dialog has), so the Windows graphics keep the original's:
Escape does nothing there.

* **The dialogs, by their controls** (`pdos_fce_dialog`, through `w16q_*` only, O4): 131 (BUTTON 101 and 102, a shown
  LISTBOX 103, STATIC 106, no EDIT; RunListBoxDialog), 130 with RunListBoxDialog's LISTBOX 103 (the Civilopedia lists),
  140 (BUTTON 101..103, a shown LISTBOX 104, STATIC 106; RunModalDialogLoop, ChooseCityProduction) and 137 (BUTTON 101
  and 102, a shown EDIT 103, STATIC 104; RunTextEntryDialog). The dialog is the active top-level window, shown and
  enabled, with at most 64 children, no control 2 and no other LISTBOX or EDIT (a hidden one counts). So a Civilopedia
  entry page (its list hidden), dialog 144's name page (D5d's I-text) and dialog 143 (D5e's I-custom) are not named.
  The block decides before the D4 rows (first in `key_down`, right after the frame is taken); the Windows graphics'
  keys (frame 0) never reach it; without the observer (CheckedNoObs) nothing is named.
* **The release.** RunTextEntryDialog's loop acts on the Go Back it sent at the next message it removes for the dialog
  (10F0:01F9..020B), so dialog 137 closes at the release (WM_KEYUP 1Bh passes to the core, as the release of a key
  whose press closed a dialog).
* **The post** (`w16_dlg_post_char`, 12.10): WM_CHAR with the dialog's hwnd, lParam repeat 1 and the press's scan
  code, for a shown, enabled top-level dialog of the manager without a control 2; anything else is refused silently
  (only a dialog closed between the decision and the post can meet it).
* **The landing's fix.** `pdos_fce.h` defines `PDOS_CHAR_TO_DIALOG` 0x0001u instead of including input.h (no
  `present/dos` file may include it: `registry.py`'s include rule); `present/input.c` checks it equals
  `PI_CHAR_TO_DIALOG` (`typedef pi_adapt_dialog_ok`, as for `PDOS_CH_LOST`); `registry.py`'s OBSERVER_FILES names
  `pdos_fce.c`.
* **A later unit** that draws one of these dialogs as a DOS screen with its own Escape row (D7: Find City or the
  production list as a DOS box) takes that dialog out of `pdos_fce_dialog` or keeps FCE's block first. D6f's rows
  follow FCE's block (a MenuZ box opened over one of FCE's dialogs is the active window, so its Escape is D6f's).
* **Customize World's Escape** (dialog 143, the Windows graphics): the original's write of -1 to worldParams[-200]
  (12D8:3764, g_advanceSource[2][36..37]) is reproduced inside the emulated DGROUP, with no fault and no effect on the
  game (FIDELITY F195; an X9 item).
* **Tests.** `test_user` `test_fce` (355 -> 365), `test_pdos_fce` (new; 43 checks in Checked and Release, 1 in
  CheckedNoObs), `ext_fce.py` (test.cmd's Checked section `ext_fce`: Escape as a real key in host form on the four
  dialogs in both graphics, `EXT_FCE_OK runs 8`). User checks FCE-1..FCE-5.
