### 12.9 Map renderer and zoom; the DOS map (D1 area D; phases M and D6)

Design of extension 5.8 with the user's levels, Z-1..Z-3, 7.5, the zoom parts of 7.3, 7.4 and 7.8,
and the DOS map rules of D6. Detailed design: [verify/ext-d1-draft-D.md](verify/ext-d1-draft-D.md)
(Revision 3). M (the Windows-profile map and zoom) is implemented: see "As implemented (M)" at the end
of this section. The bullets before it are the design; the DOS map (D6) is not built. M2's map display model
with the Windows rule set and the present check are built in D6a ("As implemented (D6a; area D)", after M's).
The DOS rule set on the main map window's client, the dos-settled check and the map element on the A canvas are
built in D6c ("As implemented (D6c; area D)", after D6a's). The zoom levels in the DOS profile (the DOS in-view source
`M_dos`, the DOS ring `G_dos` and the play palette's steps in the layer) are built in D6d ("As implemented (D6d; area
D)", after D6c's); the small map and status window (D6e) are not. Four later units of 2026-10-16/17 change the zoom in
both profiles, presentation only (P0): MZ ([ext-MZ-record.md](ext-MZ-record.md); FIDELITY F185, F130) centres the
layer on the core's view at every view change at -1, MZW ([ext-MZW-record.md](ext-MZW-record.md); F185, F132) makes the
World Map's rectangle outline the map window's area at -1 and +1 (since D6e the DOS profile's small map outlines
the same area), MZI ([ext-MZI-record.md](ext-MZI-record.md); F185)
centres the layer at +1 on the game's centring and on a new active unit, and MZS ([ext-MZS-record.md](ext-MZS-record.md);
F190) fills the in-view sources' covered pixels from the overlap of the port's bitmap (or the DOS shadows) and keeps
the rest (notes in the bullets below).

* **Files** (`present/map/`, all `pmap_`): `pmap.h` (the interface), `pmap_zoom.c` (levels, geometry,
  anchoring, refusals), `pmap_layer.c` (clip, layer index image, composition, damage, palette),
  `pmap_inview.c` (the Windows in-view source `M`), `pmap_snap.c` (state and view snapshots),
  `pmap_state.c` (`PmapSrc`, reading a snapshot or live memory), `pmap_win.c` (the Windows rules),
  `pmap_dos.c` (the DOS rules, D6), `pmap_world.c` (world canvases), `pmap_model.c` (the map display
  model in B's shadow engine), `pmap_check.c` (mirror checks), `pmap_input.c` (layer input);
  `tests/test_map.c`, `tests/harness/ext_mirror.py`, `ext_zoom.py`. build.cmd adds `present\map`.
* **Interface** (`pmap.h`): `pmap_reset`, `pmap_on_event`, `pmap_on_probe`, `pmap_on_core_present`,
  `pmap_request_zoom(level, anchored, hx, hy, &reason)`, `pmap_level`, `pmap_pending`, `pmap_overlay`
  (after scaling, before `host_show`), `pmap_hit`, `pmap_layer_point` (kinds VIEW, COVERED, RING,
  NONE), `pmap_filter_ring` (A's frame 4), `pmap_src_current` and `pmap_view_current` (the exported
  snapshot readers B's small map uses), `pmap_diag`. One zoom state serves both profiles.
* **Levels and geometry.** Levels -1, 0, +1 (decision C1): level 0 has no layer; the scale is the
  frame's own factor times 2^l, so under `integer` k host pixels per map pixel become k/2 and 2k.
  Windows level -1 is pixel-perfect only for even k; at k = 1 and odd k it is the recorded 2:1
  reduction; the DOS profile shows DOS art pixels at `m = 2·s_l`, pixel-perfect for every k (built in D6d). Under
  `fit` the levels halve and double the actual factor. +1 is refused below a 192x192 layer.
* **The layer.** Drawn in host pixels after the scaler, clipped to the map window's visible client
  region (minus the move/size outline band), composed over the base frame; in view it shows the core's
  own pixels (`M`, from the presented screen and the map port's bitmap under dialogs), out of view at
  -1 the ring from the latest snapshot. Snapshots are copied at core probes after each completed
  redraw, unit move and turn (DrawTilesToPort, RedrawMapRect, UpdateUnitSightAndContacts, DeleteUnit,
  ProcessGameTurn, BlinkActiveUnitUntilInput), never at an arbitrary poll. The renderer reimplements
  the map functions on the snapshot and draws no random number.
* **Mirror proofs (7.5, Z-2).** M1 checks at M: `key` (TileCacheLookup's key against the rules),
  `render` (TileCacheInsert's shape), `settled` (the port bitmap after DrawTilesToPort and
  RedrawMapRect against the world-canvas rules). M2, the per-present comparison of the event model
  with the virtual screen, is built and gated with D6 (decision C2), with `dos-settled` for the DOS
  rules.
* **The map display model** (M2, D6). `pmap_model` registers B's interface from `pmap_reset`:
  `PdosMacro`s with `PDOS_MAC_DROP` on DrawMapTile, DrawUnit, DrawUnitAt and DrawCityLabels and
  `PDOS_MAC_CONTEXT` on AnimateUnitMove and ResolveCombat (all with exit probes), and
  `PdosShadowHooks` for the map port `P`, the scratch port `S` and the client `Wn` (claims, black
  fills, copies, resize, free). One table serves both rule sets: DOS always from D6, Windows under
  `--mirror 2`.
* **Input (Z-3).** In view, points map exactly to the core's map pixels; covered points clamp to the
  visible region; over the ring moves clamp to the view. A press latches its class until its release
  (RV2-3). A left press on a ring square becomes, at the sync, a move and press at the small-map
  point that recentres the core's map on that square (a click the user could have made, CenterMapOn-
  SmMapClick's own arithmetic inverted; decision C6), and its release the release there; a right press
  in the ring and a press beyond the poles are not delivered. The wheel zooms over the map (I8).
* **Commands.** Chords Ctrl+Shift+Plus, Minus, 0 (audited against ACCELERATOR 128; since UX4b also
  Ctrl+Shift+Home as one more reset key, the gray Home and the keypad's 7 with Num Lock off, 12.2 and 12.5, F114); system-menu radio
  items 0x10C0..0x10E0; `--zoom default|-1|0|+1`; the settings key `zoom`; the harness `zoom` command;
  `presented.log` adds `zoom L view CWxCH view_sha256` and `view-NNNNN.bmp` dumps.
* **The DOS map (D6, section 14).** Shapes from E's `pdos_shapes` by family and DOS index; the page-2
  coast and land-base rectangles from E's page-2 picture (RV2-8); colour tables DGROUP:1946/1956
  through the registry; squares, units (with the sentry recolour), cities, labels (font 1 inferred,
  L23), edges and the DOS ring drawn with B's driver at `k = 2`; the core's redraw order and moves drive
  the animations, the blink's phases are the DOS clock's since DBL (P3, P6); the map palette is B's play palette. (As built in D6d the ring `G_dos` holds
  1280 x 800 DOS indices drawn through a k = 1 pen, one DOS pixel per `G_dos` pixel; the layer's `m = 2·s_l` gives
  each DOS pixel k x k host pixels at -1.)
* **Prompt rules met.** 5.8 (map window class `CIV` only; the small map, status window and World Map
  report not zoomed; anchoring; wrap and poles; following the core; no random numbers; mirror
  equality; hashes naming host size and zoom; input), Z-1..Z-3, O3/O4/O6/O7 through A, I1, I2, I7, I8,
  T7, D-5's map, P0 (the zoom never feeds `present_game_screen`, V4). *(MZW, 2026-10-16, the user's request on
  D6d-6; FIDELITY F185, F132: the small map's image is still not zoomed, but at -1 and +1 the World Map outlines the
  map window's area: `present/map/pmap_smrect.c` draws the layer's world rectangle on the World Map's image in the
  core's own frame colour, clipped to the world's rows, and hides the core's rectangle with the core's own pixels
  rebuilt from its OBS_DRAW events, written into the Windows profile's frame `g_win` (both profiles showed it until
  D6e; since D6e the DOS profile shows its own small map, `pdos_aplay`, whose outline surrounds the same area through
  `pdos_aplay_set_zoom_area`, 12.7 "As implemented (D6e; area B)");
  presentation only, the core and the virtual screen only read; level 0 unchanged; S1's relation check (12.1) admits
  exactly the pixels the patch owns.)* *(D6e, 2026-10-17; FIDELITY F132: the DOS small map is likewise not zoomed:
  `ext_zoom.py` compares its model, profiles.log's `smmap`, at -1 and at +1 in the same scenario, size, policy,
  profile and copy, and across the profiles at each sample; the ZOOM_OK line gains `not zoomed PAIRS/SAMPLES`, 38/448
  at the landing.)*
* **Choices** (D 20, one line each): M1 then M2 with D6; one zoom state for both profiles; `fit` levels
  halve and double the actual factor; levels kept before a map window exists; the clip excludes the
  outline band; inner host rectangles under `sharp`; in view under a dialog, the map port's bitmap (since MZS its
  overlap at the same coordinates, else the previous pixel);
  snapshots at every trigger in every run; ring labels without the 32 cap, column-major; Windows model
  shadows only under `--mirror 2`; the active-unit pan for the human's units only; the client-relative
  offset kept across view changes (since MZ at +1 only; at -1 a new view centres the layer, then the pole clamp; since
  MZI at +1 only for view changes without the game's request, SetMapViewAndRedraw's request centring the layer); a ring press becomes a recentring small-map click (C6); the cursor
  not zoomed; the zoom remembered in `zoom`; DOS ring labels unclamped; P9 corrected (the core's view
  frame), applied by B; page-2 rectangles from E's page-2 picture; the `pmap_` prefix for every public
  name and type; a press latches its class until its release.
* **Tests and gates.** `test_map.c` (geometry tables for k = 1..4 and fit factors, anchors and clamps,
  limits, input mapping and the frame-4 translation for both scales and wrap representatives,
  `pmap_filter_ring`'s parts, wheel accumulation, rules on synthetic snapshots, DOS composition order);
  `ext_mirror.py` (7.5 checks), `ext_zoom.py` (zoomed-frame goldens and determinism across switches);
  V4 in `ext_p0.py`; 7.8 chord and wheel runs. M's gate: 7.5's M1 checks and Z-1..Z-3 for Windows.
  D6's gate adds M2's present check, `dos-settled` and Z for the DOS profile.
* **Open** (D 21): D1 (the scratch port's DC and bitmap offsets, M2: answered in D6a, below), D2 (which
  DrawTile calls inside ResolveCombat draw the lunge and explosion, M2: answered in D6a, below), D3 (DGROUP
  DCFC and D806, D6: DCFC settled from the listing in D6c, D806 taken as 0, below), D4 (sight updates per AI
  turn, M: answered in Mb1, below), D5 (drawing outside the macros, M2: answered in D6a, below; for the DOS rule
  set in D6c, below), D6 (label and digit fonts:
  font 1 inferred, B's OB4; not settled by D6c's listing reading, font 1 kept), D7 (L11, L12, D6: L12 read from the
  listing in D6d, no cycling over faded entries in the play return path, below; L11 is D6e's), D8 (posted
  button state after FlushInputEvents, M: answered in Ma, below). D9 and D10 are closed.

**As implemented (M).** Gate record [ext-M-acceptance.md](ext-M-acceptance.md) (2026-10-11): 7.5's M1
checks (`key`, `render`, `settled`) and Z-1..Z-3 for the Windows profile are met in Checked, CheckedNoObs
and Release; the Asan run is deferred to the final pass (K8); the agent UI check was not completed (below).
M was done in three units, each with its record: Ma, zoom, layer, in-view source, input and commands
([ext-Ma-record.md](ext-Ma-record.md)); Mb1, snapshots, the Windows rules, the world canvas and the ring
([ext-Mb1-record.md](ext-Mb1-record.md)); Mb2, the mirror checks, the zoomed-frame goldens and the gate
([ext-Mb2-record.md](ext-Mb2-record.md)). The results are compiled, headless and Win32-headless (hidden
window); no DOS program was executed. By decision C2 the per-present comparison M2 is built and gated
with D6, not here.

* **Files** (`present/map/`, all `pmap_`; 254 portable sources in the final build; `build.cmd`'s loop, its
  `/I` list and the clang-tidy `-I` list gain `present\map`, and `obs_lint.py` lints the files with the
  rest of `present/`). Ma: `pmap.h`, `pmap_int.h` (`PmapState pmap_g`, `PmapGeo`), `pmap_zoom.c`,
  `pmap_layer.c`, `pmap_inview.c`, `pmap_input.c`. Mb1: `pmap_snap.c`, `pmap_state.c`, `pmap_win.c`,
  `pmap_world.c`. Mb2: `pmap_check.c`. Tests: `tests/test_map.c`, `tests/harness/ext_mirror.py`,
  `ext_zoom.py` and the goldens `tests/harness/ext-zoom-golden.txt`. The design's `pmap_dos.c` and
  `pmap_model.c` are D6's and were not created in M. Under `CIV_OBSERVER 0` every entry of `pmap.h` is a
  stub: no layer, every level refused with "not in this build (CIV_OBSERVER 0)".
* **Interface** (`pmap.h`). The levels `PMAP_ZOOM_OUT/DEFAULT/IN`; the listeners `pmap_reset`,
  `pmap_on_event`, `pmap_on_probe`, `pmap_on_core_present`; the zoom `pmap_request_zoom`,
  `pmap_zoom_command`, `pmap_level`, `pmap_in_refused` (the menu's gray); the layer `pmap_pending`,
  `pmap_restore` (the base frame's give-back before the overlay), `pmap_overlay`, `pmap_active`,
  `pmap_layer_image` (A-6's hash); input `pmap_hit`, `pmap_layer_point`, `pmap_filter_ring`;
  `pmap_view_current`; from Mb1 `pmap_src_current` (never NULL: all zero before the first trigger) and
  `pmap_tick`; from Mb2 `pmap_mirror_start`, `pmap_mirror_end`; `pmap_diag`; the test seams
  `pmap_test_map` and `pmap_test_smmap` (the map window and the small map without an emulation).
* **Zoom state and levels.** One state serves both profiles. Levels -1, 0, +1 (C1); level 0 has no layer.
  For +1 host column hx shows world column `Xo + floor((hx - Rc.l) * W / (2 SW))`, for -1
  `Xo + floor((hx - Rc.l) * 2W / SW)` (rows likewise; `Rc` the map client's whole inner host rectangle,
  the formulas of `present_host_rect_inner` without the clip; `Wl`, `Hl` the ceilings). Clamps (draft D
  11.2): at +1 inside the view, never the ring; at -1 the whole view visible, the fewest rows beyond the
  poles, the world centred when `Hl > 1600`, an even origin (down, or up when down would cut the view's far
  edge). Anchors (11.1): the centre for a new level from a chord, the menu, `--zoom` and the harness
  command; the wheel keeps the world pixel under the pointer (at level 0 through `present_map_point`); a
  resize or a layout change keeps the world pixel at the layer's host centre; a new view keeps the layer's
  place on the client (11.3), then the clamps. *(MZ, 2026-10-16, the user's report of about 15:30 UTC and the
  choice "Keep the pole limit"; FIDELITY F185, F130: at -1 a new view of the core (the game's C, its automatic
  centring of the next unit, its scrolling, a ring or small-map click) centres the layer on the view, `pmap_geo_centre`
  (`centre_on_view`: the client's centre at the layer's host centre, the CENTRE anchor's arithmetic, then
  `pmap_geo_clamp`), so the pole rule alone keeps it off-centre: the centring is exact while the view's centre is at
  least half the layer's height from both poles, rows Hl/2 .. 50 - Hl/2 (Hl twice the client's height in rows: about
  rows 13..15 to 35..37 for the usual window sizes), and nearer a pole the unit stays toward it. Before MZ the kept
  offset ratcheted: after a visit near a pole the clamped offset stayed for the session. A wheel's or a menu zoom's
  anchor holds until the next view change; +1 unchanged.)* *(MZI, 2026-10-16, the user's report of about 19:24 UTC
  (the Cavalry of MZTEST.SAV at +1) and 19:45 UTC; FIDELITY F185: at +1 the game's setting of its view, the entry of
  SetMapViewAndRedraw 1110:03E9 (its callers: CenterMapViewOn for every centring on a unit or a city, the World Map
  click and the scroll keys; not while the view is ProcessCity's temporary one), gives the requested square (left +
  cols/2, top + rows/2), the unit's for CenterMapViewOn, attached to the view snapshot of that redraw (`take_view`;
  dropped if no snapshot comes before the next input wait); the next layer update (`pmap_layer_update`) puts that
  square's centre at the map window's centre (`pmap_centre_square`: the copy nearest the game view's centre, so a
  request across the date line never jumps a world width), then the +1 clamp, whether or not the view moved; view
  changes without a request (the scroll bars, ProcessCity's restore) keep the layer's place on the client. At
  BlinkActiveUnitUntilInput's entry a different active unit than the last (tracked at every level, so zooming in is
  not a new unit) is centred where the layer would have to pan to show it with the one-square margin
  (`pmap_centre_unit`; EnsureSquareInMapView 11C0:231D's rule at the layer's size), then clamped; the same unit's
  moves keep the least pan (the follow rule below, D6d-3). A wheel's or a menu zoom's new geometry drops a pending
  request: the anchor holds until the next request or the next new unit. The edge limit is the game's view (+1 never
  leaves it): with Map Making (advance 8) SetMapViewOrigin 1208:2140 stops the view at the map's north and south
  edges, so a unit within about 3 rows of them stays off-centre toward that edge; without it the view goes up to 25
  rows beyond either pole and the unit is centred. -1 unchanged (MZ). presentation-diag.log's map line gains
  `centre_view N centre_unit N`.)* The follow rule (11.3), at +1: at
  `BlinkActiveUnitUntilInput`'s and `AnimateUnitMove`'s entries (the human's units; the move's destination
  through `g_dirDX/DY`) the least pan that shows the square with a one-square margin, on the nearest copy
  within ±1280. Limit (4.3): +1 is refused with "map window too small" while its layer would be under 96
  world pixels (3 x 3 squares) on either axis; a resize below that drops the level to 0. Requests and
  commands read only the module's state, so the Win32 window procedure may call them. Windows level -1 is
  the recorded 2:1 reduction at k = 1 and odd k (FIDELITY F129).
* **The layer** (`pmap_layer.c`, A-2). Active when the level is not 0, the Windows profile is visible, the
  view has a layout, the map window exists, is shown, not iconic, its client's visible region has 1..64
  rectangles and the view snapshot's size equals the client's; each other case is a counter. The clip is
  the visible region in host pixels less the move or size outline's band (`w16q_sys_track`). An index image
  over the clip's bounding box (XRGB with area weights under `fit` and sharp, from `present_axis_taps`);
  each host pixel shows world pixel (X, Y): in view the mirror `M`, beyond the poles black, else the ring.
  `pmap_overlay` writes the image into the view inside the clip only, where the scaler just wrote and
  where the layer changed; a clip that shrinks or a layer that goes gives its area back to the base frame
  (`pmap_restore`). Every overlay and restore evaluates the activity again, so a profile switch, an options
  change or a layout change shows through `show_all` without a core present (Ma review finding 1). B-3 was
  delivered in `scale.c`: `present_host_rect_inner`, `present_axis_taps`, `present_taps_free`; the scaler's
  own sharp taps are now made by `present_axis_taps` over the whole axis (`test_present` 521 checks, the
  present goldens and `ext_frames.py` unchanged).
* **The in-view source `M`** (`pmap_inview.c`, draft D 6.1). A client-sized index copy of the core's map
  client, kept at every core present in every profile: visible pixels from the virtual screen, covered or
  off-screen ones from the map port's bitmap 12D8:455C (`OBS_BITMAP` damage on it waits for the next core present).
  *(MZS, 2026-10-16/17, the user's failed MZW-8; FIDELITY F190: a bitmap of another size than the client gives the
  covered pixels it has, the overlap at the same client coordinates, as WdwMapProc's WM_PAINT 1208:0332 copies it
  (BitBlt copies no pixel outside the bitmap); a covered pixel it does not have, or every one without a bitmap, keeps
  M's previous pixel (`port_size` counts those presents); black only where M never had a pixel (M is black when
  allocated). The visible pixels come from the screen less the move or size outline's band strips (the strips of
  `pmap_screen_exclusions`), which stay the port's or the previous pixels (`fill_m`, `copy_less_band`), so the layer
  shows no ghost of the outline; the layer's clip leaves the band itself out. Before MZS the covered pixels were
  black with `port_size` when the bitmap's size differed: with civ64's layout (F103) the bitmap is 454 x 421 for a
  453 x 421 client, so the layer drew a black block beside every open menu or dialog at -1 and +1, which the
  harness's default layout, with a bitmap of the client's size, hid.)* The map window is read through
  `w16q_wnd`, `w16q_wnd_client_vis` and `w16q_sys_track`. The view snapshot (8) is taken at the exits of
  `DrawTilesToPort` (port argument `&g_mapOffscreenPort`) and `RedrawMapRect` (window argument
  `&g_mapWindowPort`), skipped while `g_mapWindowLocked` or `g_cityScreenMapMode` is set (`take_view` stays
  in `pmap_inview.c`, called together with the state snapshot).
* **The state snapshot** (`pmap_snap.c`, 7.1). One presentation-owned `PmapSnap` (about 98 KB): the 320x200
  layered map through `g_lpScreenBuffer`'s far pointer (1420:B394), `g_tileVisibility` with the byte
  before and after (13E8:0009..13E8:0FAA), `g_units`, `g_cities`, `g_cityNames`, `g_humanPlayer`,
  `g_revealAll`, `g_mapDrawMode`, `g_mapViewerCivMask`, the map seed `g_randomSeed` 13C0:0000 (not the
  generator's `g_randSeed` 1420:4EE0, which is never read) and the rule tables `g_terrainByMapColor` (as 256
  words), `g_dirDX`/`g_dirDY`, `g_unitTypes`, `g_terrainTypes`. It is copied with `w16q_read` at every
  trigger, whatever the level, and `gen` increments. Triggers (dispatched by `pmap_on_probe`):
  `DrawTilesToPort` 1208:1866 exit (the map port, `g_cityScreenMapMode` 0; also the view), `RedrawMapRect`
  1208:1534 exit (the map window port; also the view), `UpdateUnitSightAndContacts` 11C0:0395 exit,
  `DeleteUnit` 11C0:16A4 exit, `ProcessGameTurn` 1088:0000 entry, `BlinkActiveUnitUntilInput` 11C0:0EC2
  entry. Never at an arbitrary poll. The ring is therefore a function of the latest snapshot.
* **`PmapSrc`** (`pmap_state.c`, 7.2). The reader the rules use: `pmap_src_snap` over a snapshot and
  `pmap_src_live` over the emulation through `w16q_*` (inside the guard only; the key check uses it).
  `px` outside 320x200 is 0 as GetScreenBufferPixel 11E8:0DCF returns it; other out-of-range reads give 0
  or a zero record and count `map_bad_read`. It carries `gen` and, from Mb2, `city_mode` (live:
  `g_cityScreenMapMode` 1330:0000; a snapshot takes 0).
* **The Windows rules** (`pmap_win.c`, 7.3): pure functions of a `PmapSrc` and the shapes. `mw_terrain`,
  `mw_known`, `mw_real` (1110:16C0, 1110:19E0, 1110:199B), `mw_special` (1110:1C72), `mw_hut` (1110:1CD9),
  `mw_key` (ComputeTileAppearanceKey 1118:04B4), `mw_render_key` (RenderTileFromKey 1118:0000 with CopyTile
  11A8:0328 and OverlayTile 11A8:076F, including the fast path that never processes the last pixel and
  colour 0 = the source's top-left pixel), `mw_city_at` (FindCityAt 11C8:00C4), `mw_unit_at`
  (FindUnitAtTile 1110:180E without its repair write), `mw_best_defender` (FindBestDefenderInStack 11C0:1A2C
  and RateDefenderCallback 11C0:1A81 with 16-bit products and ForEachUnitInStack 11C0:1EE8's 10-member cap),
  `mw_top_unit` (RedrawMapSquare 1110:1461, DrawUnitStack 1110:1516, DrawUnit's carried-unit rule
  1110:0ECE), `mw_square` (DrawMapTile 1110:04D0 without the flush: tile, fog edges, city marker, unit with
  stack shadow, sentry copy, letters and the grey frame) and `mw_label` (DrawCityLabels 1208:1F64 and
  DrawTextInRect 1098:0ED1: box (x-134, y+32, x+166, y+52), centred, black shadow). `mw_res_load` copies
  the 638 shapes of the nine handle tables into presentation memory through `w16q_block`/`w16q_read` and
  maps the colours (GetColorIndexRGB 1098:01B0 through the map port DC's palette) inside the guard; it
  reloads only when a handle, a block size, a colour table, the font or a text colour's physical index
  changed. `mw_square_clip(src, x, y, dst, ox, oy, clip, parts)` (Mb2) draws the core's unclipped square
  (the stack shadow's 2-pixel overhang) and splits DrawMapTile's part (`MW_PART_TILE`) from the unit
  (`MW_PART_UNIT`); `mw_square` is the cell-clipped call of both. The fog edges test `city_mode`
  (DrawMapTile 1110:065B). Text and pens are drawn on presentation memory with `w16q_draw_on`; the rules
  draw no random number.
* **The world canvas** (`pmap_world.c`, 7.4). `G_win`, 2560x1600 indices, `calloc`ed at first need (4,096,000
  bytes; failure: the ring stays black and `map_no_memory` counts). `pmap_world_update`, inside the guard
  and at level -1 only, recomputes the 4000 `MapSqDesc` descriptors when the snapshot's `gen` changed
  (unseen squares black as DrawTilesToPort mode 0; key, terrain, fog bits, the city marker's inputs with the
  bevel pen's physical index, the top unit's inputs), renders the changed squares into their cells, then
  the labels over them clipped to the cells, and damages the layer at every copy of each changed square
  (`pmap_layer_damage_world`, or `_all` above 512 squares). Labels: every city the core would label, with
  no 32-label cap, in world column-major order, wrapping east-west; each label's span is measured with its
  text, and a changed, added or removed label re-renders the squares under its old and new spans. The
  canvas also re-renders when another caller loaded changed shapes (`n_world_res`; the render and settled
  checks load them at level 0 too).
* **The ring** (level -1). `pmap_ring_px` (in `pmap_world.c`) reads the canvas: the main map's Windows
  rules from the latest snapshot, replacing Ma's interim black. The canvas updates at the tick
  (`pmap_tick`, step 3a of `present_tick`, 12.5) and at every core present, at level -1 only;
  `pmap_pending` is also 1 while the canvas is behind the snapshot, so the tick shows. A show from the
  window procedure (a resize, a switch) uses the canvas as it is; the next tick brings it up to date.
  `pmap_reset` frees the canvas and the shapes and zeroes the snapshot. The ring takes the city-screen map
  mode as 0 (FIDELITY F132).
* **Input** (`pmap_input.c`; `present/arrive.c`, `present/input.c`, 12.5). `pmap_layer_point`: in view the
  client pixel of the world pixel, exact; covered, the nearest visible client pixel; the ring and beyond the
  poles, the nearest view point, then the nearest visible client point, a ring square carrying its column
  and row. `pmap_hit` is the wheel's region (the layer's clip, or at level 0 the map client's visible host
  region). A press latches its class until its release (RV2-3). **The ring press** (`pmap_filter_ring`,
  frame 4): a left press on a ring square becomes the small-map click that `PollMouseClick` 1108:0000
  passes to `CenterMapOnSmMapClick` 1210:056E. The target view is `CenterMapViewOn`'s, left' = col - cols/2,
  top' = row - rows/2; deltaX = viewLeft - left' taken modulo 80 into the representative (nearest 0, then
  ±80, ±160) that puts the click inside the small map's visible client; clickX = (frame.left +
  frame.right)/2 - deltaX * `g_smMapScale`, which the core's signed truncating division maps back exactly
  (y without the wrap). The press becomes a move (without the left button's bit; a right button held under
  another latch stays held) and the press at that point; its frame-4 moves give nothing; its release, from
  anywhere, is the release at the same point. Press and release are consumed, not delivered
  (`ring_refused`), when the small map is hidden (its window, or the data block's `visible` flag at +0x2C
  that `CenterMapOnSmMapClick` tests), iconic, disabled, captured by another window or the point is
  covered; a right press on the ring never is delivered, nor is a press beyond the poles. The small map
  is read with `w16q_*` inside the filter's guard. When no layer is drawn, a held view press's remaining
  parts take the visible frame's mapping (`PI_LAYER_GONE`).
* **Commands.** Chords Ctrl+Shift+Plus, Minus and 0 (main row and keypad; audited in Ma against ACCELERATOR
  128, 12.2); the wheel over the map client, each full 120 of delta one step (remainder kept, forward zooms
  in; the command carries the steps and the wheel's host point as the anchor; elsewhere the wheel is ignored
  and the sum clears); the system-menu radio items 0x10C0..0x10E0 (12.2); `--zoom default|-1|0|+1`; the
  settings key `zoom`; the harness `at <ms> zoom -1|0|+1|reset` (`zoom 1` is accepted beside `+1`; `zoom
  0` goes through `pmap_request_zoom`, `zoom reset` through `pmap_zoom_command`, the chord's path; a
  refused request writes `zoom refused <reason>` in `presented.log`) and `--zoom` (requested before the
  core starts). The harness's 7.8 mode runs the chords' and the wheel's commands as civ64 does. Under
  `CIV_OBSERVER 0` the harness `--zoom` and the zoom command exit 2. `presented.log` show lines gain `zoom L
  view CWxCH view_sha256 SHA` while the layer is drawn (L in -1, 1; the whole host view buffer's hash, its
  size first); `--dump-presented` writes `view-NNNNN.bmp` for those shows; `profiles.log` sample lines gain
  ` map windows SHA` (the layer image) while the level is not 0, which can lag the picture by one render
  (the sample is tick step 4, before the overlay in step 7); `presentation-diag.log` and civ64's debug
  log gain the `pmap ...` line (counters, the map client, the view, the layer's host rectangle and origin;
  from Mb1 also `map_no_memory`, `snaps`, `snap_us`, `turns`, `sight_turn_max`, `snap_turn_max`,
  `snap_turn_max_us`, `world_updates`, `world_squares`, `world_res`, `world_us`, `world_max_us`; the `_us`
  fields are host wall-clock time, informational; the log is parsed, never compared).
* **The mirror checks** (`pmap_check.c`, 10.1; harness `--mirror 1`, `mirror.log`). Run from the map
  listener (`pmap_on_probe` calls `pmap_check_probe` first); nothing while off. `--mirror 2` is refused
  (D6's); `--mirror` is refused under `CIV_OBSERVER 0` (exit 2). **key**: at `TileCacheLookup` 1110:030C
  entry when its return address is DrawMapTile's call (1110:0579) inside a DrawMapTile entry..exit, the key
  argument against `mw_key(pmap_src_live(), x, y)` of the enclosing DrawMapTile's square. **render**: at
  `TileCacheInsert` 1110:01A3 entry from DrawMapTile (1110:05B8), the shape block the core rendered
  (`TileNew(32, 32)`: width, height and 1,024 pixels) against `mw_render_key(key, terrain)`. **settled**:
  at the exits of `DrawTilesToPort` 1208:1866 (the map window into the map port, modes 0 and 1) and
  `RedrawMapRect` 1208:1534, the port bitmap (12D8:455C) copied at the entry is redrawn by the rules in the
  core's own order and compared whole with the bitmap the core left. Mode 0: the squares of
  `GetVisibleTileRange` 1208:1D21 column-major, clipped by the window data's `clientRect`, each
  `RedrawMapSquare` at `RestoreBlockXY` 1110:0448's position or `FillRect BLACK_BRUSH`, then
  `DrawCityLabels(&window->rect)`. RedrawMapRect: its first/end columns and rows, the update rectangle
  grown and clipped as compiled, `EraseRect` with `g_hEraseBrush` 1420:995E, then
  `DrawCityLabels(&updateRect)`. Mode 1 (the dissolve): the order of the call's own `RedrawMapSquare`,
  `DrawUnit` and `DrawCityLabels` entries (a `DrawCityLabels` from 1208:1B66, return address 1208:1B69,
  erases then labels; from DrawMapTile's flush it only labels, and the unit follows those labels), so no
  random number is read or drawn. Labels follow the core's policy (`CollectVisibleCityLabels` 1208:1DD7:
  the 32-label cap, column-major order, the top row clamped without moving the pixel origin;
  `DrawCityLabels` 1208:1F64: whole labels whose box meets the rectangle). Mode 2 and other modes draw
  nothing into the port and are not checked. `mirror.log` lines: settled checks always write a line (with
  ` call NAME mode M ops N squares S labels L`); key and render checks write one only on a mismatch (at
  most 2,000 mismatch lines; counted always); the end line adds `key K render R settled S mismatched k r s
  keys D units U cities C` after `unexplained 0` (the coverage `ext_mirror.py` prints).
* **`ext_mirror.py`** (16.2, in `test.cmd checked` after `ext_adapt.py`; output
  `build/test/Checked/ext-mirror.txt`): `--mirror 1` on every scenario row (the 23 rows of
  `scenarios.py`), gate3b's S2, S2b, S2c, S3 and the soaks S4 and S5, 29 runs. Besides the checks it
  compares every run's frame, snapshot and result lines and `events.log` with ext_p0's V0 output and, for
  S4 and S5, with uninit.py's runs (P0: the checks change no frame, checkpoint or event).
* **`ext_zoom.py`** (16.3, in `test.cmd checked`; output `build/test/Checked/ext-zoom.txt`): scenarios
  `new`, `load` and `orders` at 640x480, 1280x960, 1920x1440 `integer`, 2560x1600 `fit` sharp and 1366x768
  `fit` nearest, and S4 at 1920x1440 only, at levels -1 and +1: 32 combinations, each run twice. The two
  runs' zoomed shows must be equal (determinism, Z-2 and 7.4); `view_sha256` is sampled every 5,000 ms of
  virtual time to the run's last zoomed show (the hash of the last zoomed show at or before it) and compared
  with the goldens `tests/harness/ext-zoom-golden.txt`, which `ext_zoom.py --update` writes after the
  determinism check. The full run takes about 9 minutes of wall time with 8 jobs.
* **Results (Checked, final cycle).** `test_map`: 127 checks (CheckedNoObs 1, the refusal; Release 127).
  **MIRROR_OK runs 29**: 8,240 checks (key 6,925, render 1,229, settled 86), 0 mismatches, every run's core
  trace equal to its reference (29 of 29). The settled checks: 78 at DrawTilesToPort mode 1 (the dissolve of
  a new map view), 8 at mode 0; 61 of the 86 drew a city label; RedrawMapRect was reached by no run.
  Coverage: at most 65 distinct key and terrain pairs per run (S4), 7 unit-mark and 2 city-state
  combinations. S4: 1,887 checks (key 1,807, render 65, settled 15); S5: 1,359 (1,337, 11, 11). **ZOOM_OK
  combinations 32 runs 64 samples 388**: the layer drawn in every run (164 to 3,686 zoomed shows per run), no
  `zoom refused` line, all 388 samples equal to the goldens (354 distinct hashes). `EXT_ADAPT_OK checks 20`
  (D3b2's 11 and M's 9: zoomkeys, ring, ringref). `EXT_G1_OK runs 77` on Checked and CheckedNoObs. Findings:
  one mismatch, in the check and not the rules (gate3b S3, the first settled check, mode 1): with
  `g_autoFlushToWindow` 1, DrawMapTile's flush 1110:0CF1 calls `DrawCityLabels(&rc)` for its square and the
  first replay took every `DrawCityLabels` entry for an erased square; the recording by kind above is the
  fix. No renderer fix was needed in `pmap_win.c` or `pmap_world.c`.
* **P0 evidence** (12.11; V4). `ext_p0.py` V4 runs the 27 P0 runs at `--host-size 1920x1440 --presented 1`
  with a generated script of the scenario's lines plus `zoom +1`, `zoom -1`, `zoom reset`, `zoom 0` in turn
  every 1,301 ms to the watchdog (1,490 zoom commands over the 27 runs): equal to V0 in all 27 (frame,
  snapshot and result lines, `events.log`, exit status); no `zoom refused` line; the layer drawn in 22 runs
  (2,715 shows with the zoom fields); the five runs without a layer (start, birth, fame-quit, fame-reload,
  display16) never have the map window shown at a zoom level. `P0_OK checks 279` (Checked: D3b2's 224 and
  V4's 55). All 27 V4 runs updated the world canvas (242 canvas updates, 264,076 squares rendered), so the
  ring ran under the comparison. V4's 27 `observer-probes.log` files equal V0's. CheckedNoObs: `P0_OK
  checks 56` (V5's 54 and the two refusals: `--zoom +1` and a script's `zoom +1` exit 2 with the message).
  The soaks S4 and S5 are not P0 runs (they are `ext_mirror.py`'s). In Mb1's final cycle every snapshot
  copy and canvas update ran inside the guard (probe, tick, core present) with no O4 fault in any run.
* **Measured costs** (informational; one machine, varying load). Layer render (`test_map`'s `costs()`, a
  synthetic map client, mean of ten, Release at -1 / +1, Ma's run): 1920x1440 `integer` 8.2 / 9.9 ms,
  3840x2160 13.7 / 20.0 ms, 2560x1600 `fit` sharp 35.7 / 25.7 ms; palette reconversion 1.0 / 0.9 ms,
  1.9 / 1.9 ms and 39.2 / 24.8 ms (Mb2's Release run: 10.8 / 9.9, 20.1 / 15.7 and 39.2 / 23.1 ms; reconversion
  1.1 / 1.2 ms and 39.3 / 24.1 ms at 1920x1440 and 2560x1600). Nearest is inside or near draft D 18's estimate (8 to 15 ms; about 4 ms);
  under `fit` sharp the layer keeps area-weighted XRGB, so a palette change weighs every pixel again and
  costs as much as a full render (25 to 40 ms at 4.4 M pixels, about 125 to 200 ms of CPU per second while
  the map palette cycles), a deviation from D 18. Memory: one index (nearest) or one XRGB (sharp) per host
  pixel of the clip's box, freed when the layer goes; `M` one index per client pixel; the canvas 4,096,000
  bytes and the shapes about 0.6 MB. Snapshot copy (S4, `soak.script`, 92 turns, 1920x1440, `--zoom -1`,
  Checked harness): about 98 KB, 4,433 copies in 323.9 ms, **73 µs** each (`w16q_read` per tile) against
  D 18's 5 to 8 µs. Canvas update: at most 2.0 ms (the first, 4,000 squares); 1,611 updates re-rendered
  12,228 squares in all, 162 ms, 0.1 ms per update on average; the shapes loaded 3 times.
* **Open D4 answered** (Mb1). In S4 the AI turns call `UpdateUnitSightAndContacts` at most 62 times per
  turn, not thousands; at most 67 snapshot copies and at most 5.0 ms of copying per turn (4.9 to 5.5 ms
  over three runs). The choice of 7.1 (copy at every trigger in every run) stands; a cheaper copy is
  possible and not needed.
* **Open D8 answered** (Ma). In w16 (`queue.c`) posting a mouse message records the posted MK_ state (the
  release clears MK_LBUTTON) and retrieving a button message with PM_REMOVE updates the key-state array;
  `FlushInputEvents` (11F0:4EA0) retrieves the queued input ranges with `PeekMessage(PM_REMOVE)`, so a
  release it removes still leaves the button up in both states and nothing stays down. The 7.8 `ring` run
  posts the click's move, press and release at one sync, so the release is queued when the small-map branch
  runs; its marks show the branch ran right after the press (`PollMouseClick` 1108:010B, then
  `CenterMapOnSmMapClick` and `FlushInputEvents`), and the run equals `ringref`, the same click made unzoomed
  on the small map, in `coreinput.log`, every frame, snapshot, result and event and the exit status.
* **Ma review** (a separate read-only agent session): no P0 violation. Eleven findings, nine fixed in Ma
  (listed with their fixes in [ext-Ma-record.md](ext-Ma-record.md#review)): the layer re-evaluating its
  activity at every show; the chords not consumed under `CIV_OBSERVER 0`; `w16q_draw_on`'s bounds (16-bit
  coordinates, 2^31-byte surfaces, text cut at 512 bytes, an unknown font a bad handle); `g_outline_on`
  cleared at a loop's end and by `w16_syscmd_reset`; `PI_LAYER_GONE`; the ring click's move clearing only
  the left button's bit. Kept and recorded: the small map's region cut at 64 rectangles (such a press is
  not delivered) and `profiles.log`'s map hash one render late.
* **Deviations from the design** (the tables with the reasons are in the records):
  * Ma ([deviations](ext-Ma-record.md#deviations-from-the-design)): `PiOut` gains `cmd_steps` and
    `cmd_at`; `pmap.h` gains the zoom command, the menu's gray, the give-back, the layer image and the test
    seams; `PI_LAYER_GONE`; the capture-loss release per button; the zoom chords are not chords under
    `CIV_OBSERVER 0`; the system-menu items request their level and are grayed without the observer; the
    harness spellings `zoom 1` and `zoom reset`; `profiles.log`'s `map windows` hash one render late;
    `presentation-diag.log`'s `pmap` line; V4 over the 27 P0 runs, not the soaks; the 7.8 reference in host
    form and the covered click as a unit test; the sharp layer's palette reconversion is a full render;
    `w16q_draw_on`'s bounds; `w16_syscmd_reset`; B-3 delivered in Ma; `ext_p0.py`'s refusal checks remove
    their run directories first.
  * Mb1 ([deviations](ext-Mb1-record.md#deviations-from-the-design)): the snapshot and reader carry the
    rule tables and `gen`; the shapes are copied into presentation memory at the canvas update; the canvas
    updates at the tick (step 3a) and at core presents, level -1 only; the descriptor holds the bevel pen's
    physical index (animated palette entries) and the resources' key the text colours' physical indices; a
    city bit without a city draws no marker and a broken stack ring no unit (the core reads index -1);
    GetColorIndexRGB index 10 maps as 0; the city-screen map mode as 0 in the ring; TextOut taken as
    TA_LEFT | TA_TOP; a label's span includes its measured text; `take_view` stays in `pmap_inview.c`; the
    snapshot copy costs ~73 µs (the per-turn total is under the estimate).
  * Mb2 ([deviations](ext-Mb2-record.md#deviations-from-the-design)): the settled check replays the core's
    own drawing order onto the port bitmap copied at the call's entry and compares the whole bitmap; modes
    0 and 1 are checked; mode 1's order comes from the call's own entries; key and render checks write a
    line only on a mismatch; `--mirror 2` refused (D6); `PmapSrc.city_mode`; `mw_square_clip` with `parts`;
    the canvas notices shape reloads by other callers; `ext_zoom.py` takes `orders` for "input", runs S4 at
    1920x1440 only and samples every 5,000 ms; 19's "not zoomed" small-map check not built.
* **Agent UI check: not completed.** On 2026-10-11 at about 21:23 UTC civ64.exe (the final Release build)
  was started visibly with `--data build\ext-m-ui\data` (a copy of `build/gamedata` without the `.wav` files),
  `--dos-data none --window 1920x1440 --scale-mode integer`, and computer-use access to it was granted. One
  screenshot of the title menu was taken (`build/ext-m-ui/01-title-menu-civ64-window.png`). The first
  input batch (selecting "Load a Saved Game") was stopped by a user interrupt before any action ran. The
  check was not retried: civ64 was ended and `%LOCALAPPDATA%\civ64\civ64.cfg` was found unchanged. So the
  zoom by chord and by wheel, the system-menu items and the ring click were not exercised in a visible
  window; they are covered headless and Win32-headless only. They move to the user's pending checks
  (manual-checklist-ext.md, M). The gate record leaves it to the orchestrator whether the incomplete check
  holds the gate open.
* **Not verified.** The visible zoom (above). RedrawMapRect's settled check and its view-snapshot path ran
  in no run (no scenario scrolls the map window by a strip; its exit as a snapshot trigger is checked by
  reading only). The 32-label cap, the duplicate test, the row quirk and the city-screen map mode
  (`city_mode` 1) were not reached by a settled check; coverage is what the scenarios reach (rare keys,
  unit marks and city states such as disorder and walls not all seen). The canvas's update logic (dirty
  squares, label spans, wrap) has no pixel proof against a fresh full render; `ext_zoom.py`'s goldens pin
  the zoomed frames, they do not prove them. The move/size outline is cut from the clip by reading only. In
  7.8 the recentring target square is checked in `test_map`, not read from the core's view after the click,
  and covered clicks and refused ring presses are unit tests only. Shapes changed in place under the same
  handle and size would not be seen by the canvas. M2 (`--mirror 2`), the dos-settled check, the DOS map,
  `ext_zoom.py`'s profile-switch half and Open D2 and D5 belong to D6; 19's "not zoomed" check was not
  built. Costs are from one machine under varying load. A-3b's bit equality is shown by G-1 and V0 for the
  drawing those runs reach; DrawText formats and pens no run uses are equal by inspection only. Asan was
  not built or run (deferred). No user acceptance yet: M-1, M-2 and the added items are pending.

**As implemented (D6a; area D).** Record [ext-D6a-record.md](ext-D6a-record.md) (2026-10-16; BRIEF-d6.md "Units" D6a):
the map display model `pmap_model` with the Windows rule set (M2; draft D 9.1..9.4 and B 7.5's table) and M2's present
check (D 10.1, 10.2), with the harness's `--mirror 2` and `ext_mirror.py` on it. It landed ahead of the D5 gate; the
landing and the engine's part (S8, the GR_PortDataToBitmap wiring, the engine rules) are in 12.7, "As implemented
(D6a; area B)". Compiled and headless in Checked (CheckedNoObs and Release privately in the stage); no DOS program was
executed; Asan deferred. Nothing is user-visible: the change is the harness's `--mirror 2` oracle and invisible
shadows; the Windows and DOS displays are unchanged (P0 in every mirror run; the DOS comparison, 12.7). The DOS rule
set's drawing is D6c's and B's replay of it D6b's (the brief's "Not in the unit").

* **Registration** (`present/map/pmap_model.c`, new; 9.2). The owner "pmap" of B's shadow engine; it keeps no shadows
  of its own. `pmap_reset` (`pmap_zoom.c`) calls `pmap_model_register(0)`: the six `PdosMacro`s of 9.3 with both
  rule-set bits and the `PdosShadowHooks` with the DOS bit; `pmap_mirror_start(2, ...)` calls
  `pmap_model_register(1)`, which registers the hooks again with both bits. Without `--mirror 2` the engine makes no
  Windows-rule shadow of P, S or Wn (no owner claims them): the Windows set costs nothing in play.
* **Claim** (9.2). `P` = the bitmap 12D8:455C while the DC 12D8:455A has it selected; `S` = the bitmap 12D8:44BE
  while the DC 12D8:44BC has it selected (Open D1); `Wn` = the window `g_mapWindowPort` 12B0:0004 +0. Every map
  shadow is `PDOS_RES_MAP` (device pixels), black at its claim; a surface of unknown size is 1 x 1 until resized
  (`unsized`).
* **Macros** (9.3). DROP on DrawMapTile 1110:04D0 (target: row 0..49, the map window open, `g_currentGrPort` null or
  the map port, scale 1:1; `g_cityScreenMapMode` is not tested: M's rules read it live as DrawMapTile does,
  1110:065B, and the port test leaves out the city screen's own port), DrawUnit 1110:0DBA (no dialog port, the map
  port current), DrawUnitAt 1110:0FF6 (no dialog port, a map point: vy >= 8, vx >= 200, 1220:100B) and
  DrawCityLabels 1208:1F64 (the current graf's DC shows P's bitmap); CONTEXT on AnimateUnitMove 11C0:2C32 and
  ResolveCombat 1050:0000. The Windows set's `enter` draws the function's whole effect from live state with M's
  rules (`pmap_win.c`: `mw_square_clip`, the new `mw_unit`, `mw_label`; `pmap_src_live`): the square, then at
  `g_autoFlushToWindow` the flush's labels over the square's part of the client (1110:0CF1); the unit at its square
  (1110:0DEB, IsRectOnMapScreen 1208:2061) with its stack shadow for DrawUnit and without it for DrawUnitAt alone;
  every collected label whose box meets the update rectangle (1208:1FC9). DrawMapTile also has a Windows-set
  `leave` (`l_map_tile`, the undefined bevel colour below). The DOS set's `enter` draws nothing yet (D6c).
* **Hooks** (9.4, B 7.5's table). `on_draw` explains black fills (an 8 x 8 probe of the brush in the DC's palette)
  and replays other drawing with `w16q_draw_on` (fills, frames, TextOut, DrawText; logged and counted by the
  engine); `on_copy` copies between map shadows (P -> Wn flushes and WM_PAINT, Wn -> Wn scrolls, P/S copies; the
  owner copies map-to-map SRCCOPYs itself through a buffer) and replays copies from an unshadowed 8-bit source
  (counted); `on_resize` counts (the shadow is black at its new size, the core repaints it); `on_free` drops a
  pending DrawTile. The DOS set's events are absorbed and counted (`dos_deferred`) for D6b and D6c.
* **Clipping by what reached the surface.** A window draw or copy changes only the pixels of `ObsDraw.changed` (the
  bounding box of the changed pixels, clipped by the visible region and, inside BeginPaint, the update region;
  screen coordinates less the client origin from `w16q_wnd`). Draws that changed nothing are explained (`nothing`);
  partly reaching draws are counted (`clipped`). Limit: a bounding box, so a two-rectangle region could be
  over-drawn between its rectangles (no present mismatch shows it in the 29 runs).
* **DrawTile 11A8:0CB1** (an entry-only probe, so not a macro). Into P or S while no Windows-rule DROP bracket drops
  there, it is drawn at its entry from the shape's own TileBlock with DrawTile's transparency (11A8:0CC9: 0x1A on a
  16-colour display; 11A8:0F80, 11A8:1453: 0 is the top-left pixel; 11A8:1462: 0xFF is opaque only for an even
  width); its last copy back to the port (11A8:135F) is then explained. Inside the two contexts these are the move
  and combat frames (`tiles`); outside them `tiles_outside`, unexplained. The Windows set needs no shape identity
  (9.3's `g_mapSprites` lookup is the DOS set's, D6c), and any DrawTile into P or S inside a context is drawn (Open
  D2's two ResolveCombat call sites are recorded, not filtered on).
* **The undefined bevel colour** (FIDELITY F165). GetColorIndexRGB 1098:01B0 returns its uninitialised local for
  index 10 (claude/src seg019, ORIGINAL BUG; F13), so a city square's dark bevel (DrawMapTile 1110:0985) of a civ
  whose dark colour is 10 has no defined colour. The model marks those pixels (the square rendered twice with two
  values for index 10) and takes exactly them from the core's own map port bitmap (`w16q_bitmap`, read only) before
  the square's first copy out of P and at DrawMapTile's exit (`bevels`).
* **SectRect.** The game's SectRect 1100:0685 (touching rectangles meet; an inverted extent is zeroed) decides the
  label test, the flush rectangle and the unit's on-screen test, as in the core (labels are drawn for a touching
  box); M's settled check keeps its strict intersection, unchanged.
* **The present check** (`pmap_check.c`; 10.1, 10.2). At every core present (`pmap_on_core_present`, `pmap_inview.c`,
  after the map window is read): `Wn_win` against the virtual screen over the client's visible region, less an open
  menu's popups and the move/size outline (both drawn on the screen over the windows); the whole client when the
  window, its region, the exclusions or the shadow changed shape since the last check, else the present's rectangle
  and the shadow's own damage. A mismatch writes `mirror N ... kind present rect ... MISMATCH pixels P first x y
  core C ours O`; ok present checks are counted only (as key and render), and present checks take numbers in the
  `mirror N` sequence. The model's unexplained events write `unexplained draw|copy|bits|tile ...` lines (2,000 at
  most). Mode 2's end line: `end mirror checks .. mismatches .. unexplained U ..` (U: the engine's Windows-rule count
  since the start plus the model's own), M's fields, then `present .. present_mismatched .. present_pixels ..
  present_skipped .. present_noshadow .. squares .. unit_draws .. label_calls .. fills .. copies .. tiles ..
  tiles_outside .. replays .. bits .. dos_deferred .. scaled .. unsized .. resizes .. clipped .. nothing .. bevels
  ..`; mode 1's end line is unchanged. `--mirror 2` needs the DOS profile (B19): `pmap_mirror_start` refuses mode 2
  without it, and the harness exits 2 without `--dos-data`.
* **Harness and runner.** `harness_main.c`: `--mirror 1|2` (`harness.h`: the option's comment); `pmap_inview.c`'s
  listener calls `pmap_model_probe` / `pmap_model_event` and the present check. `tests/harness/ext_mirror.py`: the 29
  runs with `--mirror 2 --dos-data build\dosdata --graphics windows` (`--graphics` because the harness writes
  `profiles.log` only with it, and RV1-5's `refused 0` reads it); a run passes with `mismatches 0`, `unexplained 0`,
  `present_mismatched 0`, a present check in every run that reaches the map and `profiles.log`'s `registered
  windows,dos` and `refused 0`; `--exe`, `--root`, `--port` and `--ref` for private runs (defaults unchanged); the
  MIRROR lines and MIRROR_TOTAL extended. test.cmd's `ext_mirror` section: `est=140` and its comment and echo text
  (BUILD-TEST-ADDITIONS.md; build.cmd unchanged).
* **Open D1, D2, D5 answered.** D1: GR_PortOpen stores the bitmap at GrPort +0x1A (10D0:0141) and the DC at +0x18
  (10D0:0160): for `g_offscreenPort` 12D8:44A4 they are 12D8:44BE and 12D8:44BC, as 9.2 inferred. D2: the lunge's
  call at 1050:093A (returns to 1050:093F) draws the attacker's sprite `g_mapSprites[64 + civ * 32 + type]` at (2, 2)
  of S, 11 frames; the explosion's call at 1050:0AEA (returns to 1050:0AEF) draws `g_mapSprites[32 + frame]` at
  (0, 0) of S, frames 0..7; AnimateUnitMove's frames: 11C0:2F03. D5: in all 29 runs `replays 0`, `bits 0`,
  `tiles_outside 0`; everything outside the macros was a black fill (20,179), a copy between map shadows (23,753),
  a context DrawTile (660) or a window draw that reached nothing (6,147). No other core drawing reaches P, S or Wn
  in these runs.
* **Results (Checked, the landing's cycle).** `test_map` 146 checks in Checked and Release (127 before; CheckedNoObs
  1): the model on synthetic event streams with a fake emulation (registration; DrawMapTile drawn at entry; the
  flush P -> Wn; a window fill reaching only part of the client; a black fill; an unexplained SetPixel; DrawUnit with
  DrawUnitAt muted; DrawUnitAt alone; a move frame's DrawTile into S; a DrawTile outside the contexts; a scaled
  DrawTile; DrawUnitAt into a dialog; the undefined bevel colour and the flush after it) and `pmap_present_compare`
  vectors (equal, one mismatch, under an exclusion, a smaller shadow, two visible rectangles). **MIRROR_OK runs 29**:
  `MIRROR_TOTAL runs 29 checks 21620 key 6925 render 1229 settled 86 present 13380 present_mismatched 0 unexplained
  0 squares 6601 unit_draws 3148 tiles 660 tiles_outside 0` (key, render and settled as `--mirror 1`'s 8,240), every
  run's core trace equal to ext_p0's Checked V0 (P0, 7.3); `ext_mirror` took 175.8 s (152.5 s before). End-line sums
  (the stage's private run): present_pixels 1,913,685,040, fills 20,179, copies 23,753, label_calls 17,990, replays
  0, bits 0, dos_deferred 50,739, scaled 0, unsized 0, resizes 0, clipped 1,638, nothing 6,147, bevels 1 (s4).
  `OBS_LINT_OK files 106` (105 before); 286 portable sources (285). No frame, golden, G-1 field or DOS diagnostic
  changed (`build/d6a-prep/compare.py`: 46 of 53 Checked test outputs identical, the others as expected).
* **Deviations from the design** ([record](ext-D6a-record.md#deviations-from-the-design), one line each): D 9.3's
  "`leave` is NULL for the DROP macros": DrawMapTile has a Windows-set `leave` (the bevel, which no state
  determines); D 9.4's "copy the rectangle" and "replay it": window draws and copies change only `ObsDraw.changed`,
  and the owner copies map-to-map SRCCOPYs itself through a buffer (whole-client erases and flushes inside
  BeginPaint reach only the update region; drawing the whole rectangle made present mismatches); B 7.4/7.5's engine
  rules (12.7); the game's SectRect for the labels, the flush and the unit test; `ext_mirror.py`'s `--graphics
  windows` beyond the brief's options; files beyond the brief's list (`pmap_win.c`'s `mw_unit`, `pmap_zoom.c`'s
  registration, `pmap_inview.c`'s listener and present hooks, `harness.h`'s comment).
* **Not run or not verified.** CheckedNoObs and Release were not built at the slot (the stage's private builds
  passed: `test_map` 1 / 146, `test_pdos_shadow` 172 / 174); the D5 gate's eight steps cover them. Privately not run:
  `gen/dos/nodos.py check`, `registry.py check`, ext_p0, ext_frames, ext_timeline, ext_adapt, ext_zoom and ext_g1;
  at the slot `test.cmd checked` ran them (NODOS_OK with 0 hits, 1,690 files, 1,687 before: the three files new to
  the tree and the Checked executables about 35 KB larger; the others identical) and `ext_g1.py check` gave
  `EXT_G1_OK runs 77`, unchanged. The DOS rule set draws and replays nothing yet (D6b, D6c); `dos-settled`, the DOS
  map and Z for the DOS profile are not built. Asan deferred.

**As implemented (D6c; area D).** Record [ext-D6c-record.md](ext-D6c-record.md) (2026-10-16; BRIEF-d6.md "Units" D6c):
draft D 14.0..14.6 built as the DOS rule set of the map display model (`present/map/pmap_dos.c`, new), its events
outside the macros (D 9.3, 9.4), draft D 10.1's dos-settled check under `--mirror 2`, and `Wn_dos` composed on the A
canvas as the map element (draft B 6.2, 7.5's last bullet; D 6.2, 17 B-2). The first user-visible D6 change: with the
DOS profile the main map window's client shows the DOS map, DOS art at 2x, from the first map show on. Landed
2026-10-16 after the UX2 docs pass, by the orchestrator's acceptance of about 09:55 UTC (Open D3 from the listing, D806
as 0 recorded as a deviation, L23 / OB4 left open, checklist D5-7 to D6f): every base re-verified and the 9 staged files
and the record copied at 09:50:56 UTC by `build/d6c-prep/land.py` (manifest `build/d6c-prep/landed.sha256`); test.cmd's
`ext_mirror` comment block and echo by `build/d6c-prep/patch_testcmd.py` (its list line `est=140` and build.cmd
unchanged); the three-step cycle ALL_GREEN 10:15 UTC (`build/d6c-status.txt`; [ext-D6c-record.md](ext-D6c-record.md)
"Landing"). Compiled and headless in Checked (CheckedNoObs and Release privately in the stage); no DOS program was
executed; Asan deferred. The core is unchanged (P0 in the 29 mirror runs, ext_p0's DOS variations and the stage's
27-run DOS comparison; `EXT_G1_OK runs 77`). The A canvas's part (the element's screen exclusions) is in 12.7, "As
implemented (D6c; area B)". The user's check is pending (`manual-checklist-ext.md` D6c-1..D6c-7, user build
`build/user-builds/d6c/`).

* **Art** (`pmap_dos.c`; D 14.0). Shapes from E's `pdos_shape(PDOS_SH_<family>, DOS index)`: terrain B886 (`16 T +
  mask`, E's TER257 row rule inside `pdos_shapes`), coast quarters D294, river mouths D2D4, roads B27A, railroads B29A,
  rivers 6DFE, unexplored edges 7EEC, the grassland marker B880, icons and units D4CE, citizens 6E96. The page-2
  rectangles from `pdos_pic(PDOS_F_SP299_PIC)` at their page-2 coordinates (the whole-square coasts (E0h + 10h j, 64h),
  j 0..5, R:1AEA:0635..072B; the land base (100h, 78h), R:1AEA:07B2..07CD), copied opaquely (G#20). The civilization
  colours DGROUP:1946 (main) and 1956 (secondary) through the registry (`PDOS_T_CIV_MAIN_COLOUR`,
  `PDOS_T_CIV_SECOND_COLOUR`), their low byte. Every drawing constant cites its R:1AEA address in the source. A missing
  shape or picture draws nothing and counts `no_art` (0 in every run).
* **Positions** (14.1, Choice B23). A k = 2 `PdosPort` on the shadow's surface whose origin carries the map position's
  parity: DOS pixel (i, j) of a square at map pixel (sx, sy) covers map pixels (sx + 2i, sy + 2j) and the next. An odd
  position is drawn with its parity and counted `odd` (0 in every run: the core's positions are even).
* **One square** (14.2; R:1AEA:03BA), in the listing's order: 1 the ocean quarters (the 8-neighbour land mask, x
  wrapped, rows on the map; `m9 = (mask << 2) + ((mask >> 6) & 3)`), 1a the whole-square coasts (masks 1Ch, C1h, 07h,
  70h, 8Fh, F8h), 1b the river mouths (x wrapped, no row check), 2 the land base, 3 irrigation (I & 2, not ocean, DCFC
  clear, not a city), 4 the river (the N/E/S/W River-or-Ocean mask, no wrap, + 16 unless the page-2 byte (x, y + 96h),
  read as the core's `g_lpScreenBuffer` (x, y + 150), has `& 8`; masks 0 and 16 draw nothing), 4' other land
  (B886[16 T + mask]), 5 the grassland marker at (4, 4) for `((7x + 11y) & 2) == 0`, 6 pollution, 7 roads and
  railroads (B29A[d - 1] when both squares have the city or railroad bits, else B27A[d - 1]; none: the 2 x 2 dot at
  (7, 7), colour 0 with a railroad, else 6), 8 the mine (DCFC clear), 9 the special by the core's
  `HasSpecialResource`, 10 the hut by the core's `HasHut`, 11 the fortress, 12 the unexplored edges by the core's fog
  decision 1110:065B (not in the city-screen map, draw mode not 2, the human player's bit), 13 the city (DCFC clear).
  "DCFC clear" is `g_mapDrawMode == 0` (Open D3, below).
* **Cities** (14.4; R:1AEA:0C38..0DE7): outline (1, 1, 13, 13) colour 15, outline (2, 1, 12, 12) in 1956[owner], fill
  (2, 2, 12, 12) in 1946[owner], D4CE[1Ch] at (1, 1), G#3 5 -> 1956[owner] over (2, 2, 12, 12); the size (the real one
  for the player's city, else the known one) at (6, 5), or (3, 5) from 10, colour 0, or the disorder shape 6E96[4] at
  (5, 2); outline (0, 0, 15, 15) colour 0 when a unit (the core's `FindUnitAtTile`, read-only) or a stored unit is
  there; walls D4CE[1Dh] at (1, 1). A foreign city with known size 0 is skipped (R:1AEA:0C1C..0C35, D806 0).
* **Units** (14.3; R:1AEA:0E29, 0FB3): the core's unit (the macro's argument). DrawUnit: on ocean a sentried unit whose
  domain is not 2 is carried and not drawn (R:1AEA:0F3C..0F55, the same decision as the core's 1110:0ECE); another
  unit in the stack draws D4CE[40h + 20h civ + type] at (1, 1) first. Then the shape at (0, 0); fortified D4CE[1Dh];
  fortifying "F" shadowed at (4, 7); the player's unit with a goto target "G"; working non-air units their letter
  (R:1AEA:109D..1172) with the outline (-1, -1, 15, 15) colour 7; ink colour 9 for civ 1, else 15; sentry: G#3 5 -> 7
  then 8 -> 7 over (0, 0, 16, 16) (R:1AEA:1191..11CC). DrawUnitAt draws R:1AEA:0FB3's part only.
* **Labels** (14.5; R:1AEA:0008 step 4, R:1AEA:0132..0195). For each label of the core's list, drawn only when `sy +
  32` is inside the client (DOS `py < B8h`): at x = `max(sx - 16, 0)` from the client's left edge, y = `sy + 32`; the
  folded name (Choice B13) truncated by R:1F4D:04F7 to `floor((Cw + 14 - sx) / 2)` DOS pixels (DOS `147h - px`, the
  DOS map's right edge replaced by the client's); shadowed text in colour 11, font 1 (inferred, below).
  `pmap_dos_label_box` gives the label's own DOS box (the text width and the font's line height plus the shadow row,
  at 2x). **Choice:** labels are redrawn whole by their DOS box: DrawMapTile's flush and DrawCityLabels draw every
  label whose DOS box meets the rectangle (the Windows box `(x, y + 32, x + 166, y + 52)` is the Windows label's).
* **Move and combat frames** (14.6): `pmap_dos_sprite(index, ...)`, D4CE[index], the DOS counterpart of
  `g_mapSprites[index]`, at the DrawTile position, at the core's pace and order.
* **Clipping** (choice). Every primitive is clipped to the shadow (G#6 for G#32's sprites; G#20's copy, G#3 and the
  fill limited to the port), which is G#32's unclipped result wherever the surface has the pixel; the fill is a plain
  fill (R:0000:0BFA's one-row skips at the DOS screen's column 0 and span column are DOS-screen effects the map never
  reaches).
* **The macros in the DOS rule set** (`pmap_model.c`; D 9.3). The four DROP macros draw from live state at the entry
  by `pmap_dos.c`: DrawMapTile 1110:04D0 (the square; with `g_autoFlushToWindow`, the labels whose DOS box meets the
  flush's rectangle, the game's SectRect of the square and the window data's client rectangle), DrawUnit 1110:0DBA
  (R:1AEA:0E29 at `unit_square`'s position), DrawUnitAt 1110:0FF6 (R:1AEA:0FB3 at MapLogicalPointToPort 1220:101E's
  position), DrawCityLabels 1208:1F64 (the core's list, `g_cityLabelCount` 1408:0000, every label whose DOS box meets
  the update rectangle, whole).
* **Events outside the macros** (D 9.4). A window draw into a DOS map shadow: a black brush's fill (the model's
  `black_brush` rule) is DOS colour 0 where it reached the surface (`dos_fills`); anything else draws nothing and is
  unexplained (`dos_unexplained`, also the engine's DOS count; an `unexplained dos-draw` line under `--mirror 2`). A
  copy into a DOS map shadow: DrawTileImpl's last copy (11A8:135F) of a frame drawn at its entry is explained; a
  SRCCOPY between DOS map shadows is copied where it reached the destination (`dos_copies`); anything else is
  unexplained (`dos-copy`; B7: never pasted). Raw bits into a DOS map shadow outside the macros: unexplained
  (`dos-bits`), never pasted. DrawTile 11A8:0CB1 (entry probe) into P or S outside a DOS DROP bracket: the handle's
  index in `g_mapSprites` (1420:9350, 320 handles, read at each frame, since a loaded game cuts new handles) gives
  D4CE[index]; a handle not in the table (a temporary copy) inside AnimateUnitMove 11C0:2C32 or ResolveCombat
  1050:0000 is the context's unit, D4CE[64 + 32 civ + type] (`dos_tile_ctx`; for ResolveCombat the attacker; the
  core draws grey copies of the moving unit); drawn at the DrawTile position, its last copy explained; outside those
  contexts unexplained (`dos-tile`, Open D5); on the scaled port nothing is drawn (`dos_scaled`). A DOS map shadow
  about to be freed forgets its pending DrawTile copy and first removes the map element that shows it
  (`pmap_dos_surface_freed`). `dos_deferred` is no longer counted (0; the field stays in the end line);
  `pmap_model_black_brush` exports the fill rule for the dos-settled replay; `pmap_dos_reset` runs in the model's
  registration.
* **The dos-settled check** (`pmap_check.c`; D 10.1). Under `--mirror 2`, at every settled call (DrawTilesToPort
  1208:1866 modes 0 and 1, and RedrawMapRect: the same calls as M's settled check, the D6c decision), `P_dos` (the DOS
  shadow of the map port's bitmap) is copied at the entry (black when the shadow does not exist yet; a `P_dos` of
  another size than the bitmap skips the check, `dos_skipped`), redrawn at the exit by the DOS rules in the core's
  order of the same calls (`replay_ops`, now shared by the settled and dos-settled replays: the squares, the black of
  an unseen square or an erase, the labels by the DOS label rule and box) and compared with `P_dos`. A mismatch writes
  `mirror N virtual_us US kind dos-settled rect l t r b MISMATCH pixels P first x y core C ours O`; ok checks are
  counted. The end line adds `dos_settled D dos_mismatched X dos_skipped K dos_unexplained U` and the DOS rule set's
  counters (`dos_squares` .. `dos_clip_over`, RUNNING.md; the `dos_drawn_*`, `dos_labels` and `dos_sprites` counters
  include the replay's drawing). The present check's screen exclusions moved into `pmap_screen_exclusions`
  (unchanged behaviour), shared with the map element.
* **The map element** (draft B 6.2, 7.5; D 6.2, 17 B-2). `pmap_dos_present`, at every core present after the map
  window is read (`pmap_on_core_present`, after the present check; `pmap_inview.c`'s one call): `Wn_dos` (the DOS
  shadow of the map window's client) on the A canvas at the client's position, id the map window's handle, compose
  scale 1 (`Wn_dos` is at map pixels, the DOS art already at 2x), opaque, `rgb8` NULL (the A machine's play palette,
  reconverted at each step of D4a's `CyclePaletteColors` wiring), clipped to the client's visible region and excluding
  what is drawn on the screen over the windows (an open menu's popups, the move or size outline:
  `pmap_screen_exclusions`). Set only when its rectangle, clip, exclusions or surface size changed (compared
  bytewise; 690 sets in the 29 mirror runs); removed when the window is hidden, iconic, empty or has no visible region,
  when its region has more than `PDOS_CLIP_MAX` rectangles (`clip_over`), and before the shadow's surface is freed.
  Under `CIV_OBSERVER 0` `pmap_dos_present`, `pmap_dos_reset` and `pmap_dos_diag` are stubs. `pmap_int.h`: the DOS
  rule set's API, `PmapDosDiag`, the D6c counters of `PmapModelDiag`, the `PMAP_DL_*` trace steps and the test seam
  `PmapDosRes`.
* **Open D3 settled; D806; Open D6** (from the listing). The listing writes DCFC at two places only, R:0403:37AB (1)
  and R:0403:37C9 (0), around one full map redraw reached from the key dispatch at R:0403:3B47..3B4C (the 't' key);
  the core's counterpart is the same key, 11F0:3E87 't' (Hide Units), which sets `g_mapDrawMode = 1`, redraws the map
  and waits for a key or click. So "DCFC clear" is `g_mapDrawMode == 0` (layers 3, 8, 13), no longer inferred. D806
  (reveal all): every instruction of the listing that names it is a compare with 0 (34, e.g. O10:0184, O12:007B,
  O13:0792, R:0238:1112, R:0403:099B); none writes it, and it lies above DGROUP:6B30h (start-up zeroed, inferred; an

stage's `patch_testcmd.py` (the seven replacements of BUILD-TEST-ADDITIONS.md, CRLF kept; build.cmd unchanged); the

Compiled and headless in Checked (CheckedNoObs and Release privately in the stage); no DOS program was executed; Asan
deferred. The core is unchanged (P0 in ext_p0's V4d; `EXT_G1_OK runs 77`). The user's check is pending
